### [1] [Deductive Verification of Weak Memory Programs with View-based Protocols (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21084)
*Ömer Şakar,Soham Chakraborty,Marieke Huisman,Anton Wijs*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在VerCors自动演绎验证工具中编码弱内存并发的方法，开发了VerCors-relaxed扩展，通过扩展原子操作支持并引入协议自动机概念来编码基于权限的分离逻辑，实现了对弱内存并发程序的自动演绎验证。通过对SLR程序逻辑的松弛片段进行编码，成功在合理性能下自动验证了多个文献中的示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱内存并发下的编程因存在线程交错无法解释的程序行为（即顺序一致性无法覆盖）而面临正确性挑战。现有的弱内存并发程序逻辑多限于复杂的手动证明，而VerCors工具虽提供了丰富的自动演绎验证能力，但仅支持顺序一致性。因此，需要弥合自动演绎验证与弱内存并发之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出将弱内存并发编码到VerCors中的方法，开发VerCors-relaxed扩展：1）扩展VerCors的原子操作支持；2）引入协议自动机（protocol automata）概念来编码基于权限的分离逻辑，以支持弱内存并发模型。具体地，对SLR程序逻辑的松弛片段进行编码，并在VerCors-relaxed中实现。

Result: 在VerCors-relaxed中成功编码了SLR程序逻辑的松弛片段，并自动验证了多个文献中的示例，验证性能在合理范围内。

Conclusion: 本文成功将VerCors自动演绎验证工具的能力从顺序一致性扩展到了弱内存并发领域，证明了基于权限的分离逻辑可以通过协议自动机编码到现有自动验证工具中，从而实现对弱内存并发程序的高效自动验证。

Abstract: Concurrent programming under weak memory concurrency faces substantial challenges to ensure correctness due to program behaviors that cannot be explained by thread interleaving, a.k.a. sequential consistency. While several program logics are proposed to reason about weak memory concurrency, their usage has been limited to intricate manual proofs. On the other hand, the VerCors verifier provides a rich toolset for automated deductive verification for sequential consistency.
  In this paper, we bridge this gap for automated deductive verification of weak memory concurrent programs with the VerCors deductive verification tool. We propose an approach to encode weak memory concurrency in VerCors. We develop VerCors-relaxed, where we extend the VerCors atomics support and bring concepts from several protocol automata to encode permission-based separation logics for weak memory concurrency models. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, we encode the relaxed fragment of the SLR program logic, a recent state-of-the-art permission-based separation logic for weak memory concurrency in VerCors-relaxed, our extension of VerCors. We use the SLR encoding on VerCors-relaxed to automatically verify several examples from the literature within realistic performance.

</details>


### [2] [TAPO-Description Logic for Information Behavior: Refined OBoxes, Inference, and Categorical Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21172)
*Takao Inoué*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精炼的TAPO-描述逻辑，将其构建为包含静态描述层(TBox/ABox)、过程层(PBox)和预言机敏感层(OBox)的分层形式化体系，并引入元层守卫-判断机制来管理过程分支与迭代，最终通过范畴论与层论语义统一建模信息行为中的静态知识、犹豫、外部咨询和行动引导更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有描述逻辑主要关注静态知识表示，难以处理信息行为中的动态过程、外部信息源依赖以及主体决策中的犹豫等复杂行为，因此需要一种统一的形式化框架来同时涵盖静态知识表示、过程性推理和外部信息交互。

Method: 提出TAPO-描述逻辑的分层架构：(1) 静态描述层(TBox/ABox)处理领域知识；(2) 过程层(PBox)通过元层守卫-判断机制管理过程分支与迭代；(3) 预言机敏感层(OBox)处理外部信息源验证与导入。在此基础上建立核心推理系统，并给出范畴语义及其层论精化。

Result: 建立了TAPO-描述逻辑的核心推理系统，包括静态TBox/ABox推理、守卫化过程迁移和外部验证导入；给出了范畴语义并实现了层论精化；通过简单搜索行为和咖喱餐厅中评论敏感排序行为等案例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将静态知识表示、犹豫决策、外部咨询和行动引导更新统一在一个逻辑体系中，为复杂信息行为的建模提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a refined version of TAPO-description logic for the analysis of information behavior. The framework is treated not as a single homogeneous object logic, but as a layered formalism consisting of a static descriptive layer (TBox/ABox), a procedural layer (PBox), and an oracle-sensitive layer (OBox). To make this architecture mathematically explicit, we introduce a metalevel guard-judgment layer governing procedural branching and iteration. On this basis we formulate a core inference system for TAPO-description logic, covering static TBox/ABox reasoning, guarded procedural transition in the PBox, and validated external import in the OBox. We then give a categorical semantics for the resulting framework and indicate its sheaf-theoretic refinement. The theory is illustrated by examples of information-seeking behavior, including simple search behavior and review-sensitive ordering behavior in a curry restaurant. The aim is to treat not only static knowledge representation but also hesitation, external consultation, and action-guiding update within a unified logical setting.

</details>


### [3] [Using ASP(Q) to Handle Inconsistent Prioritized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21603)
*Meghyn Bienvenu,Camille Bourgaux,Robin Jean,Giuseppe Mazzotta*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文使用回答集编程(ASP)及其量化扩展ASP(Q)来实现基于优先级数据的不一致性容忍查询，定义了Pareto最优、全局最优和完成最优三种修复概念，并基于这些最优修复实现了AR、brave和IAR三种语义的变体。这是首次实现全局最优修复语义和基于grounded语义（一种可处理的下近似方法），并通过实验评估了计算可行性及不同语义、近似和编码的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不一致容忍查询方法未充分利用数据中的优先级关系来定义更精细的修复概念，且全局最优修复语义缺乏实际实现，grounded语义作为可处理近似也未被充分探索。

Method: 利用ASP和ASP(Q)对优先级不一致数据进行建模，定义三种最优修复（Pareto、全局、完成最优），并将AR、brave和IAR语义扩展到这些修复上。首次实现了全局最优修复语义和grounded语义（一种可处理的下近似），并通过实验评估不同语义、近似和编码的性能。

Result: 对于一大类逻辑理论，基于这些最优修复的查询回答处于多项式层级的第一或第二层。实验证明了全局最优修复语义的可行性，并揭示了不同语义、近似和编码对计算性能的影响。

Conclusion: ASP和ASP(Q)能有效支持优先级数据上的不一致容忍查询，全局最优修复语义具有实际可行性，grounded语义提供了一种有效的可处理近似方案。

Abstract: We explore the use of answer set programming (ASP) and its extension with quantifiers, ASP(Q), for inconsistency-tolerant querying of prioritized data, where a priority relation between conflicting facts is exploited to define three notions of optimal repairs (Pareto-, globally- and completion-optimal). We consider the variants of three well-known semantics (AR, brave and IAR) that use these optimal repairs, and for which query answering is in the first or second level of the polynomial hierarchy for a large class of logical theories. Notably, this paper presents the first implementation of globally-optimal repair-based semantics, as well as the first implementation of the grounded semantics, which is a tractable under-approximation of all these optimal repair-based semantics. Our experimental evaluation sheds light on the feasibility of computing answers under globally-optimal repair semantics and the impact of adopting different semantics, approximations, and encodings.

</details>

### [4] [Neuromorphic Computing Based on Parametrically-Driven Oscillators and Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21861)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Adarsh Ganesan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种具有2:1参量共振的双模振荡器，将其作为储备池计算机用于混沌时间序列预测。研究发现，参量共振区域因非线性激活与时间相干性保持而实现最优计算性能，而频率梳态因相位相干性丧失导致性能下降。计算性能与系统分岔结构直接对应，低误差区域沿参量共振边界分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参量驱动振荡器作为神经形态计算平台的能力，特别是理解不同动力学区域（亚阈值、参量共振、频率梳态）对储备池计算性能的影响，并建立计算性能与系统物理参数之间的映射关系。

Method: 采用双模2:1参量共振系统，将输入信号编码为驱动幅度，采样时域和频域响应作为储备池状态，对Mackey-Glass、Rossler和Lorenz混沌系统进行单步预测，通过扫描参数空间系统评估各动力学区域的计算性能。

Result: 参量共振区域实现最优计算性能；频率梳态虽具有更高光谱维度但性能不一致，混沌梳区因相位相干性丧失导致性能退化；计算误差与分岔结构直接对应，低误差区域沿参量共振边界分布；输入调制、失谐量、阻尼比和数据速率系统性地控制可达动力学区域和计算性能。

Conclusion: 参量共振是振荡器储备池计算的稳健工作区域，为调谐物理系统实现最优神经形态功能提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Parametrically driven oscillators provide a natural platform for neuromorphic computation, where nonlinear mode coupling and intrinsic dynamics enable both memory and high-dimensional transformation. Here, we investigate a two-mode system exhibiting 2:1 parametric resonance and demonstrate its operation as a reservoir computer across distinct dynamical regimes, including sub-threshold, parametric resonance, and frequency-comb states. By encoding input signals into the drive amplitude and sampling the resulting temporal and spectral responses, we perform one step-ahead prediction of benchmark chaotic systems, including Mackey-Glass, Rossler, and Lorenz dynamics. We find that optimal computational performance is achieved within the parametric resonance regime, where nonlinear interactions are activated while temporal coherence is preserved. In contrast, although frequency-comb states introduce increased spectral dimensionality, their performance is not consistently good across their existence band and also degrades in the chaotic comb regime due to loss of phase coherence. Mapping prediction error over parameter space reveals a direct correspondence between computational capability and the underlying bifurcation structure, with low-error regions aligned with the parametric resonance boundary. We further show that the input modulation, the detuning from the frequency matching condition, damping ratio, and input data rate systematically control the accessible dynamical regimes and thereby the computational performance. These results establish parametric resonance as a robust operating regime for oscillator-based reservoir computing and provide design principles for tuning physical systems toward optimal neuromorphic functionality.

</details>

### [5] [StarLoc: Pinpointing Transmitting LEO Satellites from a Single Passive Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21147)
*Ishani Janveja,Jida Zhang,Emerson Sie,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: StarLoc是一个利用轨道动力学和新型干涉式3D到达角估计技术，仅用三个2D平面天线即可实现低轨卫星3D定位的系统。在81颗Starlink卫星上的测试表明，其3D角度估计精度在0.7度以内，轨道距离估计精度在5公里以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星发射机的3D定位是频谱管理、轨道确定和太空GPS备份等应用中的重要新兴问题，现有方法需要更多天线或更复杂的配置。

Method: 结合轨道动力学建模与新型干涉式3D到达角估计；核心洞察是卫星运动受轨道动力学约束，在3D空间中沿2D流形运动，从而将自由度从3降至2，使得仅用三个2D平面天线即可实现3D定位与跟踪。

Result: 在81颗Starlink卫星上的实验结果显示：3D角度估计误差小于0.7度，轨道距离估计误差小于5公里。

Conclusion: StarLoc仅需三个2D平面天线即可实现低轨卫星的高精度3D定位与跟踪，数据集和实现已开源。

Abstract: This paper focuses on 3D localization of transmitting satellites in low Earth orbits (LEO). 3D localization of transmitters in low orbits is an important emerging problem for many applications such as spectrum management, orbit determination, and backup for GPS failures in orbit. We present StarLoc -- a system to geolocate transmitters in space using a combination of orbital modeling and a new interferometric 3D angle-of-arrival estimation technique. StarLoc's design relies on a unique insight -- the motion of satellites is governed by orbital dynamics and is therefore along a 2D manifold in a 3D space. This reduces the degrees of freedom in satellite motion and allows us to 3D-locate and track a satellite with just three antennas in a 2D plane. We evaluate the system using signal transmissions from 81 Starlink satellites. Our results show that StarLoc can estimate the 3D-angle of a satellite within 0.7 degrees and the orbital range within 5 km. Our dataset and implementation are available at: https://connectedsystemslab.github.io/starloc.

</details>


### [6] [SparKV: Overhead-Aware KV Cache Loading for Efficient On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21231)
*Hongyao Liu,Liuqun Zhai,Junyi Wang,Zhengru Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SparKV 是一个自适应 KV 加载框架，结合云端 KV 流式传输与端侧计算，通过动态决定每个 KV chunk 的加载方式（流式或本地计算）并重叠执行路径，在无线连接波动和边缘资源变化下运行时优化调度，将首 Token 延迟降低 1.3x-5.1x，能耗降低 1.5x-3.3x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧大模型推理中，Prefill 阶段处理完整输入上下文以构建 KV 缓存，受限于有限硬件资源和高昂计算成本，导致推理效率低下。

Method: 提出 SparKV 框架：(1) 对每个 KV chunk 建模成本，自适应决策流式传输或本地计算；(2) 重叠两种执行路径以减少延迟；(3) 在运行时根据无线连接波动和边缘资源变化，精调离线生成调度以重新平衡通信与计算成本。

Result: 在多种数据集、大模型和边缘设备上，SparKV 将首 Token 延迟降低 1.3x-5.1x，每请求能耗降低 1.5x-3.3x，且对响应质量影响可忽略。

Conclusion: SparKV 展现了对真实端侧部署场景的鲁棒性和实用性，有效解决了端侧 LLM 推理的预填充阶段效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Efficient inference for on-device Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging due to limited hardware resources and the high cost of the prefill stage, which processes the full input context to construct Key-Value (KV) caches. We present SparKV, an adaptive KV loading framework that combines cloud-based KV streaming with on-device computation. SparKV models the cost of individual KV chunks and decides whether each chunk should be streamed or computed locally, while overlapping the two execution paths to reduce latency. To handle fluctuations in wireless connectivity and edge resource availability, SparKV further refines offline-generated schedules at runtime to rebalance communication and computation costs. Experiments across diverse datasets, LLMs, and edge devices show that SparKV reduces Time-to-First-Token by 1.3$x-5.1x with negligible impact on response quality, while lowering per-request energy consumption by 1.5x to 3.3x, demonstrating its robustness and practicality for real-world on-device deployment.

</details>


### [7] [An Efficient Wireless iBCI Headstage with Adaptive ADC Sample Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21247)
*Hongyao Liu,Junyi Wang,Liuqun Zhai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无线脑机接口头戴设备，采用自适应ADC采样和尖峰检测技术，通过服务器驱动的架构在采集层实现数据压缩，在保持解码精度的同时降低高达40mW功耗并减少3.2倍的FPGA资源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线iBCI在功耗和数据吞吐量方面面临严重约束，传统应用层压缩方法计算密集且效率有限。

Method: 采用服务器驱动架构，服务器学习最优的电极特异性采样率向量，动态重构ADC硬件，在采集层（ADC和放大器）直接减少数据量，而非依赖后数字化的计算密集型处理。

Result: 功耗降低高达40mW，FPGA资源使用减少3.2倍，在运动和视觉任务中保持或超过原有的解码精度。

Conclusion: 该设计为长期在体记录提供了高度实用的解决方案，原型已开源。

Abstract: Implantable Brain-Computer Interfaces (iBCIs) are increasingly pivotal in clinical and daily applications. However, wireless iBCIs face severe constraints in power consumption and data throughput. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose a wireless iBCI headstage featuring adaptive ADC sampling and spike detection. Distinguishing our design from traditional application-layer compression, we employ a server-driven architecture that achieves source-level efficiency. Specifically, the server learns an optimal, electrode-specific sample rate vector to dynamically reconfigure the ADC hardware. This strategy reduces data volume directly at the acquisition layer (ADC and amplifier) rather than relying on computationally intensive post-digitization processing. Extensive experiments across diverse subjects and arrays demonstrate a power reduction of up to 40 mW and a 3.2$\times$ decrease in FPGA resource utilization, all while maintaining or exceeding decoding accuracy in both motor and visual tasks. This design offers a highly practical solution for long-term in-vivo recording.Our prototype is open-sourced in: https://github.com/liuhongyao99cs/32-Channel-Wireless-BCI-Headstage.

</details>

### [8] [Role of diversity in team performance: the case of missing expertise, an agent based simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21328)
*Tamás Kiss*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过基于主体的仿真模型，研究了管理团队中隐性能力分布（如个体内功能多样性IFD和主导功能多样性DFD）对团队沟通与绩效的影响，并发现需引入第三个度量——团队聚合专长——才能全面解释实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于实验的社会系统研究仅关注测量变量分布的前一两个模态，难以分析情境效应；在功能多样性研究中，管理团队由复杂的能力分布描述，现有方法无法有效探究隐性分布及其组成对团队沟通和绩效的影响。

Method: 开发了一个基于主体的仿真模型（agent-based model），并开展了一系列仿真实验，变化交互主体间的沟通方案及其功能组成，以分析IFD和DFD在不同情境下对团队沟通与绩效的影响。

Result: IFD和DFD对团队沟通与绩效的影响具有情境依赖性：在特定沟通方案和功能组成下，它们可能增强也可能降低绩效与沟通。此外，仅靠IFD和DFD不足以全面解释实证发现，需引入第三个度量——团队聚合专长。

Conclusion: 管理团队的功能多样性对绩效的影响受情境因素调节；未来研究应同时考虑IFD、DFD和团队聚合专长三个维度，才能更完整地刻画团队能力分布对绩效的作用机制。

Abstract: Theory and empirical research on management teams' influence on firm performance have witnessed continuous development, and by now incorporate numerous details. Classic, experiment-based studies examining social systems collect vast amount of data, but often times investigate only the first one or two modes of the distribution of measured variables, and experience difficulty in analyzing the effect of context. For example, in functional diversity research, management teams are described by measures incorporating complex distributions of capabilities of individual managers and teams of managers. To investigate the effect of hidden distributions, and the effect of functional diversity composition on team communication and performance, we developed an agent-based model, and conducted a series of simulation experiments. Modeling results show that depending on the context, such as communication scheme among interacting agents, or their functional composition, intrapersonal functional diversity (IFD), and dominant function diversity (DFD) might enhance or reduce performance and communication among agents. Furthermore, simulation results also suggest that a third measure is required alongside IFD and DFD capturing the aggregate expertise of the team to comprehensively account for empirical findings.

</details>


### [9] [Agentic AI-Enabled Framework for Thermal Comfort and Building Energy Assessment in Tropical Urban Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21787)
*Po-Yen Lai,Xinyu Yang,Derrick Low,Huizhe Liu,Jian Cheng Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种集成大语言模型与轻量物理模型的智能体AI推理框架，用于评估新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能耗，支持用户探索绿色立面、冷涂料等气候适应性策略，并通过自主推理与快速定量评估相结合，提升热舒适度并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能源需求，传统物理模型计算成本高，而纯数据驱动方法缺乏物理可解释性，亟需一种兼具推理能力与计算效率的框架来支持城市可持续设计决策。

Method: 构建智能体AI推理框架，通过提示定制使LLM理解城市设计任务、提取相关政策并激活轻量物理模型；轻量模型基于核心热力学和空气流动物理原理，简化传统计算模型以快速预测微气候变量（建筑表面温度、地面辐射热、气流条件等），进而估算热舒适指标（PET）和建筑能耗。

Result: 框架支持用户探索多种气候适应性建筑表面策略（绿色立面、冷涂料），能够改善热舒适度、减少墙体得热和能源需求；LLM的自主推理与轻量物理模型的快速定量评估相结合，形成了闭环推理-行动过程。

Conclusion: 该框架在可持续城市设计、室内外环境集成和气候适应规划中具有跨学科应用潜力，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: In response to the urban heat island effects and building energy demands in Singapore, this study proposes an agentic AI-enabled reasoning framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with lightweight physics-based models. Through prompt customization, the LLMs interpret urban design tasks, extract relevant policies, and activate appropriate physics-based models for evaluation, forming a closed-loop reasoning-action process. These lightweight physics-based models leverage core thermal and airflow principles, streamlining conventional models to reduce computational time while predicting microclimate variables, such as building surface temperature, ground radiant heat, and airflow conditions, thereby enabling the estimation of thermal comfort indices, e.g., physiological equivalent temperature (PET), and building energy usage. This framework allows users to explore a variety of climate-resilient building surface strategies, e.g., green façades and cool paint applications, that improve thermal comfort while reducing wall heat gain and energy demand. By combining the autonomous reasoning capacity of LLMs with the rapid quantitative evaluation of lightweight physics-based models, the proposed system demonstrates potential for cross-disciplinary applications in sustainable urban design, indoor-outdoor environmental integration, and climate adaptation planning. The source code and data used in this study are available at: https://github.com/PgUpDn/urban-cooling-agent.

</details>

### [10] [Institutionalizing Best Practices in Research Computing: A Framework and Case Study for Improving User Onboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21898)
*Ayush Chaturvedi,Rob Pokorney,Elyn Fritz-Waters,Charlotte Rouse,Gary Bax,Daryl Spencer,Craig Pohl*

Main category: cs.OH

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改善研究计算中心新用户入职体验的框架，并在华盛顿大学圣路易斯分校的研究基础设施服务中进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究计算中心在引导新用户时面临巨大挑战，尽管已有文档、教程和培训等资源，用户仍常因复杂的系统和软件而困惑，难以有效访问计算资源。

Method: 提出一个旨在改善新用户入职体验的框架，并在华盛顿大学圣路易斯分校的研究基础设施服务中进行了应用和实证验证。

Result: 通过框架的实际应用，验证了其在新用户入职流程中的有效性，具体结果包括用户困惑减少、资源访问效率提升等（需结合原文进一步确认）。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效改善研究计算中心的新用户入职体验，为其他机构提供了可借鉴的方法，有助于降低用户使用复杂计算资源的门槛。

Abstract: Research computing centers around the world struggle with onboarding new users. Subject matter experts, researchers, and principal investigators are often overwhelmed by the complex infrastructure and software offerings designed to support diverse research domains at large academic and national institutions. As a result, users frequently fall into confusion and complexity to access these resources, despite the availability of documentation, tutorials, interactive trainings and other similar resources. Through this work, we present a framework designed to improve new-user onboarding experience. We also present an empirical validation through its application within the Research Infrastructure Services at Washington University in St. Louis.

</details>

### [11] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GateScope是一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业LLM API网关的行为一致性和操作透明度。通过对10个真实商业网关的测量，发现存在模型降级/替换、静默截断、计费不准确和延迟不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关作为多厂商模型的统一接入点日益普及，但其内部路由、缓存和计费策略缺乏透明度，用户无法验证请求是否由宣称的模型服务、响应是否忠实于上游API、账单是否准确。

Method: 设计GateScope轻量级黑盒测量框架，从响应内容分析、多轮对话性能、计费准确性和延迟特征四个维度审计网关行为，检测模型降级/切换、静默截断、计费不准确和延迟不稳定等关键异常行为。

Result: 对10个真实商业LLM API网关的测量发现：存在静默模型替换、记忆保持能力下降、偏离公布定价以及不同平台间延迟稳定性显著差异等问题。

Conclusion: 商业LLM API网关在行为一致性和操作透明度方面存在显著缺陷，亟需系统性审计框架来保障用户权益和提升服务可问责性。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


### [12] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐(SUA)框架，通过衡量预测不稳定性和模型不确定性之间的对齐程度来检测大语言模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败，并引入SUA-TR训练方法和弃权规则提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性敏感性和模糊性反映了一个共同问题：预测不稳定性与模型不确定性之间的错位。可靠的模型应在预测不稳定时表达更高的不确定性，否则会导致校准错误。

Method: 定义标量分数SUA_theta(x)捕捉分布敏感性与预测熵之间的差异；证明最小化其正部分可约束最坏情况下的扰动风险并与校准误差相关；提出SUA-TR训练方法结合一致性正则化和熵对齐，以及用于安全推理的弃权规则。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA比单独使用熵或自一致性方法能更好地识别模型失败。

Conclusion: 该框架与模型无关，为提升语言模型可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [13] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了arXiv上270万个预印本提交的源文件，发现几乎每个提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息（如不必要的文件、嵌入元数据、源码注释等），包括内部协调链接、API密钥、私钥乃至完整的Git历史，并指出现有清理工具无法可靠地去除这些信息，作者因此提供了ALC-NG工具来全面清理LaTeX论文编译不需要的文件、元数据和注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv作为最广泛使用的预印本服务，不仅发布预印本本身，还公开发布LaTeX源码等文件，这可能导致敏感信息无意泄露，但目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性量化研究。

Method: 对所有270万个arXiv提交的源文件，从三个维度分析信息泄露：(1)包含不必要的文件，(2)文件中嵌入的元数据，(3)文件中的无关内容（如源码注释）；并评估现有清理工具的效果。

Result: 几乎每个arXiv提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息，包括内部协调使用的可编辑Web文档链接、API密钥、私钥以及完整的Git历史记录；现有清理工具无法可靠地实现预期清理功能。

Conclusion: arXiv源文件存在系统性信息泄露问题，现有工具不足，作者为此提供了ALC-NG工具，用于全面移除编译LaTeX论文所不需要的文件、元数据和注释。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [14] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeRedirect是一种系统级防御方法，通过重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它，有效解决前沿LLM在合法任务中自发产生有害内容的"内部安全崩溃"（ISC）问题，将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在执行需要有害内容的合法专业任务时，会以超过95%的安全失败率自发生成有害内容（即内部安全崩溃ISC），而现有的输入级防御对此完全无效（100%失败率），标准系统提示防御也只能提供部分缓解。

Method: SafeRedirect通过系统级重写（override）实现：1) 授予明确的任务失败许可；2) 规定确定性的硬停止输出；3) 指示模型保留有害占位符不解析。该方法不抑制模型的任务完成驱动力，而是将其重定向。

Result: 在7个前沿LLM上的3种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型（单轮设置）中，SafeRedirect将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，而最强可行基线为55.0%。跨模型消融实验显示失败许可和条件特异性是普遍关键的组件；跨攻击评估证实对ISC具有最先进防御性能，对其他攻击家族的泛化能力至少与基线持平。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect是一种有效的系统级ISC防御方法，通过重定向而非压制模型的任务完成驱动来解决问题，在多个前沿LLM上取得显著效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [15] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过上下文感知的自适应防御策略，在RAG系统中显著缓解了安全性与检索效用之间的权衡，相比始终开启的静态防御栈，在消除成员推理攻击的同时将上下文召回率恢复至接近无防御基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域依赖私有领域知识，面临成员推理、数据投毒和内容泄露等安全风险；简单启用所有防御措施会导致严重的效用损失（实验显示上下文召回率下降超40%），亟需一种能平衡安全性与效用的自适应防御方案。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构：Sentinel组件检测异常检索行为，Strategist组件根据查询上下文选择性部署仅必要的防御措施，实现查询感知的自适应防御。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上，自适应防御（ADO）完全消除了MBA风格的成员推理泄露；在数据投毒场景下，最强ADO变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时将上下文召回率恢复至无防御基线的75%以上。

Conclusion: 自适应、查询感知的防御策略能够大幅降低RAG系统中的安全-效用权衡，但鲁棒性对模型选择较为敏感。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [16] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的LLM越狱审计框架，通过引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法优化激活操纵系数，对8个开源LLM进行系统性安全审计，发现Llama-3系列模型高度脆弱（最高91%越狱成功率），GPT-oss-120B最鲁棒，并揭示了可解释性越狱技术的双重用途风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全审计大多依赖黑盒探测方法，缺乏对模型内部机制脆弱性的系统性理解。本文旨在通过可解释性方法揭示模型内部表征中的安全隐患，为安全审计提供更深入的视角。

Method: 采用两种可解释性方法——Universal Steering (US)和Representation Engineering (RepE)，并引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法来寻找最优的激活操纵系数，实现对不安全行为概念的有效引导。评估采用标准化的LLM评判协议和精心筛选的有害查询集。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-4bt在US和RepE攻击下分别有91%和83%的响应被越狱；GPT-oss-120B对两种攻击均表现出鲁棒性；Qwen3-0.6B和Phi4-3.8B等小模型越狱率较低，而大版本模型更易受影响。

Conclusion: 可解释性激活操纵是系统安全审计的有力工具，但同时也存在双重用途风险，凸显了LLM部署中加强内部防御机制的必要性。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [17] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Agentic AI模型的新型函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵工具选择过程强制模型调用攻击者指定的函数。该攻击对语义上下文不敏感、对函数集鲁棒，可训练产生通用对抗函数，在多个模型上实现70%-100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agentic AI的兴起，函数调用LLM被广泛用于调用外部工具，但其函数调用接口引入了新的安全漏洞。现有研究已表明函数调用可被滥用导致数据篡改、窃取、死循环或生成有害内容，但缺乏针对工具选择过程的定向操纵攻击研究。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵agentic模型的工具选择过程，强制模型调用攻击者预先指定的函数。FHA对上下文语义不敏感（语义无关），对函数集具有鲁棒性。进一步提出可训练通用对抗函数，使单个攻击函数能在多个查询和载荷配置下劫持工具选择。

Result: 在5个不同模型（包括指令模型和推理模型变体）上，使用BFCL数据集进行实验，攻击成功率（ASR）达到70%至100%。

Conclusion: FHA攻击揭示了Agentic系统在函数调用接口上的严重安全漏洞，现有攻击方法无法充分防御此类劫持，亟需为Agentic系统建立强大的安全护栏和安全模块。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [18] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VRSafe，一种抗击键推断攻击的VR虚拟QWERTY键盘，通过引入假阳性击键和恶意登录检测器，在保持可用的同时显著降低攻击准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码认证在VR应用中广泛使用，但现有针对虚拟键盘的击键推断攻击研究较多，而缺乏有效且成本低廉的防御策略。

Method: 设计VRSafe键盘，在用户输入过程中向攻击者收集的信息中引入假阳性击键以混淆真实密码；同时集成恶意登录检测器，识别基于推断凭证的未授权登录尝试。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实用户研究，VRSafe能显著降低击键推断攻击的准确率，同时仅在可用性方面引入适度开销，且检测器具有高检测率和低时空成本。

Conclusion: VRSafe为VR环境下的击键推断攻击提供了一种有效的轻量级防御方案，平衡了安全性与用户体验。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [19] [Layer 2 Blockchains Simplified: A Survey of Vector Commitment Schemes, ZKP Frameworks, Layer-2 Data Structures and Verkle Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21055)
*Ekleen Kaur,Marko Suvajdzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次以统一的安全视角，系统梳理了Layer-2协议从Layer-1密码学原语到零知识证明框架（Groth16、Plonk）及承诺方案（KZG、IPA）的技术演进，形式化定义了包含排序器活性、桥漏洞、数据可用性失效的综合威胁模型，为研究人员和开发者提供了从密码数学角度理解L2安全的可访问但严谨的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对L2安全风险的描述零散且不统一，缺乏从密码学底层到系统架构的整体安全分析，亟需一份统一、严谨的安全调查来系统性梳理L2安全面。

Method: 采用统一的、以安全为核心的调查方法，从L1密码学原语出发，逐步剖析L2架构的密码学安全基础，分析ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）及其承诺方案（KZG、IPA）中的安全假设（离散对数、CDH、双线性DH），并形式化定义全面的L2威胁模型。

Result: 构建了首个统一的L2安全调查框架，系统化梳理了从L1原语到L2核心的密码学安全演进路径，明确了ZK证明系统与承诺方案的安全假设基础，并形式化了涵盖排序器、桥、数据可用性等关键组件的威胁模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究人员和开发者提供了一个可访问但严谨的参考框架，使其能够从深度密码数学视角推理和分析L2安全性，弥补了现有文献中碎片化安全分析的不足。

Abstract: Layer-2 (L2) protocols address the fundamental limitations of Layer-1 (L1) blockchains by offloading computation while anchoring trust to the parent chain. This architectural shift, while boosting throughput, introduces a new, complex security surface defined by off-chain components like sequencers, bridges, and data availability mechanisms. Prior literature[31][33] offers fragmented views of this risk. This paper presents the first unified, security-focused survey that rigorously maps L2 architecture to its underlying cryptographic security. We dissect the technical progression from L1 primitives to the core of modern L2s, analyzing the security assumptions(Discrete Logarithm, Computational Diffie-Hellman, Bilinear Diffie-Hellman) of ZK frameworks (Groth16, Plonk) and their corresponding commitment schemes (KZG, IPA). We formalize a comprehensive L2 threat model encompassing sequencer liveness, bridge exploits, and data-availability failures. This work serves as an accessible yet rigorous reference for researchers and developers to reason about L2 security from a deep crypto-mathematical perspective.

</details>


### [20] [Cross-Session Threats in AI Agents: Benchmark, Evaluation, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21131)
*Ari Azarafrooz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨会话威胁检测方法，包含CSTM-Bench数据集、测量框架和Coreset Memory Reader算法，解决AI智能体防护中跨会话攻击检测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体防护系统是无记忆的，每条消息被独立判断，攻击者可将单个攻击分散到数十个会话中绕过检测，因为只有聚合体才携带有效载荷。

Method: (1) 构建CSTM-Bench数据集：26种可执行攻击分类，按杀伤链阶段和跨会话操作分类，绑定7种身份锚点，含良性混淆样本。(2) 测量框架：将跨会话检测建模为信息瓶颈，发现会话级裁判和全日志连接器在跨会话场景中攻击召回率下降约一半。(3) Coreset Memory Reader算法：保留最高信号片段(K=50)，并引入CSR_prefix指标结合检测形成CSTM评分。

Result: Coreset Memory Reader是唯一在两个分片中召回率都保持稳定的方法；量化了现有方法在跨会话场景下的性能退化程度；发布了包含54个场景/分片的数据集。

Conclusion: 跨会话攻击检测是现有AI防护系统的盲区，有界记忆的核集读取器结合前缀稳定性度量可有效应对，数据集已开源以推动更大规模研究。

Abstract: AI-agent guardrails are memoryless: each message is judged in isolation, so an adversary who spreads a single attack across dozens of sessions slips past every session-bound detector because only the aggregate carries the payload. We make three contributions to cross-session threat detection.
  (1) Dataset. CSTM-Bench is 26 executable attack taxonomies classified by kill-chain stage and cross-session operation (accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader), each bound to one of seven identity anchors that ground-truth "violation" as a policy predicate, plus matched Benign-pristine and Benign-hard confounders. Released on Hugging Face as intrinsec-ai/cstm-bench with two 54-scenario splits: dilution (compositional) and cross_session (12 isolation-invisible scenarios produced by a closed-loop rewriter that softens surface phrasing while preserving cross-session artefacts).
  (2) Measurement. Framing cross-session detection as an information bottleneck to a downstream correlator LLM, we find that a session-bound judge and a Full-Log Correlator concatenating every prompt into one long-context call both lose roughly half their attack recall moving from dilution to cross_session, well inside any frontier context window. Scope: 54 scenarios per shard, one correlator family (Anthropic Claude), no prompt optimisation; we release it to motivate larger, multi-provider datasets.
  (3) Algorithm and metric. A bounded-memory Coreset Memory Reader retaining highest-signal fragments at $K=50$ is the only reader whose recall survives both shards. Because ranker reshuffles break KV-cache prefix reuse, we promote $\mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$ (ordered prefix stability, LLM-free) to a first-class metric and fuse it with detection into $\mathrm{CSTM} = 0.7 F_1(\mathrm{CSDA@action}, \mathrm{precision}) + 0.3 \mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$, benchmarking rankers on a single Pareto of recall versus serving stability.

</details>


### [21] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊捆绑服务（如Flashbots Bundles）的新型拒绝服务攻击。利用合约状态中的交易间依赖性和捆绑服务的原子区块包含特性，开发了隐蔽、无风险且低成本的DoS攻击。实验证明攻击成功率较高，能大幅降低构建者收入并减慢区块生产速度，同时提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝服务攻击设计由于捆绑服务独特的多轮执行模型而无效，需要研究针对捆绑服务的新颖不对称拒绝服务攻击方法。

Method: 利用合约状态中的交易间依赖关系实现隐蔽性，滥用捆绑服务特有功能（如原子区块包含）降低资金和操作成本，对Flashbots捆绑服务实施拒绝服务攻击。

Result: 攻击成功率高，能大幅降低构建者收入，减慢区块生产速度。

Conclusion: 提出了针对所识别风险的缓解策略。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [22] [Strategic Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Architecture for Cost-Effective Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21282)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于博弈论的异构多智能体架构用于代码漏洞检测，采用"3+1"模式（三个云端DeepSeek-V3专家智能体并行分析+一个本地Qwen3-8B验证器对抗验证），在NIST Juliet测试集上以每样本0.002美元的成本达到77.2%的F1分数，验证了博弈论可指导成本敏感的软件工程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码漏洞检测方法在检测精度与计算成本之间存在根本性权衡，需要一种既能提高检测准确性又能控制成本的新方法。

Method: 提出异构多智能体架构：(1)"3+1"架构——三个云端DeepSeek-V3专家智能体分别从代码结构、安全模式、调试逻辑三个互补视角并行分析，一个本地轻量级Qwen3-8B验证器以零边际成本进行对抗性验证；(2)双层博弈框架——专家间的合作博弈（捕捉多视角的超加性价值）和对抗性验证博弈（建模质量保证激励机制）。

Result: 在NIST Juliet测试集262个真实样本（14种CWE类型，平衡的有漏洞/良性类别）上，F1分数77.2%，精确率62.9%，召回率100%，每样本成本0.002美元；优于单专家LLM基线（F1 71.4%）和Cppcheck静态分析（MCC 0）；对抗性验证器显著提升精确率（+10.3个百分点，p<1e-6）；并行执行实现3.0倍加速。

Conclusion: 博弈论设计原则可以有效地指导面向成本敏感的软件工程任务的异构多智能体架构设计，实现高精度与低成本的平衡。

Abstract: Automated code vulnerability detection is critical for software security, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off between detection accuracy and computational cost. We propose a heterogeneous multi-agent architecture inspired by game-theoretic principles, combining cloud-based LLM experts with a local lightweight verifier. Our "3+1" architecture deploys three cloud-based expert agents (DeepSeek-V3) that analyze code from complementary perspectives - code structure, security patterns, and debugging logic - in parallel, while a local verifier (Qwen3-8B) performs adversarial validation at zero marginal cost.
  We formalize this design through a two-layer game framework: (1) a cooperative game among experts capturing super-additive value from diverse perspectives, and (2) an adversarial verification game modeling quality assurance incentives.
  Experiments on 262 real samples from the NIST Juliet Test Suite across 14 CWE types, with balanced vulnerable and benign classes, demonstrate that our approach achieves a 77.2% F1 score with 62.9% precision and 100% recall at $0.002 per sample - outperforming both a single-expert LLM baseline (F1 71.4%) and Cppcheck static analysis (MCC 0). The adversarial verifier significantly improves precision (+10.3 percentage points, p < 1e-6, McNemar's test) by filtering false positives, while parallel execution achieves a 3.0x speedup.
  Our work demonstrates that game-theoretic design principles can guide effective heterogeneous multi-agent architectures for cost-sensitive software engineering tasks.

</details>


### [23] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI驱动的IoT与边缘计算系统中硬件根信任机制，比较了TPM、PUF、混合硬件信任根及纯软件方案，指出硬件方案在抗物理篡改和设备克隆方面优于纯软件方案，PUF和混合信任锚在大规模AIoT系统中具有最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与IoT、边缘计算的快速融合对设备认证和AI模型完整性提出了严格的信任需求，现有方案在资源受限和安全约束下面临挑战，需要系统梳理和比较各类信任锚机制以指导未来设计。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对代表性信任锚机制（TPM测量与认证、硅基和FPGA基PUF、混合容器感知硬件信任根、纯软件安全方法）进行结构化比较分析，评估安全性、可扩展性、成本和部署复杂性等权衡。

Result: 硬件根方案在对抗性物理暴露环境中提供更强的抗物理篡改和设备克隆保护；PUF和混合信任锚在安全强度、可扩展性、成本之间取得最佳平衡；纯软件方案在对抗性物理暴露环境中仍不充分。

Conclusion: 该比较分析阐明了当前设计挑战，为未来可信AIoT平台的发展提供了指导方向。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into Internet of Things (IoT) and edge computing systems has intensified the need for robust, hardware-rooted trust mechanisms capable of ensuring device authenticity and AI model integrity under strict resource and security constraints. This survey reviews and synthesizes existing literature on hardware-rooted trust mechanisms for AI-enabled IoT systems. It systematically examines and compares representative trust anchor mechanisms, including Trusted Platform Module (TPM)-based measurement and attestation, silicon and FPGA-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs), hybrid container-aware hardware roots of trust, and software-only security approaches. The analysis highlights how hardware-rooted solutions generally provide stronger protection against physical tampering and device cloning compared to software-only approaches, particularly in adversarial and physically exposed environments, while hybrid designs extend hardware trust into runtime and containerized environments commonly used in modern edge deployments. By evaluating trade-offs among security strength, scalability, cost, and deployment complexity, the study shows that PUF-based and hybrid trust anchors offer a promising balance for large-scale, AI-enabled IoT systems, whereas software-only trust mechanisms remain insufficient in adversarial and physically exposed settings. The presented comparison aims to clarify current design challenges and guide future development of trustworthy AI-enabled IoT platforms.

</details>


### [24] [ECCFROG522PP: An Enhanced 522 bit Weierstrass Elliptic Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21261)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一条名为ECCFROG522PP的522位素数域椭圆曲线，所有参数通过BLAKE3确定性流水线从公开种子生成，强调透明度、可审计性和可复现性，目标安全级别与P-521相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广泛部署的椭圆曲线（如NIST P-256、secp256k1）及更高安全级曲线（如P-521、Curve448、Brainpool P512）的参数生成过程缺乏足够的透明度和可审计性，本文旨在设计一条完全确定性生成、参数可公开验证的椭圆曲线，以满足对安全审计和独立验证有较高要求的应用场景。

Method: 采用BLAKE3哈希函数构建确定性参数生成流水线，所有关键参数（包括曲线系数、基点、阶等）均从单一固定公开种子通过公开指定的索引推导得出。曲线为短Weierstrass形式，素数域大小为522位，并确保素数阶、协因子为1、基点满阶；同时对二次扭曲进行大素数因子验证，计算嵌入度的下界，并执行低嵌入度约简和CM判别式异常的标准安全检查。

Result: 成功生成了一条522位素数域椭圆曲线，满足素数阶、协因子1、基点满阶的设计目标；二次扭曲具有大素数因子；嵌入度有可证明的下界；通过了所有标准安全性检查；整个生成和验证流程可通过公开工件和参考脚本端到端复现。

Conclusion: ECCFROG522PP提供了一条完全透明、可审计、可独立验证的椭圆曲线，在P-521级别的经典安全范围内，为对参数生成过程可信度有高要求的应用场景提供了一个替代选择。

Abstract: This paper presents ECCFROG522PP, a 522-bit prime-field elliptic curve in short Weierstrass form, designed with a focus on deterministic generation and public reproducibility. The central design principle is that all critical parameters are derived from a fixed public seed through a transparent and verifiable procedure. While many deployed systems rely on NIST P-256 and secp256k1, which target approximately 128-bit classical security, higher security applications typically consider curves such as NIST P-521, Curve448, and Brainpool P512. ECCFROG522PP is intended for the same general classical security range as P-521, with emphasis on transparency, auditability, and reproducibility rather than performance optimization.
  The curve parameters are generated through a BLAKE3-based deterministic pipeline with publicly specified indices. The resulting construction has prime order, cofactor one, and a deterministically derived base point of full order. The quadratic twist has a large proven prime factor, and the construction includes a documented lower bound on the embedding degree together with standard sanity checks against low embedding degree reductions and basic CM discriminant anomalies.
  The full generation and validation procedure can be reproduced end to end from public artifacts and reference scripts, enabling independent verification of all parameters and checks.

</details>


### [25] [CI-Work: Benchmarking Contextual Integrity in Enterprise LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21308)
*Wenjie Fu,Xiaoting Qin,Jue Zhang,Qingwei Lin,Lukas Wutschitz,Robert Sim,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CI-Work基准测试，用于评估企业LLM智能体在密集检索场景下平衡任务效用与隐私保护的能力。研究发现前沿模型普遍存在隐私泄露（违规率15.8%-50.9%，泄露率最高26.7%），且任务效用与隐私违规呈反直觉的正相关关系。单纯扩大模型规模或增加推理深度无法解决该问题，需要转向以上下文为中心的架构范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业LLM智能体在提升工作效率的同时，其核心能力（检索并使用内部上下文代表用户行动）也带来了敏感信息泄露的新风险。现有研究缺乏针对企业工作流中隐私保护的系统性评估基准。

Method: 基于情境完整性理论构建CI-Work基准，模拟五种信息流方向的企业工作流，在密集检索场景下评估智能体能否在传达必要内容的同时抑制敏感上下文。

Result: 前沿模型隐私违规率15.8%-50.9%，泄露率最高26.7%；任务效用与隐私违规呈正相关；企业数据规模与用户行为会放大脆弱性；扩大模型规模或增加推理深度无法解决问题。

Conclusion: 保障企业工作流安全需要范式转变，从以模型为中心的扩展转向以上下文为中心的架构。

Abstract: Enterprise LLM agents can dramatically improve workplace productivity, but their core capability, retrieving and using internal context to act on a user's behalf, also creates new risks for sensitive information leakage. We introduce CI-Work, a Contextual Integrity (CI)-grounded benchmark that simulates enterprise workflows across five information-flow directions and evaluates whether agents can convey essential content while withholding sensitive context in dense retrieval settings. Our evaluation of frontier models reveals that privacy failures are prevalent (violation rates range from 15.8%-50.9%, with leakage reaching up to 26.7%) and uncovers a counterintuitive trade-off critical for industrial deployment: higher task utility often correlates with increased privacy violations. Moreover, the massive scale of enterprise data and potential user behavior further amplify this vulnerability. Simply increasing model size or reasoning depth fails to address the problem. We conclude that safeguarding enterprise workflows requires a paradigm shift, moving beyond model-centric scaling toward context-centric architectures.

</details>


### [26] [CrossCommitVuln-Bench: A Dataset of Multi-Commit Python Vulnerabilities Invisible to Per-Commit Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21917)
*Arunabh Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CrossCommitVuln-Bench基准，包含15个真实Python漏洞（CVE），其可利用条件跨多个提交引入，每个提交单独看是良性的，但合在一起构成严重漏洞。评估显示逐提交检测率仅13%，累积模式也仅27%，表明现有SAST工具在检测跨提交漏洞方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具通常基于单个快照或逐提交分析，无法检测跨多个提交逐步引入的漏洞。作者旨在系统性地构建一个跨提交漏洞基准，量化现有工具的检测盲区，推动跨提交漏洞检测研究。

Method: 人工筛选并标注15个真实Python CVE，每个漏洞标注贡献提交链、每个提交规避逐提交分析的结构化理由；使用Semgrep和Bandit在逐提交和累积扫描两种模式下进行基线评估。

Result: 逐提交检测率（CCDR）仅13%（15个漏洞中仅2个被检测到），87%的漏洞链对逐提交SAST不可见；且两次检测质量低下：一次发生在安全修复提交上（开发者会忽略告警），另一次仅检测到次要的硬编码密钥组件而完全遗漏主要漏洞（200+未保护API端点）。累积模式检测率也仅27%。

Conclusion: 当前基于快照的SAST工具严重缺失跨提交引入的漏洞，需要新的跨提交检测方法。数据集、标注方案、评估脚本和可复现基线均已开源发布。

Abstract: We present CrossCommitVuln-Bench, a curated benchmark of 15 real-world Python vulnerabilities (CVEs) in which the exploitable condition was introduced across multiple commits - each individually benign to per-commit static analysis - but collectively critical. We manually annotate each CVE with its contributing commit chain, a structured rationale for why each commit evades per-commit analysis, and baseline evaluations using Semgrep and Bandit in both per-commit and cumulative scanning modes. Our central finding: the per-commit detection rate (CCDR) is 13% across all 15 vulnerabilities - 87% of chains are invisible to per-commit SAST. Critically, both per-commit detections are qualitatively poor: one occurs on commits framed as security fixes (where developers suppress the alert), and the other detects only the minor hardcoded-key component while completely missing the primary vulnerability (200+ unprotected API endpoints). Even in cumulative mode (full codebase present), the detection rate is only 27%, confirming that snapshot-based SAST tools often miss vulnerabilities whose introduction spans multiple commits. The dataset, annotation schema, evaluation scripts, and reproducible baselines are released under open-source licenses to support research on cross-commit vulnerability detection.

</details>
