*Pawan Acharya,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非平稳环境中，攻击者能否生成既能逃避恶意软件分类器检测，又能避开漂移监控机制的可疑对抗样本。作者提出了一种结合相似性正则化的方法，通过在标准化特征空间中生成目标对抗样本，平衡误分类与漂移信号最小化。实验表明，ℓ2正则化在降低漂移信号方面效果最佳，且扰动预算显著影响逃避与可检测性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习恶意软件检测模型在非平稳环境中面临关键限制——恶意软件特征和检测系统都在持续演化。研究者希望探究：攻击者能否生成同时规避分类和漂移监控的对抗样本，揭示这一安全漏洞。

Method: 在分类器的标准化特征空间中生成目标对抗示例，并加入相似性正则化器。通过约束扰动以保持与干净恶意软件的分布相似性，构建一个平衡目标误分类与漂移信号最小化的优化目标。使用多种漂移度量对分类器输出概率进行综合比较。

Result: 相似性约束可以降低输出漂移信号，其中ℓ2正则化效果最佳。扰动预算显著影响逃避-可检测性权衡：预算增加导致攻击成功率提高，同时漂移指标也更为明显。

Conclusion: 攻击者能够生成既逃避分类又规避漂移监控的对抗样本，但扰动预算与可检测性之间存在根本性权衡，这为设计更鲁棒的防御机制提供了方向。

Abstract: Deep learning has emerged as a powerful approach for malware detection, demonstrating impressive accuracy across various data representations. However, these models face critical limitations in real-world, non-stationary environments where both malware characteristics and detection systems continuously evolve. Our research investigates a fundamental security question: Can an attacker generate adversarial malware samples that simultaneously evade classification and remain inconspicuous to drift monitoring mechanisms? We propose a novel approach that generates targeted adversarial examples in the classifier's standardized feature space, augmented with sophisticated similarity regularizers. By carefully constraining perturbations to maintain distributional similarity with clean malware, we create an optimization objective that balances targeted misclassification with drift signal minimization. We quantify the effectiveness of this approach by comprehensively comparing classifier output probabilities using multiple drift metrics. Our experiments demonstrate that similarity constraints can reduce output drift signals, with $\ell_2$ regularization showing the most promising results. We observe that perturbation budget significantly influences the evasion-detectability trade-off, with increased budget leading to higher attack success rates and more substantial drift indicators.

</details>


### [28] [Provably Secure Steganography Based on List Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21394)
*Kaiyi Pang,Minhao Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于列表解码的、理论上有高容量保证的可证明安全隐写方案，通过维护候选集而非直接定位正确秘密消息，充分利用生成文本的信息内容，并引入后缀匹配机制确保正确性。该方案即插即用，在三个LLM和七个基线上实现了与先前方案相当的计算效率，同时显著提升了嵌入容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的可证明安全隐写方案虽能保证计算不可区分性，但嵌入容量受限，原因在于熵利用率低，尤其不适合大语言模型（LLMs）固有的低熵倾向，导致可行嵌入容量严重受限。

Method: 基于列表解码的思想，维护一个包含正确秘密消息的候选集合而非直接定位，从而充分利用生成文本的信息内容；引入后缀匹配机制从候选集中区分正确的秘密消息；提供了安全性和正确性的理论证明，并推导了理论容量下界。

Result: 该方法即插即用，仅需直接替换模型的标准随机采样模块。在三个LLM和七个PSS基线上的实验表明，该方法在计算效率与先前方案相当的同时，实现了嵌入容量的大幅提升。

Conclusion: 提出的基于列表解码的可证明安全隐写方案有效解决了LLM场景下嵌入容量受限的问题，通过理论证明和实验验证了其安全性和高容量优势，且即插即用的设计便于实际部署。

Abstract: Steganography embeds secret messages in seemingly innocuous carriers for covert communication under surveillance. Current Provably Secure Steganography (PSS) schemes based on language models can guarantee computational indistinguishability between the covertext and stegotext. However, achieving high embedding capacity remains a challenge for existing PSS. The inefficient entropy utilization renders them not well-suited for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose inherent low-entropy tendencies severely constrain feasible embedding capacity. To address this, we propose a provably secure steganography scheme with a theoretically proved high capacity. Our scheme is based on the concept of list decoding: it maintains a set of candidates that contain the correct secret message, instead of directly finding the correct message with more effort. This strategy fully utilizes the information content of the generated text, yielding higher capacity. To ensure the correctness of our scheme, we further introduce a suffix-matching mechanism to distinguish the correct secret message from the candidates. We provide theoretical proofs for both the security and correctness of our scheme, alongside a derivation of its theoretical capacity lower bound.
  Our approach is plug-and-play, requiring only a direct replacement of the model's standard random sampling module. Experiments on three LLMs and seven PSS baselines demonstrate that our method achieves computational efficiency comparable to prior PSS schemes while delivering a substantial improvement in embedding capacity.

</details>


### [29] [CSC: Turning the Adversary's Poison against Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21416)
*Yuchen Shi,Xin Guo,Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC)方法，通过早期特征聚类分离中毒样本并用虚拟类重新标记来抑制后门攻击，在四个基准数据集上对十二种攻击实现接近零的攻击成功率，同时最小化干净准确率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于毒物抑制的防御方法存在两个问题：对特定攻击变体的检测不充分，以及通过遗忘方法导致模型效用下降（准确率退化）。需要一种更有效的后门防御机制。

Method: CSC包含两个阶段：(1) 分离阶段：通过标准监督学习训练DNN，从早期epoch提取特征，使用DBSCAN聚类，基于类别多样性和密度指标识别异常簇；(2) 隐藏阶段：将识别的中毒样本重新标记为虚拟类，使用交叉熵损失微调模型分类器，用良性虚拟链接替换后门关联。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上对十二种投毒攻击的评估中，CSC优于九种现有防御方法，将平均攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持最小的干净准确率损失。

Conclusion: CSC贡献包括鲁棒的后门模式识别、有效的隐藏机制和优越的实证验证，推动了可信人工智能的发展。

Abstract: Poisoning-based backdoor attacks pose significant threats to deep neural networks by embedding triggers in training data, causing models to misclassify triggered inputs as adversary-specified labels while maintaining performance on clean data. Existing poison restraint-based defenses often suffer from inadequate detection against specific attack variants and compromise model utility through unlearning methods that lead to accuracy degradation. This paper conducts a comprehensive analysis of backdoor attack dynamics during model training, revealing that poisoned samples form isolated clusters in latent space early on, with triggers acting as dominant features distinct from benign ones. Leveraging these insights, we propose Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC), a novel poison suppression defense. CSC first trains a deep neural network via standard supervised learning while segregating poisoned samples through feature extraction from early epochs, DBSCAN clustering, and identification of anomalous clusters based on class diversity and density metrics. In the concealment stage, identified poisoned samples are relabeled to a virtual class, and the model's classifier is fine-tuned using cross-entropy loss to replace the backdoor association with a benign virtual linkage, preserving overall accuracy. CSC was evaluated on four benchmark datasets against twelve poisoning-based attacks, CSC outperforms nine state-of-the-art defenses by reducing average attack success rates to near zero with minimal clean accuracy loss. Contributions include robust backdoor patterns identification, an effective concealment mechanism, and superior empirical validation, advancing trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [30] [Differentially Private De-identification of Dutch Clinical Notes: A Comparative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21421)
*Michele Miranda,Xinlan Yan,Nishant Mishra,Rachel Murphy,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Sébastien Bratières,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次比较了差分隐私(DP)、命名实体识别(NER)和大语言模型(LLM)三种方法在荷兰语临床文本去标识化中的表现，发现单独使用DP会严重损害效用，但与LLM预处理结合能显著改善隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护临床叙述中的患者隐私对于在GDPR和HIPAA等法规下实现医疗数据的二次使用至关重要，而手动去标识化成本高昂且速度慢，因此需要兼具隐私保障和高效用的自动化方法。

Method: 首次对DP、NER和LLM三种方法进行荷兰语临床文本去标识化的比较研究，分别考察各方法及混合策略（NER或LLM预处理后接DP），通过隐私泄露评估和外在评估（实体和关系分类）来衡量性能。

Result: 单独使用DP机制会严重降低效用，但将其与语言预处理结合，尤其是基于LLM的预编辑，能显著改善隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 混合策略（特别是LLM预处理+DP）在隐私保护与数据效用之间取得了更好的平衡，为自动化临床文本去标识化提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Protecting patient privacy in clinical narratives is essential for enabling secondary use of healthcare data under regulations such as GDPR and HIPAA. While manual de-identification remains the gold standard, it is costly and slow, motivating the need for automated methods that combine privacy guarantees with high utility. Most automated text de-identification pipelines employed named entity recognition (NER) to identify protected entities for redaction. Although methods based on differential privacy (DP) provide formal privacy guarantees, more recently also large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text de-identification in the clinical domain. In this work, we present the first comparative study of DP, NER, and LLMs for Dutch clinical text de-identification. We investigate these methods separately as well as hybrid strategies that apply NER or LLM preprocessing prior to DP, and assess performance in terms of privacy leakage and extrinsic evaluation (entity and relation classification). We show that DP mechanisms alone degrade utility substantially, but combining them with linguistic preprocessing, especially LLM-based redaction, significantly improves the privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [31] [A Stackelberg Model for Hybridization in Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21436)
*Willie Kouam,Stefan Rass,Zahra Seyedi,Shahzad Ahmad,Eckhard Pfluegel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从博弈论视角研究密码学中的混合加密策略，将防御者与攻击者的互动建模为资源约束下的Stackelberg博弈。防御者随机选择加密算法（经典、后量子或混合），攻击者观察到选择后选择最优密码分析方法。攻击者子问题用动态规划求解，防御者优化则转化为线性规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将密码学中的混合加密策略选择问题形式化为一个博弈论模型，考虑防御者和攻击者在资源约束下的理性决策互动，从而为混合加密的安全性和成本权衡提供理论分析框架。

Method: 采用Stackelberg博弈建模：防御者作为领导者随机化选择加密算法，攻击者作为追随者观察到防御者选择后，通过条件优化（"攻击者子博弈"）选择破解方法。攻击者子博弈用动态规划求解，防御者的Stackelberg优化问题转化为线性规划求解。

Result: 建立了防御者与攻击者在资源约束下互动的Stackelberg博弈模型，将攻击者决策形式化为条件优化问题，并提出了动态规划求解方法；防御者优化问题则成功转化为线性规划形式。

Conclusion: 该模型为混合加密策略提供了博弈论分析框架，能够帮助防御者在安全性和运行成本之间做出更优的权衡决策，为后量子密码时代的加密策略设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Similar to a strategic interaction between rational and intelligent agents, cryptography problems can be examined through the prism of game theory. In this setting, the agent aiming to protect a message is called the defender, while the one attempting to decrypt it, generally for malicious purposes, is the attacker. To strengthen security in cryptography, various strategies have been developed, among which hybridization stands out as a key concept in modern cryptographic design. This strategy allows the defender to select among different encryption algorithms (classical, post-quantum, or hybrid) while carefully balancing security and operational costs. On the other side, the attacker, limited by available resources, chooses cryptanalysis methods capable of breaching the selected algorithm. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg cryptographic hybridization problem under resource constraints. Here, the defender randomizes over encryption algorithms, and the attacker observes the choice before selecting suitable cryptanalysis methods. The attacker's decision is framed as a conditional optimization problem, which we refer to as the ``attacker subgame''. We then propose a dynamic programming approach for the attacker's subgame, while the defender's Stackelberg optimization is formulated as a linear program.

</details>


### [32] [MCP Pitfall Lab: Exposing Developer Pitfalls in MCP Tool Server Security under Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21477)
*Run Hao,Zhuoran Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCP Pitfall Lab，一个协议感知的安全测试框架，用于评估和加固MCP工具服务器。通过三个工作流挑战（邮件、文档、加密）和六种服务器变体，建模三种攻击家族（工具元数据投毒、傀儡服务器、多模态图像到工具链），实现可重现的漏洞场景测试。静态分析在36个二进制标签上对四类可静态检查的陷阱达到F1=1.0，应用加固后所有一级发现归零（29→0），风险评分从10.0降至0.0，平均仅需27行代码修改。在19次运行语料中，63.2%的运行中agent叙述与追踪证据存在分歧，凸显了基于追踪的审计与回归测试的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议在工具集成LLM智能体中广泛应用，但其多层设计和第三方服务器生态引入了工具元数据、不可信输出、跨工具流、多模态输入和供应链向量等多维风险。现有基准主要衡量对恶意输入的鲁棒性，但缺乏可操作的修复指导。

Method: 提出了MCP Pitfall Lab框架，包含三个工作流挑战（邮件、文档、加密）和六种服务器变体（基准版与加固版），建模三种攻击家族（工具元数据投毒、傀儡服务器、多模态图像到工具链）。使用MCP追踪和客观验证器（而非agent自述）验证结果，采用分层评估：Tier-1静态分析和Tier-2追踪/数据流分析。

Result: 静态分析在36个二进制标签上对P1、P2、P5、P6四类陷阱达到F1=1.0，P3（跨工具转发）和P4（图像到工具泄漏）需追踪/数据流分析。应用加固后所有29个一级发现归零，风险评分从10.0降至0.0，平均仅需27行代码修改。在19次运行中，63.2%的agent叙述与追踪证据不符，100%的sink-action运行存在分歧。

Conclusion: MCP Pitfall Lab实现了在多向量条件下对MCP工具服务器进行实用的端到端评估与加固，验证了基于追踪的审计和回归测试对检测agent与真实行为差异的重要性。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP) is increasingly adopted for tool-integrated LLM agents, but its multi-layer design and third-party server ecosystem expand risks across tool metadata, untrusted outputs, cross-tool flows, multimodal inputs, and supply-chain vectors. Existing MCP benchmarks largely measure robustness to malicious inputs but offer limited remediation guidance. We present MCP Pitfall Lab, a protocol-aware security testing framework that operationalizes developer pitfalls as reproducible scenarios and validates outcomes with MCP traces and objective validators (rather than agent self-report). We instantiate three workflow challenges (email, document, crypto) with six server variants (baseline and hardened) and model three attack families: tool-metadata poisoning, puppet servers, and multimodal image-to-tool chains, in a unified, trace-grounded evaluation. In Tier-1 static analysis over six variants (36 binary labels), our analyzer achieves F1 = 1.0 on four statically checkable pitfall classes (P1, P2, P5, P6) and flags cross-tool forwarding and image-to-tool leakage (P3, P4) as trace/dataflow-dependent. Applying recommended hardening eliminates all Tier-1 findings (29 to 0) and reduces the framework risk score (10.0 to 0.0) at a mean cost of 27 lines of code (LOC). Finally, in a preliminary 19-run corpus from the email system challenge (tool poisoning and puppet attacks), agent narratives diverge from trace evidence in 63.2% of runs and 100% of sink-action runs, motivating trace-based auditing and regression testing. Overall, Pitfall Lab enables practical, end-to-end assessment and hardening of MCP tool servers under realistic multi-vector conditions.

</details>


### [33] [Benchmarking the Utility of Privacy-Preserving Cox Regression Under Data-Driven Clipping Bounds: A Multi-Dataset Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21491)
*Keita Fukuyama,Yukiko Mori,Tomohiro Kuroda,Hiroaki Kikuchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了差分隐私（DP）机制（拉普拉斯机制和随机响应）对Cox比例风险模型统计效用的影响，使用5个临床数据集、15个ε水平（0.1-1000）和1000次蒙特卡洛迭代。结果表明：在标准DP水平（ε≤1）下，约90%的显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机水平；输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）在ε≥5时表现最佳；实际应用中，预测性能需要ε≥10，显著性保留需要ε≥30-60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私虽然能保证个体隐私，但系统评估其对生存分析中统计效用的影响仍有限，缺乏在真实临床数据上的大规模实证研究。

Method: 使用5个临床数据集（n=168-6524），在15个ε水平（0.1-1000）下，通过1000次蒙特卡洛迭代，比较三种输入扰动（仅协变量、所有输入、离散时间模型）和输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）对Cox比例风险模型的影响。使用数据驱动的裁剪边界（观测min/max），不提供正式ε-DP保障，代表正式DP下效用退化的乐观下界。

Result: ε≤1时约90%显著协变量失去显著性；预测性能在多数条件下接近随机（C-index≈0.5）；仅扰动协变量的输入扰动方法保留风险集结构且恢复最佳；输出扰动在ε≥5时保持近基线性能；n≈3000时显著性在ε=3-10快速恢复；实际需要ε≥10（预测性能）至ε≥30-60（显著性保留）；中等至高ε范围假阳性率增加。

Conclusion: 差分隐私对生存分析的统计效用有显著影响，标准DP水平下几乎无法保留统计结论；实际应用需采用较大的ε值；不同的隐私保护方法在不同ε范围和任务目标下表现差异显著，需根据具体需求选择。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is a mathematical framework that guarantees individual privacy; however, systematic evaluation of its impact on statistical utility in survival analyses remains limited. In this study, we systematically evaluated the impact of DP mechanisms (Laplace mechanism and Randomized Response) with data-driven clipping bounds on the Cox proportional hazards model, using 5 clinical datasets ($n = 168$--$6{,}524$), 15 levels of $\varepsilon$ (0.1--1000), and $B = 1{,}000$ Monte Carlo iterations. The data-driven clipping bounds used here are observed min/max and therefore do not provide formal $\varepsilon$-DP guarantees; the results represent an optimistic lower bound on utility degradation under formal DP. We compared three types of input perturbations (covariates only, all inputs, and the discrete-time model) with output perturbations (dfbeta-based sensitivity), using loss of significance rate (LSR), C-index, and coefficient bias as metrics. At standard DP levels ($\varepsilon \leq 1$), approximately 90% (90--94%) of the significant covariates lost significance, even in the largest dataset ($n = 6{,}524$), and the predictive performance approached random levels (test C-index $\approx 0.5$) under many conditions. Among the input perturbation approaches, perturbing only covariates preserved the risk-set structure and achieved the best recovery, whereas output perturbation (dfbeta-based sensitivity) maintained near-baseline performance at $\varepsilon \geq 5$. At $n \approx 3{,}000$, the significance recovered rapidly at $\varepsilon = 3$--10; however, in practice, $\varepsilon \geq 10$ (for predictive performance) to $\varepsilon \geq 30$--60 (for significance preservation) is required. In the moderate-to-high $\varepsilon$ range, false-positive rates increased for variables whose baseline $p$-values were near the significance threshold.

</details>


### [34] [Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against LLMs Based on Natural Style Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21700)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Guoheng Sun,Xicheng Zhang,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadStyle提出了一种利用LLM作为恶意样本生成器的后门攻击框架，通过风格级触发器实现隐蔽攻击，并设计了辅助目标损失函数稳定载荷注入，在多个LLM上实现了高成功率和强隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击方法存在三个主要缺陷：显式触发器模式破坏自然性、长文本生成中攻击者指定载荷注入不可靠、威胁模型不完整导致无法清晰说明后门如何在实践中传递和激活。

Method: 使用LLM作为恶意样本生成器，构造携带不可察觉风格级触发器（如写作风格模仿）的恶意样本，保持语义和流畅性；设计辅助目标损失函数，在微调时强化恶意输入中的目标内容并惩罚其在良性响应中出现；在提示驱动和PEFT两种注入策略下进行系统评估。

Result: 在LLaMA、Phi、DeepSeek、GPT系列等7个受害LLM上，BadStyle实现了高攻击成功率；辅助目标损失使风格级触发器的平均ASR提升约30%；后门在未知下游部署场景中依然有效；能一致规避输入级防御并通过简单伪装绕过输出级防御。

Conclusion: BadStyle是一个完整的后门攻击框架，通过风格级触发器和辅助损失函数解决了现有方法的自然性、稳定性和威胁模型完整性问题，展示了LLM在安全关键应用中的潜在风险。

Abstract: The growing application of large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical domains has raised urgent concerns about their security. Many recent studies have demonstrated the feasibility of backdoor attacks against LLMs. However, existing methods suffer from three key shortcomings: explicit trigger patterns that compromise naturalness, unreliable injection of attacker-specified payloads in long-form generation, and incompletely specified threat models that obscure how backdoors are delivered and activated in practice. To address these gaps, we present BadStyle, a complete backdoor attack framework and pipeline. BadStyle leverages an LLM as a poisoned sample generator to construct natural and stealthy poisoned samples that carry imperceptible style-level triggers while preserving semantics and fluency. To stabilize payload injection during fine-tuning, we design an auxiliary target loss that reinforces the attacker-specified target content in responses to poisoned inputs and penalizes its emergence in benign responses. We further ground the attack in a realistic threat model and systematically evaluate BadStyle under both prompt-induced and PEFT-based injection strategies. Extensive experiments across seven victim LLMs, including LLaMA, Phi, DeepSeek, and GPT series, demonstrate that BadStyle achieves high attack success rates (ASRs) while maintaining strong stealthiness. The proposed auxiliary target loss substantially improves the stability of backdoor activation, yielding an average ASR improvement of around 30% across style-level triggers. Even in downstream deployment scenarios unknown during injection, the implanted backdoor remains effective. Moreover, BadStyle consistently evades representative input-level defenses and bypasses output-level defenses through simple camouflage.

</details>


### [35] [Adversarial Robustness of Near-Field Millimeter-Wave Imaging under Waveform-Domain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21774)
*Lhamo Dorje,Jordan Madden,Soamar Homsi,Xiaohua Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了近场毫米波成像算法在波形域物理攻击下的对抗鲁棒性，提出了一种可微成像攻击框架，并在真实数据集上验证了10种成像算法的高脆弱性，发现深度学习算法比经典算法更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波成像已广泛应用于安检等安全关键场景，但其自身安全性尚未被充分探索，亟需研究其在物理攻击下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出白盒对抗攻击模型，利用可微成像流水线优化攻击波形；构建真实毫米波成像测试平台，采集干净和攻击波形数据集。

Result: 10种成像算法均高度脆弱，攻击者能以中等发射功率隐藏或篡改目标；深度学习算法比经典算法表现出更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 毫米波成像系统存在严重安全风险，亟需开发更鲁棒和安全的成像系统。

Abstract: Near-field millimeter-wave (mmWave) imaging is widely deployed in safety-critical applications such as airport passenger screening, yet its own security remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of the adversarial robustness of mmWave imaging algorithms under waveform-domain physical attacks that directly manipulate the image reconstruction process. We propose a practical white-box adversarial model and develop a differential imaging attack framework that leverages the differentiable imaging pipeline to optimize attack waveforms. We also construct a real measured dataset of clean and attack waveforms using a mmWave imaging testbed. Experiments on 10 representative imaging algorithms show that mmWave imaging is highly vulnerable to such attacks, enabling an adversary to conceal or alter targets with moderate transmission power. Surprisingly, deep-learning-based imaging algorithms demonstrate higher robustness than classical algorithms. These findings expose critical security risks and motivate the development of robust and secure mmWave imaging systems.

</details>


### [36] [Black-Box Skill Stealing Attack from Proprietary LLM Agents: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21829)
*Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Yu Liu,Chi Liu,Qingchuan Zhao,Hongwei Li,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了针对LLM智能体系统的黑盒技能窃取攻击，发现仅需3次交互即可提取专有技能内容，并提出了多阶段防御策略，揭示了当前商业智能体生态系统在版权保护方面的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体技能经济（技能市场已有超过9万条技能、创作者收入超过10万美元）的兴起，技能成为可重复使用的核心资产。然而，攻击者可通过与公开智能体交互来窃取隐藏的专有技能内容，造成严重的版权风险。目前缺乏对这一新兴攻击面的系统性研究。

Method: 首先从已有提示窃取方法中推导出攻击分类法，然后构建自动化窃取提示生成智能体：从模型生成的种子提示出发，通过场景合理化（scenario rationalization）和结构注入（structure injection）进行扩展，并利用嵌入过滤（embedding filtering）确保多样性。在3种商业智能体架构和5种LLM上评估攻击效果，并设计输入、推理、输出三阶段防御策略。

Result: 仅需3次交互即可成功提取智能体技能，攻击成本低廉且易于自动化。尽管提出的三阶段防御取得较好效果，但攻击者可反复尝试不同变体，只需一次成功即可突破保护。

Conclusion: 当前商业智能体生态系统中版权风险被严重忽视，亟需更强健的防御策略以提供更可靠的保护保障。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on skills to encapsulate reusable capabilities via progressively disclosed instructions. High-quality skills inject expert knowledge into general-purpose models, improving performance on specialized tasks. This quality and ease of dissemination drive the emergence of a skill economy: free skill marketplaces already report 90368 published skills, while paid marketplaces report more than 2000 listings and over $100,000 in creator earnings. Yet this growing marketplace also creates a new attack surface, as adversaries can interact with public agent to extract hidden proprietary skill content. We present the first empirical study of black-box skill stealing against LLM agent systems. To study this threat, we first derive an attack taxonomy from prior prompt-stealing methods and build an automated stealing prompt generation agent. This agent starts from model-generated seed prompts, expands them through scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity via embedding filtering. This process yields a reproducible pipeline for evaluating agent systems. We evaluate such attacks across 3 commercial agent architectures and 5 LLMs. Our results show that agent skills can be extracted with only 3 interactions, posing a serious copyright risk. To mitigate this threat, we design defenses across three stages of the agent pipeline: input, inference, and output. Although these defenses achieve strong results, the attack remains inexpensive and readily automatable, allowing an adversary to launch repeated attempts with different variants; only one successful attempt is sufficient to compromise the protected skill. Overall, our findings suggest that these copyright risks are largely overlooked across proprietary agent ecosystems. We therefore advocate for more robust defense strategies that provide stronger protection guarantees.

</details>


### [37] [Cross-Modal Phantom: Coordinated Camera-LiDAR Spoofing Against Multi-Sensor Fusion in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21841)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自动驾驶多传感器融合（MSF）中一个未被充分探索的漏洞：攻击者通过伪造跨传感器一致性（红外投影欺骗相机+LiDAR信号注入），使多个传感器对同一虚假目标达成一致，从而绕过MSF的冗余安全机制。在KITTI数据集上的实验表明，这种协调欺骗对最先进感知模型的攻击成功率达85.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多传感器融合通过冗余设计来防范单传感器故障，但融合过程本身可能引入新的脆弱性。攻击者能否通过制造跨传感器一致性来绕过这种冗余机制，是一个尚未被充分研究的潜在安全问题。

Method: 设计了一种数据级（早期融合）协调攻击：通过模拟红外投影（生成透视感知的图像块）和LiDAR信号注入（生成3D点簇）两种物理欺骗源，使得相机和LiDAR在空间上对齐地产生一致的虚假目标。使用KITTI数据集中的400个场景进行大规模评估。

Result: 对最先进的感知模型实现了85.5%的成功攻击率。

Conclusion: 首次提供了定量证据，证明恶意的跨模态一致性可以危害基于多传感器融合的感知系统，揭示了现代自动驾驶汽车核心数据融合逻辑中的关键漏洞。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) increasingly depend on Multi-Sensor Fusion (MSF) to combine complementary modalities such as cameras and LiDAR for robust perception. While this redundancy is intended to safeguard against single-sensor failures, the fusion process itself introduces a subtle and underexplored vulnerability. In this work, we investigate whether an attacker can bypass MSF's redundancy by fabricating cross-sensor consistency, making multiple sensors agree on the same false object. We design a coordinated, data-level (early-fusion) attack that emulates the outcome of two synchronized physical spoofing sources: an infrared (IR) projection that induces a false camera detection and a LiDAR signal injection that produces a matching 3D point cluster. Rather than implementing the physical attack hardware, we simulate its sensor-level outcomes by inserting perspective-aware image patches and synthetic LiDAR point clusters aligned in 3D space. This approach preserves the perceptual effects that real IR and IEMI-based spoofing would create at the sensor output. Using 400 KITTI scenes, our large-scale evaluation shows that the coordinated spoofing deceives a state-of-the-art perception model with an 85.5% successful attack rate. These findings provide the first quantitative evidence that malicious cross-modal consistency can compromise MSF-based perception, revealing a critical vulnerability in the core data-fusion logic of modern autonomous vehicle systems.

</details>

### [38] [A Tendon-Driven Wrist Abduction-Adduction Joint Improves Performance of a 5 DoF Upper Limb Exoskeleton -- Implementation and Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20898)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher,Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在EXOTIC2外骨骼中集成主动腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）关节对上肢康复功能的影响。实验表明，该关节显著改善了饮水任务中的泼洒率（从56%降至3%）和抓挠任务中的水平成功率（从28%提升至75%），且未增加任务完成时间，为主动腕部控制辅助日常生活活动提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部功能对日常生活活动至关重要，但目前关于上肢外骨骼中腕部外展-内收关节辅助对功能影响的实验证据有限，尤其是针对严重运动功能障碍患者。

Method: 在五自由度EXOTIC2外骨骼中集成紧凑轻量化的肌腱驱动外展、弹簧驱动内收的腕部模块。8名无运动障碍成年人及1名肌萎缩侧索硬化症患者，在随机腕部启用/锁定条件下完成饮水和抓挠任务，评估腕部活动范围、任务完成时间、泼洒量和水平对齐等指标。

Result: 启用腕部Ab-Ad关节后，饮水任务泼洒发生率从56%降至3%，抓挠任务水平对齐成功率从28%提升至75%，任务完成时间未显著增加。

Conclusion: 集成腕部外展-内收辅助显著改善了外骨骼辅助日常生活活动的关键功能任务指标，为主动腕部控制增强任务级表现提供了实验证据。

Abstract: Wrist function is essential in performing activities of daily living (ADLs). However, there is limited experimental evidence on the functional impact of wrist Abduction-Adduction (Ab-Ad) joint assistance in upper limb exoskeletons (ULEs) for rehabilitation. This study evaluates the effect of implementing an active wrist Ab-Ad joint in a five degree of freedom (DoF) ULE, EXOTIC2 exoskeleton, to support individuals with severe motor impairments. Methods: A compact, lightweight wrist module with tendon-driven abduction and spring-driven adduction was integrated into the EXOTIC exoskeleton. Eight adults with no motor disabilities completed drinking and scratching tasks under randomized wrist-enabled and wrist-locked conditions along with a preliminary feasibility test in one individual with Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Kinematic and task performance metrics including wrist range of motion, task completion time, spillage and leveling metrics were assessed. Results: Implementing the wrist Ab-Ad DoF improved task success metrics. Spill incidence during the drinking task decreased from 56% to 3%, and leveling success for scratching task improved from 28% to 75%. Conclusion: Integrating wrist Ab-Ad assistance improved key functional task outcomes without increasing execution time. Significance: The study provides the experimental evidence that active wrist Ab-Ad control enhances task-level performance in exoskeleton-assisted ADLs.

</details>


### [39] [Clinical Evaluation of a Tongue-Controlled Wrist Abduction-Adduction Assistance in a 6-DoF Upper-Limb Exoskeleton for Individuals with ALS and SCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20967)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了舌控6自由度上肢外骨骼EXOTIC2中腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）辅助功能对日常生活活动的影响。通过对1名肌萎缩侧索硬化症和5名脊髓损伤患者进行饮水与刮棒任务测试，发现启用腕部Ab-Ad辅助可显著提高任务成功率（溢漏率从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败率从66.7%降至16.7%），且不增加用户不适感，但效果受任务情境、控制可用性和个体策略影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部自由度（特别是外展-内收）在临床场景中的实际效用尚未被充分评估，缺乏以用户为中心的证据支持其在辅助外骨骼中的必要性。

Method: 采用被试内设计，比较6名严重运动障碍患者在舌控6-DoF外骨骼EXOTIC2下，有/无腕部Ab-Ad辅助时执行饮水与刮棒两项ADL任务的表现，评估指标包括任务成功率、完成时间、运动学数据和可用性问卷。

Result: 腕部Ab-Ad辅助使溢漏率从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败率从66.7%降至16.7%；参与者使用任务特异性的腕部活动范围子集；问卷显示无额外不适感且用户感知到功能提升。

Conclusion: 腕部Ab-Ad辅助能提升外骨骼辅助下的功能性任务表现且不牺牲用户舒适度，但其有效性依赖于任务情境、控制可用性和个体策略，研究为ULE设计中纳入腕部Ab-Ad提供了临床相关且以用户为中心的证据支持。

Abstract: Upper-limb exoskeletons (ULEs) have the potential to restore functional independence in individuals with severe motor impairments; however, the clinical relevance of wrist degrees of freedom (DoF), particularly abduction-adduction (Ab-Ad), remains insufficiently evaluated. This study investigates the functional and user-perceived impact of wrist Ab-Ad assistance during two activities of daily living (ADLs). Wrist Ab-Ad assistance in a tongue-controlled 6-DoF ULE, EXOTIC2, was evaluated in a within-subject study involving one individual with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis and five individuals with spinal cord injury. Participants performed drinking and scratch stick leveling tasks with EXOTIC2 under two conditions: with and without wrist Ab-Ad assistance. Outcome measure included task success, task completion time, kinematic measures, and a usability questionnaire capturing comfort, functional perception, and acceptance. Enabling wrist Ab-Ad improved task success rates across both ADLs, with consistent reductions in spillage (from 77.8% spillages to 22.2%) and failed placements (from 66.7% to 16.7%). Participants utilized task-specific subsets of the available wrist range of motion, indicating that effective control within functional ranges was more critical than maximal joint excursion. Questionnaire responses indicated no increase in discomfort with the additional DoF and reflected perceived improvements in task performance. In conclusion, wrist Ab-Ad assistance enhances functional task performance in assistive exoskeleton use without compromising user comfort. However, its effectiveness depends on task context, control usability, and individual user strategies. This study provides clinically relevant, user-centered evidence supporting the inclusion of wrist Ab-Ad in ULEs, emphasizing the importance of balancing functional capability with usability in assistive device design.

</details>


### [40] [PREVENT-JACK: Context Steering for Swarms of Long Heavy Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21337)
*Adrian Baruck,Michael Dubé,Christoph Steup,Sanaz Mostaghim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Prevent-Jack框架，将稀疏覆盖上下文引导应用于重型铰接车辆（HAV）群，融合六种局部行为以防止折叠和碰撞，通过15000次模拟评估了多至十节拖车的车辆群性能，发现死锁和活锁在更大群体和更密集场景中更频繁发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展传统群机器人学中点状质点机器人表示，研究运动学受限、细长且铰接的重型铰接车辆（HAV）的局部去中心化协同控制，以满足现实应用需求。

Method: 提出Prevent-Jack方法，引入稀疏覆盖上下文引导（Sparsely Covered Context Steering）框架，融合六种局部行为以提供防折叠和防碰撞保证，并通过参数研究评估Evade Attraction行为对死锁预防的重要性，使用15000次模拟测试多至十节拖车的车辆群性能。

Result: 死锁和活锁在更大群体和更密集场景中更频繁发生，峰值平均影响27%/31%的车辆；较大群体表现出更多等待行为，较小群体则更多规避行为。

Conclusion: Prevent-Jack方法能有效防止折叠和碰撞，但代价是潜在的死锁和活锁；Evade Attraction行为对死锁预防至关重要；群体规模和场景密度显著影响死锁/活锁发生率及车辆行为模式。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim to extend the traditional point-mass-like robot representation in swarm robotics and instead study a swarm of long Heavy Articulated Vehicles (HAVs). HAVs are kinematically constrained, elongated, and articulated, introducing unique challenges. Local, decentralized coordination of these vehicles is motivated by many real-world applications. Our approach, Prevent-Jack, introduces the sparsely covered context steering framework in robotics. It fuses six local behaviors, providing guarantees against jackknifing and collisions at the cost of potential dead- and livelocks, tested for vehicles with up to ten trailers. We highlight the importance of the Evade Attraction behavior for deadlock prevention using a parameter study, and use 15,000 simulations to evaluate the swarm performance. Our extensive experiments and the results show that both the dead- and livelocks occur more frequently in larger swarms and denser scenarios, affecting a peak average of 27%/31% of vehicles. We observe that larger swarms exhibit increased waiting, while smaller swarms show increased evasion.

</details>


### [41] [A Survey of Legged Robotics in Non-Inertial Environments: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20990)
*I-Chia Chang,Xinyan Huang,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Sangli Teng,Wenjing Li,Maani Ghaffari,Jingang Yi,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统回顾了足式机器人在非惯性环境（如移动、倾斜或加速的地面）中的建模、状态估计与控制方法，分析了性能退化的根本原因，并指出了该领域在耦合动力学、可观测性、鲁棒性及实验验证等方面的开放问题与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统足式机器人研究假设地面是刚性与静止的，但在运输、海事、航空等实际场景中，支撑地面会移动、倾斜或加速，导致常规方法失效。为提升足式机器人在动态真实环境中的运动可靠性，需系统梳理非惯性环境下的技术挑战与现有方法。

Method: 通过总结代表性应用领域与运动特征，分析足式机器人性能退化的根本原因，综述现有建模、状态估计与控制方法及其关键假设与局限性，识别开放问题。

Result: 归纳了非惯性环境下的主要技术挑战（如机器人-环境耦合、可观测性、鲁棒性），指出现有方法在实验验证方面的不足。

Conclusion: 明确了该新兴领域的技术基础，提出了未来在自主性、系统级设计、仿生策略、安全性与测试等方面的发展方向，以推动可靠足式机器人在真实动态环境中的应用。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated remarkable agility on rigid, stationary ground, but their locomotion reliability remains limited in non-inertial environments, where the supporting ground moves, tilts, or accelerates. Such conditions arise in ground transportation, maritime platforms, and aerospace settings, and they introduce persistent time-varying disturbances that break the stationary-ground assumptions underlying conventional legged locomotion. This survey reviews the state of the art in modeling, state estimation, and control for legged robots in non-inertial environments. We summarize representative application domains and motion characteristics, analyze the root causes of locomotion performance degradation, and review existing methods together with their key assumptions and limitations. We further identify open problems in robot-environment coupling, observability, robustness, and experimental validation, and discuss future directions in autonomy, system-level design, bio-inspired strategies, safety, and testing. The survey aims to clarify the technical foundations of this emerging area and support the development of reliable legged robots for real-world dynamic environments.

</details>

Abstract: Wrist exoskeletons play a vital role in rehabilitation and assistive applications, yet conventional actuation mechanisms such as electric motors or pneumatics often introduce undesirable weight, friction, and complexity. This paper presents a novel single-cable (tendon), torsional-spring-assisted actuation mechanism for wrist abduction-adduction, and a simulation-based method for selecting its stiffness parameters. The mechanism employs a single Bowden cable passively tensioned by a spiral torsional spring (clock spring) to maintain continuous cable tension without antagonistic actuation. Kinematic and dynamic modeling of the mechanism was performed to estimate the required torque and identify optimal spring parameters. These simulation-derived parameters guided the design of a functional prototype, which was experimentally evaluated with five participants with no motor disabilities (NMD) under varying arm positions and loading conditions using three spring configurations to account for user variability and modeling uncertainties. Experimental results show consistent agreement with simulation-derived trends, with the nominal spring configuration achieving balanced motion range, torque demand, and repeatability. The results demonstrate that simulation-informed stiffness selection can effectively guide the design of compact, cable-driven wrist exoskeletons while reducing reliance on empirical tuning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中在机器人设计、编队组成和规划等领域的独立优化，缺乏考虑跨领域耦合决策与任务需求的系统级协同设计方法。

Method: 基于单调协同设计理论，将机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器抽象为具有明确定义接口的互联设计问题，这些抽象独立于具体实现和任务，从而支持在任务特定性能约束下进行高效的联合优化。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent robotic systems requires reasoning across tightly coupled decisions spanning heterogeneous domains, including robot design, fleet composition, and planning. Much effort has been devoted to isolated improvements in these domains, whereas system-level co-design considering trade-offs and task requirements remains underexplored. In this work, we present a formal and compositional framework for the task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Building on a monotone co-design theory, we introduce general abstractions of robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators as interconnected design problems with well-defined interfaces that are agnostic to both implementations and tasks. This structure enables efficient joint optimization of robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific performance constraints. A series of case studies demonstrates the capabilities of the framework. Various component models can be seamlessly incorporated, including new robot types, task profiles, and probabilistic sensing objectives, while non-obvious design alternatives are systematically uncovered with optimality guarantees. The results highlight the flexibility, scalability, and interpretability of the proposed approach, and illustrate how formal co-design enables principled reasoning about complex heterogeneous multi-robot systems.

</details>
