TL;DR: 本文介绍了Open-H-Embodiment，这是迄今为止最大的开放医疗机器人视频与同步运动学数据集，涵盖49家机构和多种机器人平台。基于该数据集训练了两个基础模型：GR00T-H（首个开放的医疗机器人视觉-语言-动作基础模型）和Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator（首个多体态手术模拟世界模型），展示了大规模开放数据对医疗机器人研究的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主医疗机器人面临根本性数据问题：现有数据集规模小、单一形态、极少开放共享，限制了基础模型的发展。因此需要构建大规模、多形态、开放共享的医疗机器人数据集以推动领域进步。

Method: 收集了来自49+机构、涵盖CMR Versius、达芬奇、dVRK、Rob Surgical BiTrack、MIRA、Moon Surgical Maestro等多种机器人平台的医疗机器人视频与同步运动学数据，形成Open-H-Embodiment数据集。基于该数据训练了GR00T-H（VLA模型）和Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator（动作条件世界模型）。

Result: GR00T-H在结构化缝合基准测试中实现了25%的端到端任务完成率（其他模型为0%），在29步离体缝合序列上平均成功率达64%。Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator支持从单个检查点进行9种机器人平台的跨体态手术模拟，支持策略评估和合成数据生成。

Conclusion: 开放的大规模医疗机器人数据收集可以作为研究社区的关键基础设施，推动机器人学习和世界建模等领域的进步。

Abstract: Autonomous medical robots hold promise to improve patient outcomes, reduce provider workload, democratize access to care, and enable superhuman precision. However, autonomous medical robotics has been limited by a fundamental data problem: existing medical robotic datasets are small, single-embodiment, and rarely shared openly, restricting the development of foundation models that the field needs to advance. We introduce Open-H-Embodiment, the largest open dataset of medical robotic video with synchronized kinematics to date, spanning more than 49 institutions and multiple robotic platforms including the CMR Versius, Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci, da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), Rob Surgical BiTrack, Virtual Incision's MIRA, Moon Surgical Maestro, and a variety of custom systems, spanning surgical manipulation, robotic ultrasound, and endoscopy procedures. We demonstrate the research enabled by this dataset through two foundation models. GR00T-H is the first open foundation vision-language-action model for medical robotics, which is the only evaluated model to achieve full end-to-end task completion on a structured suturing benchmark (25% of trials vs. 0% for all others) and achieves 64% average success across a 29-step ex vivo suturing sequence. We also train Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator, the first action-conditioned world model to enable multi-embodiment surgical simulation from a single checkpoint, spanning nine robotic platforms and supporting in silico policy evaluation and synthetic data generation for the medical domain. These results suggest that open, large-scale medical robot data collection can serve as critical infrastructure for the research community, enabling advances in robot learning, world modeling, and beyond.

</details>


### [45] [Neuro-Symbolic Manipulation Understanding with Enriched Semantic Event Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21053)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出eSEC-LAM，一种将经典语义事件链(eSEC)转化为显式事件级符号状态的神经符号框架，通过置信度感知谓词、功能物体角色、 affordance先验、原始层抽象和显著性引导解释线索增强符号状态，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100、VISOR和Assembly101数据集上实现竞争性的动作识别、显著提升的下一个原始动作预测，并对感知噪声具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典语义事件链(eSEC)虽能提供可解释的关系描述，但本质上仍是描述性的，无法直接支持不确定感知下的决策推理，缺乏对操作理解的概率化、结构化表达能力。

Method: 提出eSEC-LAM框架：(1) 基于基础模型的感知前端提取确定性谓词；(2) 将eSEC转化为置信度感知谓词、功能物体角色、affordance先验、原始层抽象和显著性引导解释线索构成的显式符号状态；(3) 通过基于原始操作前/后置条件的轻量级符号推理进行当前动作推断和下一个原始动作预测。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，eSEC-LAM在动作识别上达到竞争性性能，显著提升下一个原始动作预测精度，在感知退化条件下比传统符号方法和端到端视频基线更鲁棒，并提供基于显式关系证据的时间一致性解释轨迹。

Conclusion: 富语义事件链不仅可以作为可解释的操作描述符，还可以作为神经符号动作推理的有效内部状态表示。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in human environments must reason about how object interactions evolve over time, which actions are currently being performed, and what manipulation step is likely to follow. Classical enriched Semantic Event Chains (eSECs) provide an interpretable relational description of manipulation, but remain primarily descriptive and do not directly support uncertainty-aware decision making. In this paper, we propose eSEC-LAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that transforms eSECs into an explicit event-level symbolic state for manipulation understanding. The proposed formulation augments classical eSECs with confidence-aware predicates, functional object roles, affordance priors, primitive-level abstraction, and saliency-guided explanation cues. These enriched symbolic states are derived from a foundation-model-based perception front-end through deterministic predicate extraction, while current-action inference and next-primitive prediction are performed using lightweight symbolic reasoning over primitive pre- and post-conditions. We evaluate the proposed framework on EPIC-KITCHENS-100, EPIC-KITCHENS VISOR, and Assembly101 across action recognition, next-primitive prediction, robustness to perception noise, and explanation consistency. Experimental results show that eSEC-LAM achieves competitive action recognition, substantially improves next-primitive prediction, remains more robust under degraded perceptual conditions than both classical symbolic and end-to-end video baselines, and provides temporally consistent explanation traces grounded in explicit relational evidence. These findings demonstrate that enriched Semantic Event Chains can serve not only as interpretable descriptors of manipulation, but also as effective internal states for neuro-symbolic action reasoning.

</details>


### [46] [Impact-Aware Model Predictive Control for UAV Landing on a Heaving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21078)
*Jess Stephenson,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种冲击感知的模型预测控制（MPC）框架，通过将着陆建模为基于牛顿恢复系数的速度级刚体冲击，并将其作为线性互补问题（LCP）嵌入MPC动力学中，以预测不连续冲击后速度并抑制反弹，在仿真和实验中分别实现了着陆鲁棒性提升和86.2%的冲击后偏转减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在起伏海洋平台上着陆时，由于相对垂直运动会产生巨大冲击力并导致触地反弹，现有控制方法难以有效应对这一挑战。

Method: 将着陆过程建模为基于牛顿恢复系数的速度级刚体冲击，以线性互补问题（LCP）形式嵌入MPC动力学中，实现冲击感知的预测与控制。

Result: 仿真中恢复了感知预测降低了冲击前相对速度并提高了着陆鲁棒性；在起伏甲板实验台上，相比跟踪MPC，冲击后偏转减少了86.2%。

Conclusion: 所提出的冲击感知MPC框架能有效抑制无人机在起伏平台着陆时的反弹，显著降低冲击后偏转，提升着陆安全性。

Abstract: Landing UAVs on heaving marine platforms is challenging because relative vertical motion can generate large impact forces and cause rebound on touchdown. To address this, we develop an impact-aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that models landing as a velocity-level rigid-body impact governed by Newton's restitution law. We embed this as a linear complementarity problem (LCP) within the MPC dynamics to predict the discontinuous post-impact velocity and suppress rebound. In simulation, restitution-aware prediction reduces pre-impact relative velocity and improves landing robustness. Experiments on a heaving-deck testbed show an 86.2% reduction in post-impact deflection compared to a tracking MPC.

</details>


### [47] [Self-Predictive Representation for Autonomous UAV Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21130)
*Angel Ayala,Donling Sui,Francisco Cruz,Mitchell Torok,Mohammad Deghat,Bruno J. T. Fernandes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为AmelPred/AmelPredSto的新型自预测状态表征学习模型，用于解决无人机在未知目标三维物体目标导航中的数据样本效率问题，实验表明其随机版本与actor-critic算法结合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在无人机导航中面临数据样本效率低下和未知目标识别两大挑战，现有方法多依赖坐标导航而非直接搜索目标，亟需提升样本效率。

Method: 将未知目标位置形式化为MDP；提出AmelPred自预测模型（及其随机变体AmelPredSto）作为感知模块；将其与无模型RL算法结合，在3D OGN场景中分析不同状态表征学习方法与RL算法的交互。

Result: AmelPredSto是表现最佳的SRL模型，与actor-critic算法结合时显著提升了RL算法的样本效率，在解决OGN问题上取得实质性改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的自预测状态表征方法能有效提升无人机在未知目标三维导航中的样本效率，为RL在无人机自主导航中的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have revolutionized industries through their versatility with applications including aerial surveillance, search and rescue, agriculture, and delivery. Their autonomous capabilities offer unique advantages, such as operating in large open space environments. Reinforcement Learning (RL) empowers UAVs to learn intricate navigation policies, enabling them to optimize flight behavior autonomously. However, one of its main challenge is the inefficiency in using data sample to achieve a good policy. In object-goal navigation (OGN) settings, target recognition arises as an extra challenge. Most UAV-related approaches use relative or absolute coordinates to move from an initial position to a predefined location, rather than to find the target directly. This study addresses the data sample efficiency issue in solving a 3D OGN problem, in addition to, the formalization of the unknown target location setting as a Markov decision process. Experiments are conducted to analyze the interplay of different state representation learning (SRL) methods for perception with a model-free RL algorithm for planning in an autonomous navigation system. The main contribution of this study is the development of the perception module, featuring a novel self-predictive model named AmelPred. Empirical results demonstrate that its stochastic version, AmelPredSto, is the best-performing SRL model when combined with actor-critic RL algorithms. The obtained results show substantial improvement in RL algorithms' efficiency by using AmelPredSto in solving the OGN problem.

</details>


### [48] [Navigating the Clutter: Waypoint-Based Bi-Level Planning for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21138)
*Jiabao Ji,Yongchao Chen,Yang Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Chuchu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合多机器人控制框架，通过引入路径点（waypoints）作为运动轨迹的简洁表达，并结合基于课程学习的改进RLVR算法，实现任务规划与运动规划的联合优化，在密集障碍物和多机器人场景中显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在杂乱环境中的控制面临复杂的物理约束（机器人间碰撞、机器人与障碍物碰撞、不可达运动），而高层任务规划与低层运动规划的失败都可能导致约束违反。由于低层运动轨迹的参数化复杂以及跨两个规划层的信用分配模糊，联合优化任务与运动规划非常困难。

Method: 提出混合多机器人控制框架：1）引入路径点（waypoints）作为运动轨迹的简单且具表现力的表示，实现低层规划的有效参数化；2）采用基于课程学习的训练策略，结合改进的RLVR算法，将运动规划器的运动可行性反馈传播到任务规划器。

Result: 在BoxNet3D-OBS基准测试（密集障碍物、最多9个机器人）上，该方法一致性地优于忽略运动信息的基线和基于VLA的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化任务与运动规划，并设计有效的参数化表示和信用分配机制，该方法能有效提升多机器人在复杂环境中的控制性能。

Abstract: Multi-robot control in cluttered environments is a challenging problem that involves complex physical constraints, including robot-robot collisions, robot-obstacle collisions, and unreachable motions. Successful planning in such settings requires joint optimization over high-level task planning and low-level motion planning, as violations of physical constraints may arise from failures at either level. However, jointly optimizing task and motion planning is difficult due to the complex parameterization of low-level motion trajectories and the ambiguity of credit assignment across the two planning levels. In this paper, we propose a hybrid multi-robot control framework that jointly optimizes task and motion planning. To enable effective parameterization of low-level planning, we introduce waypoints, a simple yet expressive representation for motion trajectories. To address the credit assignment challenge, we adopt a curriculum-based training strategy with a modified RLVR algorithm that propagates motion feasibility feedback from the motion planner to the task planner. Experiments on BoxNet3D-OBS, a challenging multi-robot benchmark with dense obstacles and up to nine robots, show that our approach consistently improves task success over motion-agnostic and VLA-based baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/UCSB-NLP-Chang/navigate-cluster

</details>


### [49] [A Replicable Robotics Awareness Method Using LLM-Enabled Robotics Interaction: Evidence from a Corporate Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21377)
*S. A. Prieto,M. A. Gopee,Y. Ben Arab,B. García de Soto,J. Esteba,P. Olivera Brizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于挑战的机器人意识提升方法，通过将LLM赋能的人形机器人活动引入AD Ports Group的实际工作场景，让非专业员工通过语音指令与机器人协作完成物流任务。102份问卷显示该方法在满意度（8.46/10）、兴趣提升（4.47/5）和人机协作理解（4.45/5）方面表现良好，但可靠性和可预测性评分较低，指出了未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏将LLM赋能机器人用于非专业用户在真实组织环境中进行结构化机器人意识提升的证据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用挑战式学习方法，设计基于LLM控制框架的仿人机器人活动，员工在物流场景中通过语音命令进行团队角色扮演式人机协作，随后通过16天的问卷调查收集102份反馈进行定量评估。

Result: 活动获得高满意度（8.46/10），显著提升了参与者对机器人/AI的兴趣（4.47/5）和对人机协作的理解（4.45/5）；直接交互者报告交互自然（4.37/5）且随进程更易上手（4.74/5）；但可靠性和可预测性评分较低。

Conclusion: 基于挑战的LLM赋能人形机器人交互是一种有前景且可复制的工业环境机器人意识提升方法，但需解决技术可靠性和可预测性等设计挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being explored as interfaces between humans and robotic systems, yet there remains limited evidence on how such technologies can be used not only for interaction, but also as a structured means of introducing robotics to non-specialist users in real organizational settings. This paper introduces and evaluates a challenge-based method for robotics awareness, implemented through an LLM-enabled humanoid robot activity conducted with employees of AD Ports Group in the United Arab Emirates. In the event, participants engaged with a humanoid robot in a logistics-inspired task environment using voice commands interpreted through an LLM-based control framework. The activity was designed as a team-based, role-driven experience intended to expose participants to embodied AI and human-robot collaboration without requiring prior robotics expertise. To evaluate the approach, a post-event survey remained open for 16 days and collected 102 responses. Results indicate strong overall reception, with high satisfaction (8.46/10), increased interest in robotics and AI (4.47/5), and improved understanding of emerging forms of human-robot collaboration (4.45/5). Participants who interacted directly with the robot also reported natural interaction (4.37/5) and a strong sense that interaction became easier as the activity progressed (4.74/5). At the same time, lower ratings for reliability and predictability point to important technical and design challenges for future iterations. The findings suggest that challenge-based, LLM-enabled humanoid interaction can serve as a promising and replicable method for robotics awareness in industrial and operational environments.

</details>


### [50] [Full-Body Dynamic Safety for Robot Manipulators: 3D Poisson Safety Functions for CBF-Based Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21189)
*Meg Wilkinson,Gilbert Bahati,Ryan M. Bena,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用3D泊松安全函数（PSF）实现机械臂在动态环境中的全身碰撞避免，通过庞特里亚金差构造缓冲域并求解泊松方程生成全局光滑的控制障碍函数，在7自由度机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CBF的安全滤波器在高维构型空间中需要强制执行大量全身安全约束，导致理论和计算上的挑战，亟需一种既能保证全身碰撞避免又具有计算可行性的框架。

Method: 1) 对环境占据数据进行采样，按预设分辨率对机械臂表面采样；2) 通过庞特里亚金差根据采样分辨率收缩自由空间构造缓冲域；3) 在缓冲域上求解泊松方程，合成全局光滑的CBF（即3D泊松安全函数）；4) 将每个采样点的安全函数值作为任务空间CBF约束，通过多约束二次规划实现实时安全滤波器。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂动态环境实验中验证了框架的有效性，证明在缓冲域中保持采样点安全即可保证整个连续机器人表面的碰撞避免。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将全身碰撞避免问题转化为单个泊松方程求解的框架，降低了多约束CBF的计算复杂度，同时提供了严格的安全性保证，适用于动态环境下的实时控制。

Abstract: Collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires enforcing full-body safety constraints in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Control Barrier Function (CBF) based safety filters have proven effective in enabling safe behaviors, but enforcing the high number of constraints needed for safe manipulation leads to theoretic and computational challenges. This work presents a framework for full-body collision avoidance for manipulators in dynamic environments by leveraging 3D Poisson Safety Functions (PSFs). In particular, given environmental occupancy data, we sample the manipulator surface at a prescribed resolution and shrink free space via a Pontryagin difference according to this resolution. On this buffered domain, we synthesize a globally smooth CBF by solving Poisson's equation, yielding a single safety function for the entire environment. This safety function, evaluated at each sampled point, yields task-space CBF constraints enforced by a real-time safety filter via a multi-constraint quadratic program. We prove that keeping the sample points safe in the buffered region guarantees collision avoidance for the entire continuous robot surface. The framework is validated on a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [51] [How VLAs (Really) Work In Open-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21192)
*Amir Rasouli,Yangzheng Wu,Zhiyuan Li,Rui Heng Yang,Xuan Zhao,Charles Eret,Sajjad Pakdamansavoji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出当前视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在BEHAVIOR1K等基准上的评估仅关注最终成功率，忽略了操作安全性和过程一致性。作者分析了现有SOTA模型的鲁棒性、安全性、任务意识等问题，并提出了包含安全违规评估的新协议，最后讨论了VLA模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在长时域任务基准中的评估仅考虑最终状态的成功率，无法反映操作过程中的安全问题和真实性能，这可能夸大报告结果并掩盖实际部署中的核心挑战。

Method: 对BEHAVIOR1K Challenge上的SOTA模型进行全面分析，从可重复性、一致性、安全性、任务意识及任务未完成的关键因素等维度评估策略，并提出包含安全违规检测的新评估协议。

Result: 现有评估协议无法捕捉安全违规行为，导致性能被高估；提出的新协议能更真实地衡量策略在复杂交互场景下的实际表现。

Conclusion: 需要引入考虑安全性和过程一致性的评估指标，以推动VLA模型向真实世界安全部署的方向发展。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have been extensively used in robotics applications, achieving great success in various manipulation problems. More recently, VLAs have been used in long-horizon tasks and evaluated on benchmarks, such as BEHAVIOR1K (B1K), for solving complex household chores. The common metric for measuring progress in such benchmarks is success rate or partial score based on satisfaction of progress-agnostic criteria, meaning only the final states of the objects are considered, regardless of the events that lead to such states. In this paper, we argue that using such evaluation protocols say little about safety aspects of operation and can potentially exaggerate reported performance, undermining core challenges for future real-world deployment. To this end, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art models on the B1K Challenge and evaluate policies in terms of robustness via reproducibility and consistency of performance, safety aspects of policies operations, task awareness, and key elements leading to the incompletion of tasks. We then propose evaluation protocols to capture safety violations to better measure the true performance of the policies in more complex and interactive scenarios. At the end, we discuss the limitations of the existing VLAs and motivate future research.

</details>


### [52] [CorridorVLA: Explicit Spatial Constraints for Generative Action Heads via Sparse Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21241)
*Dachong Li,ZhuangZhuang Chen,Jin Zhang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CorridorVLA通过预测稀疏空间锚点（Δ-位置）定义显式容差走廊，引导流匹配动作头生成，在LIBERO-Plus基准上提升成功率3.4%-12.4%，GR00T-Corr变体达83.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常通过隐式潜在特征注入空间引导，缺乏显式且可解释的物理约束，导致动作生成与物理空间的对齐不足。

Method: 预测稀疏空间锚点作为增量物理变化（Δ-位置），在训练目标中定义显式容差走廊（corridor）约束；采用流匹配动作头，对超出走廊的轨迹施加校正梯度，同时允许接触与执行噪声的微小偏差。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准上，SmolVLA和GR00T的改进版本分别获得3.4%-12.4%的成功率提升；GR00T-Corr变体达到83.21%的成功率。

Conclusion: 与动作对齐的物理线索能为生成式动作策略提供直接且可解释的约束，补充视觉或潜在形式编码的空间引导。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models often use intermediate representations to connect multimodal inputs with continuous control, yet spatial guidance is often injected implicitly through latent features. We propose $CorridorVLA$, which predicts sparse spatial anchors as incremental physical changes (e.g., $Δ$-positions) and uses them to impose an explicit tolerance region in the training objective for action generation. The anchors define a corridor that guides a flow-matching action head: trajectories whose implied spatial evolution falls outside it receive corrective gradients, while minor deviations from contacts and execution noise are permitted. On the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark, CorridorVLA yields consistent gains across both SmolVLA and GR00T, improving success rate by $3.4\%$--$12.4\%$ over the corresponding baselines; notably, our GR00T-Corr variant reaches a success rate of $83.21\%$. These results indicate that action-aligned physical cues can provide direct and interpretable constraints for generative action policies, complementing spatial guidance encoded in visual or latent forms. Code is available at https://github.com/corridorVLA.

</details>


### [53] [Reasoning About Traversability: Language-Guided Off-Road 3D Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21249)
*Byounggun Park,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言精炼框架，将标注重构为动作对齐对，结合偏好优化策略和地形感知规划，在ORAD-3D基准上将轨迹误差从1.01m降至0.97m，可穿越性合规性从0.621提升至0.644，高程不一致性从0.428降至0.322。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野数据集语言标注与车辆动作和地形几何弱对齐，导致VLM在非结构化环境中的语义推理与规划能力受限。

Method: 1) 语言精炼框架：将标注重构为动作对齐对；2) 偏好优化策略：构建几何感知难负样本，显式惩罚与局部高程轮廓不一致的轨迹；3) 提出越野专用指标量化可穿越性合规性和高程一致性。

Result: ORAD-3D基准上：轨迹误差1.01m→0.97m，可穿越性合规性0.621→0.644，高程不一致性0.428→0.322。

Conclusion: 动作对齐监督和地形感知优化能有效提升越野驾驶的鲁棒性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable high-level semantic reasoning for end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly in unstructured environments, existing off-road datasets suffer from language annotations that are weakly aligned with vehicle actions and terrain geometry. To address this misalignment, we propose a language refinement framework that restructures annotations into action-aligned pairs, enabling a VLM to generate refined scene descriptions and 3D future trajectories directly from a single image. To further encourage terrain-aware planning, we introduce a preference optimization strategy that constructs geometry-aware hard negatives and explicitly penalizes trajectories inconsistent with local elevation profiles. Furthermore, we propose off-road-specific metrics to quantify traversability compliance and elevation consistency, addressing the limitations of conventional on-road evaluation. Experiments on the ORAD-3D benchmark demonstrate that our approach reduces average trajectory error from 1.01m to 0.97m, improves traversability compliance from 0.621 to 0.644, and decreases elevation inconsistency from 0.428 to 0.322, highlighting the efficacy of action-aligned supervision and terrain-aware optimization for robust off-road driving.

</details>


### [54] [FingerViP: Learning Real-World Dexterous Manipulation with Fingertip Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21331)
*Zhen Zhang,Weinan Wang,Hejia Sun,Qingpeng Ding,Xiangyu Chu,Guoxin Fang,K. W. Samuel Au*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FingerViP，一种利用指尖视觉感知的灵巧操作学习系统。通过在每根手指嵌入微型摄像头，结合扩散策略模型，实现了多视角融合的灵巧操作，在按钮按压、物体取回、开柜等复杂任务中达到80.8%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前灵巧操作通常依赖单一腕部视角，容易受到遮挡，限制了需要多视角感知的任务性能。为突破这一瓶颈，作者提出通过在指尖嵌入微型摄像头提供全方位的多视角反馈。

Method: 设计视觉增强指尖模块（嵌入微型摄像头），安装在多指手的每根手指上；开发基于扩散策略的全身体感视觉运动策略，以第三视角相机和多视角指尖视觉为条件；每个指尖视觉特征附加相机位姿编码和手指关节电流编码以改善视角-本体感知对齐与接触感知。

Result: 在按压密闭箱内按钮、从不稳定支架取回细棒、从遮挡窗帘后取物体、长时间柜门打开与物体取回等多项真实世界任务中达到80.8%整体成功率。

Conclusion: 多视角指尖视觉能够有效提升灵巧操作中感知的完整性和鲁棒性，硬件与代码将全部开源。

Abstract: The current practice of dexterous manipulation generally relies on a single wrist-mounted view, which is often occluded and limits performance on tasks requiring multi-view perception. In this work, we present FingerViP, a learning system that utilizes a visuomotor policy with fingertip visual perception for dexterous manipulation. Specifically, we design a vision-enhanced fingertip module with an embedded miniature camera and install the modules on each finger of a multi-fingered hand. The fingertip cameras substantially improve visual perception by providing comprehensive, multi-view feedback of both the hand and its surrounding environment. Building on the integrated fingertip modules, we develop a diffusion-based whole-body visuomotor policy conditioned on a third-view camera and multi-view fingertip vision, which effectively learns complex manipulation skills directly from human demonstrations. To improve view-proprioception alignment and contact awareness, each fingertip visual feature is augmented with its corresponding camera pose encoding and per-finger joint-current encoding. We validate the effectiveness of the multi-view fingertip vision and demonstrate the robustness and adaptability of FingerViP on various challenging real-world tasks, including pressing buttons inside a confined box, retrieving sticks from an unstable support, retrieving objects behind an occluding curtain, and performing long-horizon cabinet opening and object retrieval, achieving an overall success rate of 80.8%. All hardware designs and code will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [55] [Learn Weightlessness: Imitate Non-Self-Stabilizing Motions on Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21351)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Wenqiang Que,Dong Wang,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受人类在非自稳定运动中利用“失重”状态放松关节以与环境交互的生物学机制启发，本文提出失重机制（WM）和自动标注策略，使机器人能在执行目标运动时动态决定放松哪些关节及程度，从而在无任务特定调优的情况下，实现对不同环境配置（不同高度椅子、不同倾角床、依靠墙壁）的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类人全身控制方法通常强制执行刚性轨迹跟踪，忽略了与环境的物理交互，导致对依赖环境的运动（如坐椅子、躺床、靠墙）适应性不足。

Method: 设计了一种失重状态自动标注策略用于数据集标注，并提出了失重机制（WM），该机制动态决定放松哪些关节及放松程度，使得机器人在执行目标运动的同时实现有效的环境交互。

Result: 在3种代表性非自稳定任务（不同高度椅子、不同倾角床、肩肘靠墙）上，在仿真和Unitree G1机器人上验证了WM方法，仅需单动作演示训练且无需任务特定调优，即可在多样化环境配置下实现强泛化并保持运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文工作弥合了精确轨迹跟踪与自适应环境交互之间的差距，为接触丰富的人形机器人控制提供了一种生物启发的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of imitation and reinforcement learning has enabled remarkable advances in humanoid whole-body control, facilitating diverse human-like behaviors. However, research on environment-dependent motions remains limited. Existing methods typically enforce rigid trajectory tracking while neglecting physical interactions with the environment. We observe that humans naturally exploit a "weightless" state during non-self-stabilizing (NSS) motions--selectively relaxing specific joints to allow passive body--environment contact, thereby stabilizing the body and completing the motion. Inspired by this biological mechanism, we design a weightlessness-state auto-labeling strategy for dataset annotation; and we propose the Weightlessness Mechanism (WM), a method that dynamically determines which joints to relax and to what level, together enabling effective environmental interaction while executing target motions. We evaluate our approach on 3 representative NSS tasks: sitting on chairs of varying heights, lying down on beds with different inclinations, and leaning against walls via shoulder or elbow. Extensive experiments in simulation and on the Unitree G1 robot demonstrate that our WM method, trained on single-action demonstrations without any task-specific tuning, achieves strong generalization across diverse environmental configurations while maintaining motion stability. Our work bridges the gap between precise trajectory tracking and adaptive environmental interaction, offering a biologically-inspired solution for contact-rich humanoid control.

</details>


### [56] [RPG: Robust Policy Gating for Smooth Multi-Skill Transitions in Humanoid Fighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21355)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Yubo Dong,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li,Junbo Tan,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RPG混合专家策略框架，通过运动过渡随机化和时间随机化训练统一策略，实现了人形机器人在长时间动态格斗中多技能之间的平滑稳定过渡，并在仿真和Unitree G1实物机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人格斗方法依赖多单技能策略切换或通用策略模仿参考运动，技能间初末状态不匹配导致域外扰动，产生不平稳或不稳定的行为，亟需一种能够实现多技能平滑稳定过渡的框架。

Method: 提出RPG混合专家策略框架，包含运动过渡随机化和时间随机化训练方法，生成统一策略；设计集成行走/跑步 locomotion 与格斗技能的控制流水线，支持任意时长的人形战斗，可随时无缝中断或切换动作策略。

Result: 仿真实验证明该框架的有效性，在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实物部署进一步验证了其鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: RPG框架能够有效解决人形机器人格斗中多技能切换的不稳定性问题，实现平滑、稳定的长时间动态战斗，并具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have demonstrated impressive motor skills in a wide range of tasks, yet whole-body control for humanlike long-time, dynamic fighting remains particularly challenging due to the stringent requirements on agility and stability. While imitation learning enables robots to execute human-like fighting skills, existing approaches often rely on switching among multiple single-skill policies or employing a general policy to imitate input reference motions. These strategies suffer from instability when transitioning between skills, as the mismatch of initial and terminal states across skills or reference motions introduces out-of-domain disturbances, resulting in unsmooth or unstable behaviors. In this work, we propose RPG, a hybrid expert policy framework, for smooth and stable humanoid multi-skills transition. Our approach incorporates motion transition randomization and temporal randomization to train a unified policy that generates agile fighting actions with stability and smoothness during skill transitions. Furthermore, we design a control pipeline that integrates walking/running locomotion with fighting skills, allowing humanlike long-time combat of arbitrary duration that can be seamlessly interrupted or transit action policies at any time. Extensive experiments in simulation demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, and real-world deployment on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot further validates its robustness and applicability.

</details>


### [57] [A Deployable Embodied Vision-Language Navigation System with Hierarchical Cognition and Context-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21363)
*Kuan Xu,Ruimeng Liu,Yizhuo Yang,Denan Liang,Tongxing Jin,Shenghai Yuan,Chen Wang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可部署的具身视觉-语言导航系统，通过将系统解耦为异步感知、记忆集成和推理三个模块，并在认知记忆图上进行子图分解与加权旅行维修工问题优化，实现了在资源受限硬件上的高效实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法在强推理能力与高效部署之间存在根本性权衡，难以在真实机器人平台上同时满足计算、内存、能耗和实时执行约束。

Method: 1) 将系统解耦为三个异步模块：实时感知模块、记忆集成模块和推理模块；2) 增量构建认知记忆图编码场景信息，并将其分解为子图以适配VLM推理；3) 将探索问题形式化为上下文感知的加权旅行维修工问题(WTRP)，最小化视点的加权等待时间。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，该方法在导航成功率和效率上优于现有VLN方法，同时在资源受限硬件上保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 通过系统异步解耦与认知记忆图驱动的优化，成功弥合了具身智能与嵌入式部署之间的差距，实现了高效且鲁棒的VLN系统。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between embodied intelligence and embedded deployment remains a key challenge in intelligent robotic systems, where perception, reasoning, and planning must operate under strict constraints on computation, memory, energy, and real-time execution. In vision-language navigation (VLN), existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between strong reasoning capabilities and efficient deployment on real-world platforms. In this paper, we present a deployable embodied VLN system that achieves both high efficiency and robust high-level reasoning on real-world robotic platforms. To achieve this, we decouple the system into three asynchronous modules: a real-time perception module for continuous environment sensing, a memory integration module for spatial-semantic aggregation, and a reasoning module for high-level decision making. We incrementally construct a cognitive memory graph to encode scene information, which is further decomposed into subgraphs to enable reasoning with a vision-language model (VLM). To further improve navigation efficiency and accuracy, we also leverage the cognitive memory graph to formulate the exploration problem as a context-aware Weighted Traveling Repairman Problem (WTRP), which minimizes the weighted waiting time of viewpoints. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate improved navigation success and efficiency over existing VLN approaches, while maintaining real-time performance on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [58] [From Noise to Intent: Anchoring Generative VLA Policies with Residual Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21391)
*Yiming Zhong,Yaoyu He,Zemin Yang,Pengfei Tian,Yifan Huang,Qingqiu Huang,Xinge Zhu,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResVLA提出了一种"从意图精炼"的新范式，通过频谱分析将机器人控制解耦为确定性低频锚点和随机高频残差，利用残差扩散桥聚焦于局部动态精炼，解决了具身智能中认知与行动之间的时空尺度失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式VLA策略采用"从噪声生成"范式，忽视了认知与行动之间的根本性时空尺度失配，导致表示效率低下和优化过程中的条件对齐薄弱。

Method: ResVLA采用频谱分析将机器人运动控制解耦为全局意图（确定性低频锚点）和局部动态（随机高频残差），通过将生成过程锚定在预测的意图上，利用残差扩散桥仅聚焦于精炼局部动态。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明ResVLA在性能、对语言和机器人本体扰动的鲁棒性以及收敛速度方面均优于标准生成基线；在真实机器人实验中同样表现出色。

Conclusion: "从意图精炼"的新范式有效弥合了高层语义理解与低层物理控制之间的鸿沟，为解决具身智能中的时空尺度失配问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Bridging high-level semantic understanding with low-level physical control remains a persistent challenge in embodied intelligence, stemming from the fundamental spatiotemporal scale mismatch between cognition and action. Existing generative VLA policies typically adopt a "Generation-from-Noise" paradigm, which disregards this disparity, leading to representation inefficiency and weak condition alignment during optimization. In this work, we propose ResVLA, an architecture that shifts the paradigm to "Refinement-from-Intent." Recognizing that robotic motion naturally decomposes into global intent and local dynamics, ResVLA utilizes spectral analysis to decouple control into a deterministic low-frequency anchor and a stochastic high-frequency residual. By anchoring the generative process on the predicted intent, our model focuses strictly on refining local dynamics via a residual diffusion bridge. Extensive simulation experiments show that ResVLA achieves competitive performance, strong robustness to language and robot embodiment perturbations, and faster convergence than standard generative baselines. It also demonstrates strong performance in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [59] [Ufil: A Unified Framework for Infrastructure-based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21471)
*Simon Schäfer,Lucas Hegerath,Marius Molz,Massimo Marcon,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ufil，一个面向基础设施定位的统一框架，提供标准化对象模型和可复用的多目标跟踪组件，支持异构数据源融合（ETSI ITS-G5 CAM、激光雷达、路面传感器），在CARLA仿真和CPM Lab实验平台上实现了车道级横向定位精度（RMSE约0.3米）和100毫秒以下的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施定位系统存在碎片化问题，感知、跟踪和中间件紧耦合，难以复用和扩展，阻碍了研究与开发。

Method: 提出Ufil统一框架，包含标准化对象模型和可复用的多目标跟踪组件，提供预测、检测、关联、状态更新和轨迹管理的接口与参考实现；支持异构数据源（车载ITS-G5 CAM、激光雷达、路面传感器）融合，基于C++/ROS 2实现开源。

Result: 在三车道高速公路场景中（仿真423辆车、实验平台355辆车），融合系统达到横向位置RMSE 0.31 m（CARLA）和0.29 m（CPM Lab），平均绝对朝向误差约2.2°，中位端到端延迟低于100 ms。

Conclusion: Ufil是一个可扩展、异构融合的基础设施定位统一框架，能在不同规模平台上运行且保持高精度和低延迟，可用于推动基础设施定位研究。

Abstract: Infrastructure-based localization enhances road safety and traffic management by providing state estimates of road users. Development is hindered by fragmented, application-specific stacks that tightly couple perception, tracking, and middleware. We introduce Ufil, a Unified Framework for Infrastructure-Based Localization with a standardized object model and reusable multi-object tracking components. Ufil offers interfaces and reference implementations for prediction, detection, association, state update, and track management, allowing researchers to improve components without reimplementing the pipeline. Ufil is open-source C++/ROS 2 software with documentation and executable examples. We demonstrate Ufil by integrating three heterogeneous data sources into a single localization pipeline combining (i) vehicle onboard units broadcasting ETSI ITS-G5 Cooperative Awareness Messages, (ii) a lidar-based roadside sensor node, and (iii) an in-road sensitive surface layer. The pipeline runs unchanged in the CARLA simulator and a small-scale CAV testbed, demonstrating Ufil's scale-independent execution model. In a three-lane highway scenario with 423 and 355 vehicles in simulation and testbed, respectively, the fused system achieves lane-level lateral accuracy with mean lateral position RMSEs of 0.31 m in CARLA and 0.29 m in the CPM Lab, and mean absolute orientation errors around 2.2°. Median end-to-end latencies from sensing to fused output remain below 100 ms across all modalities in both environments.

</details>


### [60] [MISTY: High-Throughput Motion Planning via Mixer-based Single-step Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21489)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Yiqian Tu,Zhiyuan Liu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISTY提出了一种基于MLP-Mixer和单步推理的高吞吐量生成式运动规划器，通过隐空间漂移损失实现领先的闭环规划性能，在nuPlan基准测试中达到80.32/82.21的综合分数，推理速度超过99 FPS，是迭代扩散规划器的10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的运动规划器由于需要迭代的神经函数评估，存在高推理延迟的问题，难以满足自动驾驶对实时性的要求。

Method: 1) 向量化子图编码器捕获环境上下文；2) 变分自编码器将专家轨迹压缩到32维隐空间；3) 超轻量MLP-Mixer解码器消除二次注意力复杂度；4) 引入隐空间漂移损失，将复杂分布演化转移到训练阶段，通过显式的吸引力和排斥力实现主动超车等新颖机动。

Result: 在nuPlan的Test14-hard分割上，非反应式设置得分80.32，反应式设置得分82.21；端到端延迟10.1 ms，运行速度超过99 FPS。

Conclusion: MISTY通过单步推理实现了比迭代扩散规划器高一个数量级的加速，同时保持了鲁棒的生成质量和最先进的闭环性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal trajectory generation is essential for safe autonomous driving, yet existing diffusion-based planners suffer from high inference latency due to iterative neural function evaluations. This paper presents MISTY (Mixer-based Inference for Single-step Trajectory-drifting Yield), a high-throughput generative motion planner that achieves state-of-the-art closed-loop performance with pure single-step inference. MISTY integrates a vectorized Sub-Graph encoder to capture environment context, a Variational Autoencoder to structure expert trajectories into a compact 32-dimensional latent manifold, and an ultra-lightweight MLP-Mixer decoder to eliminate quadratic attention complexity. Importantly, we introduce a latent-space drifting loss that shifts the complex distribution evolution entirely to the training phase. By formulating explicit attractive and repulsive forces, this mechanism empowers the model to synthesize novel, proactive maneuvers, such as active overtaking, that are virtually absent from the raw expert demonstrations. Extensive evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that MISTY achieves state-of-the-art results on the challenging Test14-hard split, with comprehensive scores of 80.32 and 82.21 in non-reactive and reactive settings, respectively. Operating at over 99 FPS with an end-to-end latency of 10.1 ms, MISTY offers an order-of-magnitude speedup over iterative diffusion planners while while achieving significantly robust generation.

</details>


### [61] [X2-N: A Transformable Wheel-legged Humanoid Robot with Dual-mode Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21541)
*Yan Ning,Xingzhou Chen,Delong Li,Hao Zhang,Hanfu Gai,Tongyuan Li,Cheng Zhang,Zhihui Peng,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出X2-N，一种高自由度可变形机器人，具备人形和轮腿两种模式及无缝切换能力，并设计了基于强化学习的全身控制框架，在动态滑行、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中验证了其高效运动、强地形适应性和稳定的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人通常采用固定轮子作为足部且髋部自由度有限，导致腿部运动时的稳定性和机动性不如带平足的人形机器人；同时多数平台缺乏完整上肢及手臂，限制了灵巧操作能力。

Method: 设计高自由度可变形机器人X2-N，支持人形与轮腿双模式及关节重构切换；提出基于强化学习的全身控制框架，统一控制混合运动、变形和操作。

Result: 在动态滑行运动、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中验证了X2-N的高运动效率、强地形适应性和稳定的运动-操作性能。

Conclusion: X2-N展示了轮腿机器人在真实世界部署的潜力，兼具高效运动、地形适应与灵巧操作能力。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots combine the efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the versatility of legged systems, enabling rapid traversal over both continuous and discrete terrains. However, conventional designs typically employ fixed wheels as feet and limited degrees of freedom (DoFs) at the hips, resulting in reduced stability and mobility during legged locomotion compared to humanoids with flat feet. In addition, most existing platforms lack a full upper body with arms, which limits their ability to perform dexterous manipulation tasks.
  In this letter, we present X2-N, a high-DoF transformable robot with dual-mode locomotion and manipulation. X2-N can operate in both humanoid and wheel-legged forms and transform seamlessly between them through joint reconfiguration. We further propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body control framework tailored to this morphology, enabling unified control across hybrid locomotion, transformation, and manipulation. We validate X2-N in a range of challenging locomotion and manipulation tasks, including dynamic skating-like motion, stair climbing and package delivery. Results demonstrate high locomotion efficiency, strong terrain adaptability, and stable loco-manipulation performance of X2-N, highlighting its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [62] [SLAM as a Stochastic Control Problem with Partial Information: Optimal Solutions and Rigorous Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21693)
*Ilir Gusija,Fady Alajaji,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过最优随机控制视角重新审视主动SLAM问题，将其表述为部分信息下的决策问题，提出了一种新的探索阶段代价函数，并基于非标准POMDP推导出具有严格理论保证的近优近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动SLAM是机器人领域的基础问题，现有方法缺乏从最优随机控制角度的严格理论分析，需要建立更严谨的数学框架来指导信息采集决策。

Method: 将主动SLAM重构为非标准部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），引入编码状态几何信息的探索阶段代价函数，在一般性假设下分析正则性条件，并利用标准学习算法学习近优策略。

Result: 得到了具有严格理论证明的近似最优解，数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机控制框架能够为主动SLAM问题提供理论上有保证的近似最优策略，适用于广泛的机器人应用场景。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is a foundational state estimation problem in robotics in which a robot accurately constructs a map of its environment while also localizing itself within this construction. We study the active SLAM problem through the lens of optimal stochastic control, thereby recasting it as a decision-making problem under partial information. After reviewing several commonly studied models, we present a general stochastic control formulation of active SLAM together with a rigorous treatment of motion, sensing, and map representation. We introduce a new exploration stage cost that encodes the geometry of the state when evaluating information-gathering actions. This formulation, constructed as a nonstandard partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), is then analyzed to derive rigorously justified approximate solutions that are near-optimal. To enable this analysis, the associated regularity conditions are studied under general assumptions that apply to a wide range of robotics applications. For a particular case, we conduct an extensive numerical study in which standard learning algorithms are used to learn near-optimal policies.

</details>


### [63] [A Compact Peristaltic Pump Based on Magneto-Elastic Hysteresis with Single Pneumatic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21729)
*Minjo Park,Metin Sitti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过单一气动输入结合嵌入式被动磁铁实现蠕动运动的软膜泵，简化了传统泵送机构的复杂性，并通过建模、数值模拟和实验验证了磁弹滞回行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蠕动泵依赖机械、气动或磁驱动方式驱动膜变形，导致泵结构和控制方案复杂，亟需一种更简化的驱动方案。

Method: 采用单一气动输入与嵌入式被动磁铁相结合的软膜泵设计，通过建模分析驱动机制和系统动力学，利用数值模拟预测内部流体流动，并通过原理样机实验验证模拟结果。

Result: 数值模拟成功预测了磁弹滞回行为，并通过实验验证了该行为的有效性。

Conclusion: 该软膜泵以更简化的结构和控制实现了蠕动运动，具有在工业、环境和生物医学领域应用的潜力。

Abstract: Pumping fluids is fundamental to a wide range of industrial, environmental, and biomedical applications. Among various pumping mechanisms, peristaltic pumps enable efficient and safe fluid transport by deforming an elastic tube without direct contact with the working fluid. Although previous studies have introduced mechanical, pneumatic, or magnetic actuations to drive membrane deformation, these approaches often lead to complex pump architectures and control schemes. In this study, we present a soft membrane pump that achieves peristaltic motion through a single pneumatic input combined with an embedded passive magnet. The actuation mechanism and system dynamics were analyzed and simplified through modeling. Numerical simulations were conducted to predict the internal fluid flow, and the magneto-elastic hysteresis behavior observed in the simulations was successfully validated by experiments with a proof-of-concept prototype.

</details>


### [64] [Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21741)
*Yaxuan Li,Zhongyi Zhou,Yefei Chen,Yanjiang Guo,Jiaming Liu,Shanghang Zhang,Jianyu Chen,Yichen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-WM提出了一种基于世界模型的后训练框架，让人类在世界模型内部对策略的失败轨迹进行干预修正，生成密集校正数据并回注训练集，显著提升了真实世界任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人后训练依赖于真实物理环境的人机交互，每次校正都需要机器人执行、场景搭建、重置和操作员监督，效率低下。本文希望利用世界模型作为可复用的校正基质，摆脱对物理执行的依赖。

Method: 首先让策略在世界模型内闭环 rollout；当 rollout 出现失败或错误时，人类直接在世界模型中对中间状态进行短时校正干预；Hi-WM 缓存中间状态并支持回滚和分支，使单个失败状态可用于多条校正轨迹，生成密集监督信号；校正后的轨迹加入训练集进行后训练。

Result: 在三个真实世界操作任务（涵盖刚体和可变形物体）及两种策略主干上，Hi-WM 相比基础策略平均提升 37.9 个百分点的真实世界成功率，相比世界模型闭环基线提升 19.0 个百分点；世界模型评估与真实世界性能高度相关（r=0.953）。

Conclusion: 世界模型不仅可以作为生成器或评估器，还可以作为有效的校正基质用于可扩展的机器人后训练。

Abstract: Post-training is essential for turning pretrained generalist robot policies into reliable task-specific controllers, but existing human-in-the-loop pipelines remain tied to physical execution: each correction requires robot time, scene setup, resets, and operator supervision in the real world. Meanwhile, action-conditioned world models have been studied mainly for imagination, synthetic data generation, and policy evaluation. We propose \textbf{Human-in-the-World-Model (Hi-WM)}, a post-training framework that uses a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for failure-targeted policy improvement. A policy is first rolled out in closed loop inside the world model; when the rollout becomes incorrect or failure-prone, a human intervenes directly in the model to provide short corrective actions. Hi-WM caches intermediate states and supports rollback and branching, allowing a single failure state to be reused for multiple corrective continuations and yielding dense supervision around behaviors that the base policy handles poorly. The resulting corrective trajectories are then added back to the training set for post-training. We evaluate Hi-WM on three real-world manipulation tasks spanning both rigid and deformable object interaction, and on two policy backbones. Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points on average over the base policy and by 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, while world-model evaluation correlates strongly with real-world performance (r = 0.953). These results suggest that world models can serve not only as generators or evaluators, but also as effective corrective substrates for scalable robot post-training.

</details>


### [65] [VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21914)
*Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yating Feng,Xiang Li,Yilun Chen,Pengfei Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaBot通过融合前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型，实现了无需测试时相机标定的视角鲁棒闭环操作，在ACT和π₀策略上分别将视角泛化分数提升2.79倍和2.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端机器人操作模型在固定相机训练下对视角变化缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出VistaBot框架，包含三个关键组件：4D几何估计、视角合成潜在特征提取、潜在动作学习；集成到ACT和π₀策略中。

Result: VistaBot在VGS指标上相较于ACT和π₀分别提升2.79倍和2.63倍，同时实现高质量的新视角合成。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：几何感知合成模型、潜在动作规划器、新基准指标VGS，以及在多种环境下的广泛验证。

Abstract: Recently, end-to-end robotic manipulation models have gained significant attention for their generalizability and scalability. However, they often suffer from limited robustness to camera viewpoint changes when training with a fixed camera. In this paper, we propose VistaBot, a novel framework that integrates feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to achieve view-robust closed-loop manipulation without requiring camera calibration at test time. Our approach consists of three key components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning. VistaBot is integrated into both action-chunking (ACT) and diffusion-based ($π_0$) policies and evaluated across simulation and real-world tasks. We further introduce the View Generalization Score (VGS) as a new metric for comprehensive evaluation of cross-view generalization. Results show that VistaBot improves VGS by 2.79$\times$ and 2.63$\times$ over ACT and $π_0$, respectively, while also achieving high-quality novel view synthesis. Our contributions include a geometry-aware synthesis model, a latent action planner, a new benchmark metric, and extensive validation across diverse environments. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>

### [66] [SQLyzr: A Comprehensive Benchmark and Evaluation Platform for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21214)
*Sepideh Abedini,M. Tamer Özsu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLyzr是一个面向Text-to-SQL模型的综合评估平台，通过多样化指标、真实工作负载对齐、细粒度查询分类和错误分析，弥补了现有基准测试仅依赖单一聚合分数、缺乏真实场景评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL基准测试通常只提供单一聚合分数，缺乏在真实场景下的评估，且对不同查询类型的模型行为洞察有限，难以有效诊断和提升模型性能。

Method: SQLyzr集成了多样化评估指标（多维度捕获生成查询质量），通过工作负载对齐（匹配真实SQL使用模式）和数据库缩放实现更真实的评估，并支持细粒度查询分类、错误分析及工作负载增强。

Result: 通过交互式图形界面，用户可自定义评估设置、分析细粒度报告并探索平台附加功能，实现了对Text-to-SQL模型更全面、真实的评估与诊断。

Conclusion: SQLyzr通过解决现有基准测试的关键局限，促进了Text-to-SQL模型的评估与迭代改进，其源代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models have significantly improved with the adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs), leading to their increasing use in real-world applications. Although many benchmarks exist for evaluating the performance of text-to-SQL models, they often rely on a single aggregate score, lack evaluation under realistic settings, and provide limited insight into model behaviour across different query types. In this work, we present SQLyzr, a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation platform for text-to-SQL models. SQLyzr incorporates a diverse set of evaluation metrics that capture multiple aspects of generated queries, while enabling more realistic evaluation through workload alignment with real-world SQL usage patterns and database scaling. It further supports fine-grained query classification, error analysis, and workload augmentation, allowing users to better diagnose and improve text-to-SQL models. This demonstration showcases these capabilities through an interactive experience. Through SQLyzr's graphical interface, users can customize evaluation settings, analyze fine-grained reports, and explore additional features of the platform. We envision that SQLyzr facilitates the evaluation and iterative improvement of text-to-SQL models by addressing key limitations of existing benchmarks. The source code of SQLyzr is available at https://github.com/sepideh-abedini/SQLyzr.

</details>

### [67] [User-Centered Design of Hyperlocal Communication Platforms: Insights from the Design and Evaluation of KUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20973)
*Eljohn Evangelista,Alyssa Cea,Axel Balitaan,Clark Vince Diala,Jamlech Iram Gojo Cruz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对菲律宾超本地化通信中的延迟、算法噪音、语言障碍和数字鸿沟问题，设计了集成官方公告与社区报告的双通道原型系统KUBO，实验表明其在任务完成时间、信息回忆和用户满意度上显著优于Facebook。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菲律宾现有的超本地化通信存在延迟预警、算法过滤导致的噪音、语言差异以及数字鸿沟等问题，居民难以及时获取紧急通知和社区活动信息，亟需一个高效、包容的信息传播解决方案。

Method: 采用用户中心研究方法，包括情境调查和半结构化访谈，识别四大关键障碍。基于洞察设计了KUBO原型，包含家庭模块（官方公告和精选头条）和社区模块（居民驱动的邻里报告和讨论）。采用被试内设计进行用户评估，与Facebook对比。

Result: KUBO显著降低了任务完成时间（p<0.001），提高了任务后测验的信息回忆准确率（p=0.010），并在易用性、整体满意度和感知有效性方面获得比Facebook更高的用户满意度评分。

Conclusion: 双通道、包容性平台能够显著增强超本地化场景下的实时信息访问、理解能力和公民参与度。

Abstract: Effective hyperlocal communication is critical in the Philippines, where delayed or algorithm-filtered updates can leave residents uninformed about emergency advisories and community events. We conducted a user-centered study consisting of contextual inquiry and semi-structured interviews to identify four key barriers: delayed alerts, algorithm-driven noise, language gaps, and digital divides. Guided by these insights, we designed KUBO (Kumunidad at Balitang Opisyal), a prototype that integrates a home module for verified local government unit advisories and curated headlines, and a community module for resident-powered neighborhood reports and discussions. Using a within-subjects evaluation design, KUBO significantly reduced task completion times (p-value < 0.001), improved information recall on post-task quizzes (p-value = 0.010), and yielded higher user satisfaction ratings for ease of use, overall satisfaction, and perceived effectiveness compared to Facebook, the commonly used communication platform in the Philippines. These results demonstrate that a dual-channel, inclusive platform can substantially enhance real-time information access, comprehension, and civic engagement in hyperlocal settings.

</details>


### [68] [White Paper: Human-AI Collaboration in Conflict Analysis: Text Classifier Development with Peacebuilders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21034)
*Allan Kipyator Kipkemboi Cheboi,Julie Hawke,Hussam Abualfatah,Andrew Sutjahjo,Daniel Burkhardt Cerigo,Rachael Olpengs,William OBrien*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文记录了在肯尼亚和苏丹开展的和平建设者与数据科学家的合作研究，通过参与式标注流程开发基于BERT的AI文本分类器，用于监测在线极化和仇恨言论。结果表明，该方法提升了上下文对齐、减少了因文化细微差异导致的误分类，并增强了实践者对AI工具的所有权。研究证实参与式AI开发能同时提升技术鲁棒性、上下文有效性和规范性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI仇恨言论检测模型在跨文化语境中常因缺乏本地化知识而导致误分类，且实践者对黑箱式AI工具缺乏信任和所有权。作者希望通过参与式方法将领域专家和实践者的知识融入模型开发，以提升模型在敏感人道主义领域的适用性和可接受性。

Method: 采用参与式标注流程，让和平建设实践者和领域专家参与问题定义、标注设计、迭代验证和模型评估全过程。基于协作标注的数据集微调BERT分类器，并在保留测试集上评估性能。

Result: 模型在上下文对齐方面显著增强，由文化细微差异驱动的误分类减少，实践者对AI工具的所有权感提升。开发的两个模型（肯尼亚-极化分类器、苏丹-仇恨言论分类器）已开源并在HuggingFace上发布。

Conclusion: 参与式AI开发能够在敏感人道主义领域同时提升技术鲁棒性、上下文有效性和规范性对齐，为AI在和平建设等高风险场景中的负责任应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: This paper documents a collaborative research process involving peacebuilders and data scientists in Kenya and Sudan to develop AI-based text classifiers for monitoring online polarization and hatespeech. The method describes a participatory annotation process in which practitioners and domain experts contributed to problem definition, annotation design, iterative validation, and model evaluation. Fine-tuned BERT-based classifiers were trained on collaboratively annotated datasets and evaluated against held-out test sets. In each case, the models produced enhanced contextual alignment, reduced misclassification driven by cultural nuance, and increased practitioner ownership of AI tools. The resulting models (Kenya-polarization and Sudan-hate speech) are open-source and accessible via HuggingFace. The study contributes empirical evidence that participatory AI development can simultaneously improve technical robustness, contextual validity, and normative alignment in sensitive humanitarian domains.

</details>


### [69] [When Constraints Limit and Inspire: Characterizing Presentation Authoring Practices for Evolving Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21205)
*Linxiu Zeng,Emily Kuang,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于约束的多会话演示文稿创作框架（CMPA），通过形成性研究、原型工具ReSlide及两项用户研究，揭示了时间、受众和沟通意图等约束如何从限制转变为主动设计驱动因素，并支持内容在多次创作会话中的灵活复用与调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将演示文稿创作中的约束视为限制，但对演示者如何主动推理和利用这些约束的机制尚不清楚。作者希望通过理解约束如何涌现、被解读并影响创作决策，进而设计更好的约束感知工具。

Method: 开展了三项研究：(1) 对10位演示者进行形成性研究，观察约束的产生与解释过程；(2) 基于CMPA框架开发了原型工具ReSlide，支持约束感知的幻灯片创建与复用；(3) 分别针对单会话行为和多人多会话工作流进行了两项用户研究，与基线工具对比评估效果。

Result: 与基线工具相比，ReSlide帮助演示者将约束视为主动设计驱动因素，而非限制；在跨会话场景下，演示者能够随着约束的演变灵活地复用和调整内容。

Conclusion: 约束在演示文稿创作中具有双重角色——既是限制因素也是设计驱动因素；未来约束感知的创作工具应支持跨会话的内容演化与灵活复用。

Abstract: Authoring presentation slides involves navigating contextual constraints that shape how content is structured, adapted, and reused. While prior work frames constraints as limitations, little is known about how presenters actively reason about them. We conducted a formative study with ten presenters to examine how constraints emerge, are interpreted, and influence authoring decisions, leading to the Constraint-based Multi-session Presentation Authoring (CMPA) framework. CMPA treats time, audience, and communicative intent as key constraints shaping authoring. We instantiated CMPA in ReSlide, a research prototype for constraint-aware slide creation and reuse, and conducted two user studies on (1) single-session behaviors and (2) multi-session workflows. Compared to a baseline tool, ReSlide helped presenters treat constraints as active design drivers that guide narrative construction. The second study further shows how presenters flexibly reuse and adapt content across authoring cycles as constraints evolve. We then propose design implications for future constraint-aware presentation tools.

</details>


### [70] [TopoStyle: Supporting Iterative Design with Generative AI for 2.5D Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21315)
*Shuyue Feng,Cedric Caremel,Yoshihiro Kawahara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TopoStyle 是一个利用2D扩散模型进行2.5D拓扑优化的迭代设计工具，支持手绘和点交互两种方式，结合掩码实现局部优化，在平衡结构性能与美学的同时提高设计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑优化工具在2D人本设计方面结果单一、可定制性弱；生成式AI方法交互性有限；且传统TO很少强调结构性能与美学之间的迭代平衡。

Method: 采用2D扩散模型实现2.5D拓扑优化，提出两种交互方式（手绘界面交互和3D建模软件中点交互），支持掩码指定区域进行局部拓扑优化。

Result: 通过与现有方法的对比，评估了性能和交互方式；验证了TopoStyle通过定制化与迭代设计在平衡性能与美学的同时提升设计效率的能力。

Conclusion: TopoStyle通过结合扩散模型与多种交互方式，有效弥补了传统TO在美学-性能平衡和交互性方面的不足，多个设计案例展示了其应用潜力。

Abstract: Topology optimization(TO) is widely used in engineering because of its ability to save material and optimize structural performance. Although prior work has explored 2D human-centered design tool for TO, the results are often limited in variety and offer weak customizability. Meanwhile, due to the high computational and time costs of TO, researchers have attempted to address these issues using generative AI; however, such methods often provide limited interactivity. In addition, topology optimization in many cases needs to balance structural performance and aesthetic qualities through iterative design, a perspective that has rarely been emphasized in traditional TO. We present TopoStyle, an iterative design tool for 2.5D topology optimization using a 2D diffusion model. We explore two interaction methods. The first exports 3D parts to a graphical interface for hand-drawn interaction. The second enables direct interaction within 3D modeling software using points. Our tool also supports the use of masks to apply topology optimization to specific regions, allowing users to address customized design needs. We compare and evaluate both performance and interaction methods, and investigate how TopoStyle can balance performance and aesthetics while improving design efficiency through customization and iterative design. Finally, we demonstrate the application scenarios of TopoStyle through several design cases.

</details>


### [71] ["If We Had the Information That We Need to Interpret the World Around Us, We Wouldn't Be Disabled:" Barriers and Opportunities in Information Work among Blind and Sighted Colleagues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21338)
*Yichun Zhao,Miguel A. Nacenta,Mahadeo A. Sukhai,Sowmya Somanath*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过日记研究、访谈和焦点小组，探讨了混合视觉能力团队在工作场所中使用信息表征（如PDF、电子表格和图表）时面临的四种失败类型及相应的变通方法，揭示了受工作场所污名和社会动态影响的相互依赖信息工作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人们认识到多样性的价值，但实际工作方式未能充分支持盲人或低视力员工，尤其是在协作工作环境中，因此需要理解混合能力团队在使用信息表征时的实际挑战。

Method: 对5个团队的23名不同视觉能力的参与者进行日记研究和后续个人访谈，并对另外2个团队的7名参与者进行2次焦点小组讨论。

Result: 识别出信息表征在工作场所中的四种相互关联的失败类型及相应变通方法，这些失败受到工作场所污名的影响，并由相互依赖信息工作中的社会动态塑造。

Conclusion: 提出了一个基于实证的、新的概念性理解，有助于在当前技术环境下设计和改善混合视觉能力团队在知识工作中的体验。

Abstract: Despite recognition of the value of diversity, the way work takes place can fail to support blind or low-vision employees, especially in collaborative work settings. This paper examines how professional teams with diverse visual abilities use information representations (e.g., PDF documents, spreadsheets and charts). A diary study with follow-up individual interviews (23 participants with mixed abilities from 5 teams) and 2 separate focus groups (7 participants from 2 other teams) allowed us to characterize key dimensions of the role of representations in the workplace into four types of interrelated failures and workarounds, influenced by workplace stigmas and shaped by evolving social dynamics towards interdependent information work. We contribute this new empirically supported conceptual understanding of representation use in workplaces that can help design and improve the experiences of mixed-ability teams doing knowledge work in the current technological landscape.

</details>


### [72] [Neurodiversity and Technostress: Towards a Multimodal Research Design for Evaluating Subjective, Physiological, and Behavioral Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21404)
*Lisa van den Heuvel,Igor Ivkić,René Riedl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个整合主观、生理和行为多模态测量的受控实验设计，系统比较神经多样性与神经典型人群在标准化数字化压力条件下的技术压力反应，以推动更具包容性的数字化工作设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术压力研究主要聚焦神经典型人群，且很少在同一设计中整合多维度压力测量，缺乏对神经多样性群体的关注。

Method: 提出受控实验研究设计，结合结构化和非结构化数字任务，采用多模态测量方法（主观感知、生理激活、可观察交互行为），系统比较神经多样性与神经典型个体。

Result: 通过整合神经多样性视角，提出更差异化的数字压力理解框架，并提供更具包容性的数字化工作设计方法论。

Conclusion: 将神经多样性纳入技术压力研究有助于更全面地理解数字压力，为包容性数字化工作设计提供方法学基础。

Abstract: Digitalization has transformed modern work by increasing efficiency while also introducing new forms of strain. Technostress (TS) describes subjective, physiological, and behavioral stress responses related to digital technology use. Existing TS research has predominantly focused on neurotypical populations and rarely integrates multiple stress dimensions within a single design. This paper addresses these gaps by proposing a controlled experimental research design that systematically compares neurodivergent and neurotypical individuals under standardized digital stress conditions. The proposed design combines structured and unstructured digital tasks with a multimodal measurement approach covering subjective perceptions, physiological activation, and observable interaction behavior. By integrating neurodiversity into TS research, the paper contributes to a more differentiated understanding of digital stress and provides a methodological approach for more inclusive digital work design.

</details>


### [73] [Gradual Voluntary Participation: A Framework for Participatory AI Governance in Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21878)
*Matilde Barbini,Stefano Sorrentino,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"渐进自愿参与（GVP）"框架，解决AI系统在新闻编辑室中因不透明性导致的参与式设计挑战，通过深度与广度二维矩阵重新定义参与，平衡技术转型与利益相关者赋权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参与式设计（PD）假设用户能够塑造技术，但AI系统的黑箱性（不透明数据、固定架构、不可达目标）使其难以被影响，导致新闻编辑室中记者对AI的信任危机与参与困境。

Method: 对10名记者进行访谈，识别出"感知差距"——对AI的信任取决于在工作场所参与式流程中的感知能动性；在此基础上构建GVP框架的五大原则。

Result: 提出GVP框架：将参与重新概念化为渐进、自愿的过程，超越一次性工作坊或偏好征集；采用深度×广度的二维矩阵结构替代传统单维阶梯隐喻；可操作性体现在新闻编辑室层面的本地化部署。

Conclusion: GVP框架通过解决认知负担、参与上限和表演性咨询等问题，为混合工作场所中的本地参与式AI治理提供了新模型，实现了技术转型与利益相关者赋权之间的平衡。

Abstract: The integration of AI into journalism challenges participatory design (PD), particularly with respect to stakeholder influence, workplace perceptions, and organizational dynamics. Traditional PD assumes that users can shape technologies, yet AI systems resist influence due to opaque data, fixed architectures, and inaccessible objectives. Through interviews with 10 journalists, we identify the perception gap, showing that trust in AI depends on perceived agency within workplace participatory workflows. Informed by these findings, we introduce the Gradual Voluntary Participation (GVP) framework in journalism and its five core principles, reconceptualizing participation as a gradual and voluntary process that can be operationalized at the newsroom level, beyond fixed workshops or one-time preference-elicitation campaigns. Addressing epistemic burdens, participatory ceilings, and performative consultations, GVP treats gradualism and voluntariness as design dimensions that shape perception, legitimacy, and ownership. Moving beyond unidimensional ladder metaphors and adopting a bidimensional matrix structure, the framework maps stakeholders across depth and scope, offering a new model for local participatory AI governance that balances technological transformation with stakeholder empowerment in rapidly evolving hybrid workplaces.

</details>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [Biomedical systems biology workflow orchestration and execution with PoSyMed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20906)
*Simon Süwer,Zoe Chervontseva,Kester Bagemihl,Jan Baumbach,Olga Tsoy,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PoSyMed是一个开源模块化平台，通过容器化执行、形式化工具描述、持久化工作流状态和LLM辅助的人机交互界面，解决生物信息学工具集成、组合和复用的痛点，提升可重复性、可追溯性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学软件快速增长导致工具分发碎片化、文档不一致、依赖复杂、执行环境难以复现，即使有经验的用户也难以重用已发布工具和适配工作流。

Method: 采用后端中心架构，结合形式化工具描述、基于容器的受控构建与执行流程、持久性工作流状态、对话式用户界面；集成大语言模型作为有边界的语义助手（非自主决策者），辅助工具识别、工作流步骤建议和参数化，最终由人工监督执行。

Result: 在代表性生物学软件场景下评估了工作流支持、交互设计和平台可扩展性，验证了系统架构的有效性。

Conclusion: PoSyMed在一个统一平台内提高了实际生物医学分析的可重复性、可追溯性和透明度，并提供了公开可用的实现。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific software has created practical barriers for bioinformatics research. Although powerful statistical, artificial intelligence (AI)-based methods are now widely available, their effective use is often hindered by fragmented distribution, inconsistent documentation, complex dependencies, and difficult-to-reproduce execution environments. As a result, reusing published tools and workflow adaptation to own date remains technically demanding and time-intensive, even for experienced users. Here, we present PoSyMed, an open and modular platform for the controlled integration, composition, and execution of bioinformatics tools and workflows. PoSyMed combines a backend-centered platform architecture with formal tool descriptions, controlled container-based build and execution processes, persistent workflow state, and a dialogue-based user interface. Large language models (LLM) are integrated not as autonomous decision-makers, but as human-computer interface with bounded semantic assistants that help identify tools, propose workflow steps, and support parameterization within a typed, validated, and human-supervised execution environment. PoSyMed is designed to improve reproducibility, traceability, and transparency in practical biomedical analysis within one platform. We describe the system architecture and evaluate its behavior across representative biological software scenarios with respect to workflow support, interaction design, and platform extensibility. PoSyMed is publicly available at https://apps.cosy.bio/posymed.

</details>


### [75] [Residual Risk Analysis in Benign Code: How Far Are We? A Multi-Model Semantic and Structural Similarity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21051)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Risk Scoring (RRS)框架，通过语义和结构相似性分析，发现已修补函数中仍存在约61%的高风险代码对包含13类残留安全问题，验证了代码相似性可作为后补丁审查的实用信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞基准通常将修补后的函数视为良性，忽视了残留安全风险的可能性，补丁是否完全消除风险这一问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 基于PrimeVul数据集中的脆弱-良性函数对，使用多个代码语言模型（Code LMs）捕获语义相似性，结合Tree-sitter的抽象语法树（AST）分析结构相似性，构建统一的Residual Risk Scoring (RRS)框架，集成嵌入级语义相似性、局部AST结构相似性和跨模型一致性来估计残留风险。

Result: 良性函数在语义和结构上与其脆弱版本仍高度相似，约61%的高RRS代码对存在13类残留问题（如空指针解引用、不安全内存分配），经Cppcheck、Clang-Tidy和Facebook-Infer等静态分析工具验证。

Conclusion: 代码级相似性可作为后补丁审查的实用信号，实现更可靠、可扩展的实际开源软件管道安全评估。

Abstract: Software security relies on effective vulnerability detection and patching, yet determining whether a patch fully eliminates risk remains an underexplored challenge. Existing vulnerability benchmarks often treat patched functions as inherently benign, overlooking the possibility of residual security risks. In this work, we analyze vulnerable-benign function pairs from the PrimeVul, a benchmark dataset using multiple code language models (Code LMs) to capture semantic similarity, complemented by Tree-sitter-based abstract syntax tree (AST) analysis for structural similarity. Building on these signals, we propose Residual Risk Scoring (RRS), a unified framework that integrates embedding-based semantic similarity, localized AST-based structural similarity, and cross-model agreement to estimate residual risk in code. Our analysis shows that benign functions often remain highly similar to their vulnerable counterparts both semantically and structurally, indicating potential persistence of residual risk. We further find that approximately $61\%$ of high-RRS code pairs exhibit $13$ distinct categories of residual issues (e.g., null pointer dereferences, unsafe memory allocation), validated using state-of-the-art static analysis tools including Cppcheck, Clang-Tidy, and Facebook-Infer. These results demonstrate that code-level similarity provides a practical signal for prioritizing post-patch inspection, enabling more reliable and scalable security assessment in real-world open-source software pipelines.

</details>


### [76] [A Ground-Truth-Based Evaluation of Vulnerability Detection Across Multiple Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21111)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl,Martin Häusl,Maximilian Auch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于OSV数据库构建了用于多生态系统漏洞检测评估的基准数据集，通过实证研究发现不同检测系统之间存在系统性差异，并提供了可复现数据集的开源工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化漏洞检测工具被广泛使用，但由于漏洞数据源结构异构、标识符方案不一致以及版本范围规范含糊，导致对这些工具的评估面临挑战。

Method: 从OSV数据库提取并构建了一个将漏洞映射到具体软件包版本的基准数据集，对多个生态系统中的漏洞检测工具进行系统性比较，并开发了开源的自动化数据集重构工具以确保可复现性。

Result: 不同漏洞检测系统之间存在系统性差异，揭示了数据集构建方式对实证结果的重要影响。

Conclusion: 透明的数据集构建对于可重复的实证安全研究至关重要，本研究提供的方法和工具有助于推动漏洞检测评估的标准化。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection tools are widely used to identify security vulnerabilities in software dependencies. However, the evaluation of such tools remains challenging due to the heterogeneous structure of vulnerability data sources, inconsistent identifier schemes, and ambiguities in version range specifications. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of vulnerability detection across multiple software ecosystems using a curated ground-truth dataset derived from the Open Source Vulnerabilities (OSV) database. The dataset explicitly maps vulnerabilities to concrete package versions and enables a systematic comparison of detection results across different tools and services. Since vulnerability databases such as OSV are continuously updated, the dataset used in this study represents a snapshot of the vulnerability landscape at the time of the evaluation. To support reproducibility and future studies, we provide an open-source tool that automatically reconstructs the dataset from the current OSV database using the methodology described in this paper. Our evaluation highlights systematic differences between vulnerability detection systems and demonstrates the importance of transparent dataset construction for reproducible empirical security research.

</details>


### [77] [Hidden Dependencies and Component Variants in SBOM-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21278)
*Shawn Rasheed,Max McPhee,Lisa Patterson,Stephen MacDonell,Jens Dietrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究SBOM中组件身份和依赖关系的不匹配模式，发现隐藏的代码级依赖和组件变体（克隆）会导致不同漏洞扫描器产生不一致的漏洞报告和VEX处理结果，揭示了当前SBOM生产和消费的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着供应链攻击的增加，SBOM成为重要的漏洞管理技术，但其有效性取决于SBOM对组件身份和实际依赖关系的准确表示。当前对SBOM中组件不匹配模式如何影响漏洞分析效果缺乏系统研究。

Method: 识别并系统研究了两种不匹配模式：未表示为组件级依赖的隐藏代码级依赖，以及扫描器无法一致识别的组件变体（克隆）。通过实证评估这些模式对主流SBOM漏洞扫描器的影响。

Result: 发现这些不匹配模式会导致不同SBOM扫描器之间出现不一致的漏洞报告结果，以及不一致的VEX（Vulnerability Exploitability eXchange）声明处理。

Conclusion: 当前SBOM生产和消费存在局限性，需要更丰富的依赖表示和组件身份机制来提升SBOM在漏洞管理中的有效性。

Abstract: Software Bills of Material (SBOMs) have emerged as an important technology for vulnerability management amid rising supply-chain attacks. They represent component relationships within a software product and support software composition analysis (SCA) by linking components to known vulnerabilities. However, the effectiveness of SBOM-based analysis depends on how accurately SBOMs represent component identities and actual dependencies in software. This paper studies two mismatch patterns: hidden code-level dependencies that are not represented as component-level dependencies, and component variants (clones) that cannot be identified consistently by scanners. We show that these mismatches can lead to inconsistent vulnerability reporting and inconsistent handling of VEX statements across popular SBOM-based vulnerability scanners. These results highlight limitations in current SBOM production and consumption and motivate richer dependency representation and component identity.

</details>


### [78] [Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21380)
*Wang Shi Hai,Chen Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出IRAP方法，通过交互式检索增强偏好 elicitation，将自然语言描述的性能需求自动量化为数学函数，在四个真实数据集上相比10种SOTA方法实现最高40倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件性能需求以自然语言描述，将其量化为数学形式对软件工程至关重要，但需求表述的模糊性和人类认知的不确定性导致解释高度不确定，自动化量化是一个尚未解决且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 形式化定义了该问题，并提出IRAP（交互式检索增强偏好 elicitation）方法。该方法利用问题特定知识检索和推理偏好，同时指导与利益相关者的渐进式交互，减少认知负担。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上与10种最先进方法对比，IRAP在所有案例中均表现优越，在仅需5轮交互的情况下实现高达40倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: IRAP通过交互式检索增强偏好 elicitation，有效解决了性能需求量化中的模糊性和不确定性挑战，以极少的交互轮次取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: Since software performance requirements are documented in natural language, quantifying them into mathematical forms is essential for software engineering. Yet, the vagueness in performance requirements and uncertainty of human cognition have caused highly uncertain ambiguity in the interpretations, rendering their automated quantification an unaddressed and challenging problem. In this paper, we formalize the problem and propose IRAP, an approach that quantifies performance requirements into mathematical functions via interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation. IRAP differs from the others in that it explicitly derives from problem-specific knowledge to retrieve and reason the preferences, which also guides the progressive interaction with stakeholders, while reducing the cognitive overhead. Experiment results against 10 state-of-the-art methods on four real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of IRAP on all cases with up to 40x improvements under as few as five rounds of interactions.

</details>


### [79] [Assessing the Impact of Requirement Ambiguity on LLM-based Function-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21505)
*Di Yang,Xinou Xie,Xiuwen Yang,Ming Hu,Yihao Huang,Yueling Zhang,Weikai Miao,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Geguang Pu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Orchid基准，首个针对模糊需求的代码生成基准，包含1,304个函数级任务涵盖四种模糊类型。实验表明模糊性会持续降低所有LLM的代码生成性能，且高级模型受影响更显著，LLM缺乏自主识别和解决需求模糊性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常假设需求清晰无歧义，忽略了真实软件开发中自然语言固有的模糊性问题，导致对LLM在模糊需求下代码生成行为的理解存在实证空白。

Method: 构建了Orchid基准数据集，包含1,304个函数级任务，涵盖词汇、句法、语义和模糊性四种模糊类型，并基于此设计系统性实验评估LLM在模糊需求下的代码生成表现。

Result: 1) 模糊性持续降低所有评估LLM的性能，且对高级模型负面影响最显著；2) LLM常对同一模糊需求生成功能不同的实现；3) LLM缺乏自主识别或解决模糊性的能力。

Conclusion: 模糊需求与清晰需求之间存在显著的性能差距，亟需在下一代自动化软件工程工具中引入模糊感知技术。

Abstract: Software requirement ambiguity is ubiquitous in real-world development, stemming from the inherent imprecision of natural language and the varying interpretations of stakeholders. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating code from precise specifications, such ambiguity poses a significant obstacle to reliable automated code generation. Existing benchmarks typically assume clear and unambiguous requirements, leaving an empirical gap in understanding how LLMs behave when faced with the inherent uncertainty of real-world software requirements. In this paper, we introduce Orchid, the first code generation benchmark specifically designed with ambiguous requirements. It comprises 1,304 function-level tasks covering four distinct types of ambiguity: lexical, syntactic, semantic, and vagueness. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the impact of requirement ambiguity on LLM-based code generation. Our results demonstrate that ambiguity consistently degrades the performance of all evaluated LLMs, with the most pronounced negative effects observed in highly advanced models. Furthermore, we observe that LLMs frequently produce functionally divergent implementations for the same ambiguous requirement and lack the capability to identify or resolve such ambiguity autonomously. These findings reveal a significant performance gap between clear and ambiguous requirements, underscoring the urgent need for ambiguity-aware techniques in the next generation of automated software engineering tools. The Orchid benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SII-YDD/Orchid.

</details>


### [80] [SpecSyn: LLM-based Synthesis and Refinement of Formal Specifications for Real-world Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21570)
*Lezhi Ma,Shangqing Liu,Yi Li,Qiong Wu,Han Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecSyn是一种基于LLM的规范生成方法，通过程序分解和基于语义非等价变异与变体辨别的规范精炼机制，显著提升了规范生成的精确率（>90%）和召回率（>75%），成功处理了实际程序中1365个目标属性中的1071个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的规范生成方法缺乏处理大规模输入程序的有效策略，也没有评估和保证所生成规范强度的机制，导致其难以从真实世界的复杂程序中提取精确规范以支持目标属性的验证。

Method: SpecSyn首先将输入程序分解为独立片段，分别进行迭代规范生成。创新性地引入基于语义非等价程序变异和变体辨别的规范精炼机制，用于评估和增强生成规范的语义强度。

Result: SpecSyn保持超过90%的精确率和超过75%的召回率，显著优于现有基于LLM的方法。在进一步评估中，成功处理了1365个目标属性中的1071个。

Conclusion: SpecSyn通过程序分解和基于变异的规范精炼机制，有效解决了现有LLM方法在真实世界程序验证中的局限性，证明了其在真实世界程序验证任务中的适用性。

Abstract: Program verification is a formal technique to rigorously ensure the correctness and fault-freeness of software systems. However, constructing comprehensive interprocedural specifications for full verification obligations is time-consuming and labor-intensive, giving rise to automated specification generation approaches. Despite the significant advancements in these approaches brought by Large Language Models (LLMs), existing LLM-empowered approaches still suffer from significant limitations: they lack effective strategies for handling sizable input programs, and are typically equipped with no mechanisms to evaluate and guarantee the strength of the generated specifications. The limitations impair their ability to extract precise specifications from real-world complicated programs to support the verification of target properties, thereby hindering the applicability of existing approaches in verification tasks on real-world programs. To remedy this gap, we propose SpecSyn, a novel LLM-based specification generation method. SpecSyn first decomposes the input program into individual segments, which are handled respectively by the subsequent iterative specification generation process. Innovatively, we incorporate into the process a specification refinement mechanism based on semantic-non-equivalent program mutations and variant discrimination, assessing and enhancing the semantic strength of the generated specifications. Extensive experiments show that SpecSyn maintains high precision over 90% and outstanding recall over 75%, significantly outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. In further evaluations, SpecSyn successfully handles 1071 out of 1365 target properties for open-source programs, proving its applicability on real-world program verification tasks.

</details>


### [81] [A Metamorphic Testing Approach to Diagnosing Memorization in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21579)
*Milan De Koning,Ali Asgari,Pouria Derakhshanfar,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过将蜕变测试与负对数似然相结合，发现LLM在程序修复中因数据泄漏导致性能被高估，且在变换后的基准测试上修复成功率显著下降（-4.1%至-15.98%），且下降幅度与NLL高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动程序修复中的表现可能因数据泄漏（模型记忆了基准测试中的错误修复）而被高估，现有评估方法无法有效揭示这一问题。

Method: 将蜕变测试（MT）与负对数似然（NLL）相结合。对Defects4J和GitBug-Java两个数据集应用保持语义的变换构建变体基准，评估7个LLM在原始和变换版本上的修复成功率，并分析性能下降与NLL的关系。

Result: 所有评估的LLM在变换基准上的补丁生成成功率均大幅下降（GPT-4o下降-4.1%，Llama-3.1下降-15.98%），且性能下降程度与NLL强相关。

Conclusion: MT与NLL结合能提供更强、更可靠的数据泄漏证据；单独使用蜕变测试也有助于减轻数据泄漏对LLM-based APR评估的影响。

Abstract: LLM-based automated program repair (APR) techniques have shown promising results in reducing debugging costs. However, prior results can be affected by data leakage: large language models (LLMs) may memorize bug fixes when evaluation benchmarks overlap with their pretraining data, leading to inflated performance estimates. In this paper, we investigate whether we can better reveal data leakage by combining metamorphic testing (MT) with negative log-likelihood (NLL), which has been used in prior work as a proxy for memorization. We construct variant benchmarks by applying semantics-preserving transformations to two widely used datasets, Defects4J and GitBug-Java. Using these benchmarks, we evaluate the repair success rates of seven LLMs on both original and transformed versions, and analyze the relationship between performance degradation and NLL. Our results show that all evaluated state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit substantial drops in patch generation success rates on transformed benchmarks, ranging from -4.1% for GPT-4o to -15.98% for Llama-3.1. Furthermore, we find that this degradation strongly correlates with NLL on the original benchmarks, suggesting that models perform better on instances they are more likely to have memorized. These findings show that combining MT with NLL provides stronger and more reliable evidence of data leakage, while metamorphic testing alone can help mitigate its effects in LLM-based APR evaluations.
