Extensive experiments over 6 benchmarks show that our method significantly enhances both grounding accuracy and critic reliability.

</details>


### [119] [Understanding and Mitigating Spurious Signal Amplification in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21327)
*Yongcan Yu,Lingxiao He,Jian Liang,Kuangpu Guo,Meng Wang,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDRL框架，通过频率采样去噪、去偏优势估计和基于共识的离策略精炼，有效缓解测试时强化学习中的伪信号问题，在多个数学推理基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习（TTRL）在推理阶段通过伪标签进行模型自适应，易受标签噪声产生的虚假优化信号影响。现有方法未有效处理中等一致性响应构成的模糊区域带来的奖励噪声，且组相对优势估计会放大此类噪声。

Method: DDRL包含三个核心组件：1）基于频率的采样策略，排除模糊样本并维持正负例平衡；2）采用固定优势值的去偏优势估计，消除组相对策略优化引入的偏差；3）基于共识的离策略精炼阶段，利用拒绝采样数据集实现高效稳定的模型更新。

Result: 在多种大语言模型和多个数学推理基准上的实验表明，DDRL持续优于现有的TTRL基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地处理测试时强化学习中的伪信号问题，DDRL为推理阶段模型自适应提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) always adapts models at inference time via pseudo-labeling, leaving it vulnerable to spurious optimization signals from label noise. Through an empirical study, we observe that responses with medium consistency form an ambiguity region and constitute the primary source of reward noise. Crucially, we find that such spurious signals can be even amplified through group-relative advantage estimation. Motivated by these findings, we propose a unified framework, Debiased and Denoised test-time Reinforcement Learning (DDRL), to mitigate spurious signals. Concretely, DDRL first applies a frequency-based sampling strategy to exclude ambiguous samples while maintaining a balanced set of positive and negative examples. It then adopts a debiased advantage estimation with fixed advantages, removing the bias introduced by group-relative policy optimization. Finally, DDRL incorporates a consensus-based off-policy refinement stage, which leverages the rejection-sampled dataset to enable efficient and stable model updates. Experiments on three large language models across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DDRL consistently outperforms existing TTRL baselines. The code will soon be released at https://github.com/yuyongcan/DDRL.

</details>


### [120] [Sub-Token Routing in LoRA for Adaptation and Query-Aware KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21335)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出子令牌路由（sub-token routing）机制，在LoRA适配的Transformer中实现KV缓存压缩。通过查询无关和查询感知两种设计，在令牌表示内部进行细粒度值组路由，相比传统令牌级方法实现了更好的质量-压缩权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer效率优化（如令牌、页面、头或层）的粒度过粗。相关令牌内部并非均匀一致，在保留预算下，有价值的值组在令牌间和令牌内分布不均，因此KV压缩不应是令牌级的全有全无决策。

Method: 提出两种子令牌路由机制：1) 查询无关设计：结合路由子空间LoRA与KV路径上的值组路由，用于压缩感知语言建模；2) 查询感知设计：基于预测器的选择器，利用查询条件相关性在上下文令牌/值组对上分配全局保留预算，用于下游任务保持的KV压缩。

Result: 查询无关设计改善了语言建模的质量-压缩权衡；查询感知设计在降低KV预算下保持了下游行为。令牌级和子令牌级路由形成互补的压缩轴：令牌级决定哪些令牌全局存活，子令牌级决定存活令牌的内部压缩方式。

Conclusion: 子令牌路由为Transformer效率优化提供了比传统粗粒度单元更精细的控制轴，令牌级与子令牌级路由可互补使用以实现更优的KV压缩策略。

Abstract: Sub-token routing offers a finer control axis for transformer efficiency than the coarse units used in most prior work, such as tokens, pages, heads, or layers. In this paper, we study routing within a token representation itself in LoRA-adapted transformers. The motivation is that a relevant token need not be internally uniform: under a retention budget, preserved value groups are distributed unevenly both across tokens and within tokens, which suggests that KV compression need not be an all-or-nothing decision at token level. We study this fine-grained routing mechanism in two settings. For compression-aware language modeling, we introduce a query-independent design that combines routed subspace LoRA with value-group routing on the KV path. For downstream-task-preserving KV compression, we introduce a query-aware design in which a predictor-based selector allocates a global retention budget over context-token/value-group pairs using query-conditioned relevance. Experiments show that the query-independent design improves the quality-compression tradeoff for language modeling, while the query-aware design preserves downstream behavior under reduced KV budgets. We further examine the relation between token-level and sub-token-level query-aware routing, and show that they form complementary compression axes: token-level methods determine which tokens survive globally, while sub-token routing determines how the surviving tokens are compressed internally.

</details>


### [121] [Decoupled Travel Planning with Behavior Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21354)
*Duanyang Yuan,Sihang Zhou,Yanning Hou,Xiaoshu Chen,Haoyuan Chen,Ke Liang,Jiyuan Liu,Chuan Ma,Xinwang Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出行为森林方法，将多约束旅行规划问题分解为并行行为树森林，每棵树负责一个子任务，通过全局协调机制整合，以LLM作为决策引擎，在TravelPlanner和ChinaTravel基准上分别提升6.67%和11.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划方法将所有子任务和约束置于单一决策空间中，无法区分局部约束和全局约束，导致模型在每个决策步骤需同时推理局部和全局约束，增加了推理负担并降低了规划效率。

Method: 将决策过程构建为并行行为树森林，每棵行为树负责一个子任务，引入全局协调机制管理树间交互；LLM嵌入行为树节点作为决策引擎，执行基于任务特定约束的局部推理，行为树提供显式控制结构引导LLM生成。

Result: 在TravelPlanner基准上比最先进方法提升6.67%，在ChinaTravel基准上提升11.82%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过解耦复杂任务和约束为可管理的子空间，实现任务特定推理，降低LLM认知负担，有效提升复杂多约束旅行规划的性能。

Abstract: Behavior sequences, composed of executable steps, serve as the operational foundation for multi-constraint planning problems such as travel planning. In such tasks, each planning step is not only constrained locally but also influenced by global constraints spanning multiple subtasks, leading to a tightly coupled and complex decision process. Existing travel planning methods typically rely on a single decision space that entangles all subtasks and constraints, failing to distinguish between locally acting constraints within a subtask and global constraints that span multiple subtasks. Consequently, the model is forced to jointly reason over local and global constraints at each decision step, increasing the reasoning burden and reducing planning efficiency. To address this problem, we propose the Behavior Forest method. Specifically, our approach structures the decision-making process into a forest of parallel behavior trees, where each behavior tree is responsible for a subtask. A global coordination mechanism is introduced to orchestrate the interactions among these trees, enabling modular and coherent travel planning. Within this framework, large language models are embedded as decision engines within behavior tree nodes, performing localized reasoning conditioned on task-specific constraints to generate candidate subplans and adapt decisions based on coordination feedback. The behavior trees, in turn, provide an explicit control structure that guides LLM generation. This design decouples complex tasks and constraints into manageable subspaces, enabling task-specific reasoning and reducing the cognitive load of LLM. Experimental results show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 6.67% on the TravelPlanner and by 11.82% on the ChinaTravel benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness in increasing LLM performance for complex multi-constraint travel planning.

</details>


### [122] [Supervised Learning Has a Necessary Geometric Blind Spot: Theory, Consequences, and Minimal Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21395)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个定理：经验风险最小化（ERM）必然导致学习到的编码器在训练数据中与标签相关但在测试时无关的方向上保持非零的Jacobian敏感性，这一几何盲点是监督学习的固有属性。论文引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI）来诊断该问题，并证明PMH方法能有效修复该盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示监督学习中ERM目标函数固有的几何缺陷——模型必须在与标签相关但实际上是干扰的方向上保持非零梯度敏感性，从而统一解释对抗脆弱性、纹理偏差、腐败鲁棒性差和鲁棒性-准确率权衡等此前被视为独立的问题。

Method: 1) 提出定理1证明监督学习的几何盲点；2) 引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI）度量各向异性路径长度畸变；3) 证明Proposition 5：高斯形式的扰动是均匀惩罚编码器Jacobian的唯一扰动律；4) 在多任务（7个视觉任务、BERT/SST-2、ImageNet ViT-B/16）和不同规模模型（CLIP、DINO、SAM、不同规模语言模型）上验证。

Result: PGD对抗训练达到Jacobian Frobenius范数2.91但TDI最差（1.336），而PMH达到TDI 0.904。盲点在基础模型尺度上存在，随语言模型规模增加单调恶化（盲点比率从0.860降至0.742），ERM微调放大54%，PMH可修复11倍。

Conclusion: 监督学习的几何盲点是ERM的数学必然结果而非偶然失败，TDI是唯一能检测该关键失效模式的指标，而PMH通过单一项训练项即可有效修复该盲点。

Abstract: We prove that empirical risk minimisation (ERM) imposes a necessary geometric constraint on learned representations: any encoder that minimises supervised loss must retain non-zero Jacobian sensitivity in directions that are label-correlated in training data but nuisance at test time. This is not a contingent failure of current methods; it is a mathematical consequence of the supervised objective itself. We call this the geometric blind spot of supervised learning (Theorem 1), and show it holds across proper scoring rules, architectures, and dataset sizes.
  This single theorem unifies four lines of prior empirical work that were previously treated separately: non-robust predictive features, texture bias, corruption fragility, and the robustness-accuracy tradeoff. In this framing, adversarial vulnerability is one consequence of a broader structural fact about supervised learning geometry.
  We introduce Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a diagnostic that measures the theorem's bounded quantity directly, and show why common alternatives miss the key failure mode. PGD adversarial training reaches Jacobian Frobenius 2.91 yet has the worst clean-input geometry (TDI 1.336), while PMH achieves TDI 0.904. TDI is the only metric that detects this dissociation because it measures isotropic path-length distortion -- the exact quantity Theorem 1 bounds.
  Across seven vision tasks, BERT/SST-2, and ImageNet ViT-B/16 backbones used by CLIP, DINO, and SAM, the blind spot is measurable and repairable. It is present at foundation-model scale, worsens monotonically across language-model sizes (blind-spot ratio 0.860 to 0.765 to 0.742 from 66M to 340M), and is amplified by task-specific ERM fine-tuning (+54%), while PMH repairs it by 11x with one additional training term whose Gaussian form Proposition 5 proves is the unique perturbation law that uniformly penalises the encoder Jacobian.

</details>


### [123] [Even More Guarantees for Variational Inference in the Presence of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21407)
*Lena Zellinger,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分推断中当变分族不包含目标分布时，仍能恢复目标特征的条件，推导了在前向KL散度和α散度下保证均值精确恢复的充分条件，并分析了优化失败的原因及变分族与α值的选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分推断中变分族通常过于简单而无法包含真实目标分布，这种模型设定错误下能否以及如何恢复目标分布的关键特征是一个核心理论问题。

Method: 基于目标对称性拓展了位置-尺度族的鲁棒变分推断结果，利用前向KL散度和α散度推导均值精确恢复的充分条件，并通过反例分析条件缺失时优化失效的原因。

Result: 给出了在模型设定错误下仍能精确恢复目标均值的充分条件，揭示了优化失败的具体机制，并为变分族和α值的选择提供了初步指导原则。

Conclusion: 即使变分族存在设定错误，在满足特定对称性条件下仍可恢复目标均值，且α散度的选择对鲁棒性有重要影响。

Abstract: When approximating an intractable density via variational inference (VI) the variational family is typically chosen as a simple parametric family that very likely does not contain the target. This raises the question: Under which conditions can we recover characteristics of the target despite misspecification? In this work, we extend previous results on robust VI with location-scale families under target symmetries. We derive sufficient conditions guaranteeing exact recovery of the mean when using the forward Kullback-Leibler divergence and $α$-divergences. We further show how and why optimization can fail to recover the target mean in the absence of our sufficient conditions, providing initial guidelines on the choice of the variational family and $α$-value.

</details>


### [124] [Conditional anomaly detection with soft harmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21462)
*Michal Valko,Branislav Kveton,Hamed Valizadegan,Gregory F. Cooper,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于软调和解的非参数条件异常检测方法，通过估计标签置信度来识别异常标注，并加入正则化以避免检测孤立点和边界点。在合成数据集、UCI机器学习数据集以及真实电子健康记录数据集上验证了该方法在检测异常标签方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件异常检测方法在处理异常标注（即响应或类标签异常的数据实例）时存在局限性，需要一种新的非参数方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 基于软调和解（soft harmonic solution）的非参数方法，通过估计标签的置信度来检测异常标注；引入正则化项以避免将孤立点或分布支持边界上的样本误判为异常。

Result: 在合成数据集和UCI机器学习数据集上，与多个基线方法相比，该方法在检测异常标签方面表现更优；在真实电子健康记录数据集中，成功识别出异常的患者管理决策。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于软调和解的正则化条件异常检测方法能够有效识别异常标签，并在实际医疗场景中具有应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of conditional anomaly detection that aims to identify data instances with an unusual response or a class label. We develop a new non-parametric approach for conditional anomaly detection based on the soft harmonic solution, with which we estimate the confidence of the label to detect anomalous mislabeling. We further regularize the solution to avoid the detection of isolated examples and examples on the boundary of the distribution support. We demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method on several synthetic and UCI ML datasets in detecting unusual labels when compared to several baseline approaches. We also evaluate the performance of our method on a real-world electronic health record dataset where we seek to identify unusual patient-management decisions.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamical Priors as a Training Objective in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21464)
*Sukesh Subaharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态先验强化学习（DP-RL），通过在策略梯度学习中引入基于证据积累和滞后效应的外部状态动力学辅助损失，在不修改奖励、环境或策略架构的情况下，塑造动作概率的时间演化轨迹，从而产生具有时间相干性的决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL优化奖励但缺乏对决策时间演化的约束，导致策略可能出现置信度突变、振荡或退化性不活动等时间不连贯行为。

Method: 在策略梯度学习中引入基于外部状态动力学的辅助损失，该动力学实现了证据积累和滞后效应，从而塑造动作概率在时间上的演化过程。

Result: 在三个最小环境中，动态先验以任务依赖的方式系统地改变了决策轨迹，促进了具有时间结构的、无法用通用平滑解释的行为。

Conclusion: 训练目标本身即可控制RL智能体决策的时间几何特性。

Abstract: Standard reinforcement learning (RL) optimizes policies for reward but imposes few constraints on how decisions evolve over time. As a result, policies may achieve high performance while exhibiting temporally incoherent behavior such as abrupt confidence shifts, oscillations, or degenerate inactivity. We introduce Dynamical Prior Reinforcement Learning (DP-RL), a training framework that augments policy gradient learning with an auxiliary loss derived from external state dynamics that implement evidence accumulation and hysteresis. Without modifying the reward, environment, or policy architecture, this prior shapes the temporal evolution of action probabilities during learning. Across three minimal environments, we show that dynamical priors systematically alter decision trajectories in task-dependent ways, promoting temporally structured behavior that cannot be explained by generic smoothing. These results demonstrate that training objectives alone can control the temporal geometry of decision-making in RL agents.

</details>


### [126] [Generalizing Numerical Reasoning in Table Data through Operation Sketches and Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21495)
*Hanjun Cho,Gahyun Yoo,Hanseong Kim,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaNOS是一个持续预训练框架，通过头部匿名化、操作草图设计和保证正确性的自监督预训练，解耦领域语义与数值操作结构，提高了专家领域表格数值推理的跨域迁移能力。在FinQA上仅用10%训练数据达到80.13%执行准确率，超SFT全量数据（73.97%）及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5-Pro等闭源模型，且跨域差距小于2pp（SFT超10pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值推理模型在专家领域表格上域内精度高但跨域迁移能力差，SFT训练模型倾向于依赖表头-操作捷径而非结构推理，导致对域偏移鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出TaNOS框架，包含三个组件：①头部匿名化（header anonymization）减少词汇记忆；②操作草图（operation sketches）提供最小化结构线索；③基于程序优先（program-first）的自监督预训练，从给定表格构造正确性有保证的程序-问题对。

Result: 在8B指令微调模型上，FinQA任务仅用10%训练数据达到80.13%执行准确率，优于SFT全量数据（73.97%）及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5-Pro。域偏移实验中，跨域差距小于2pp，而SFT超过10pp。

Conclusion: 结构引导的操作草图、与表头无关的表示以及正确性有保证的自监督学习能够有效提升数值推理在多样化专家领域表格上的鲁棒性和跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Numerical reasoning over expert-domain tables often exhibits high in-domain accuracy but limited robustness to domain shift. Models trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on specific datasets tend to rely on header-operation shortcuts rather than structural reasoning. We introduce TaNOS, a continual pre-training framework comprising three components: (i) header anonymization to reduce lexical memorization, (ii) operation sketches that provide minimal structural cues, and (iii) self-supervised pretraining that constructs correctness-guaranteed program-question pairs from given tables in a program-first manner. By decoupling domain semantics and numerical operation structure, TaNOS improves the transferability of numerical reasoning. Applied to an 8B instruction-tuned model, TaNOS achieves 80.13% execution accuracy on FinQA with only 10% train data, outperforming SFT baseline (73.97%) with full train data and proprietary models such as GPT-5, Gemini-2.5-Pro. Furthermore, in the domain-shift experiments, TaNOS displays nearly-negligible cross-domain gap (<2pp) when standard SFT shows over 10pp gap. These results suggest that structural guidance with operation sketches, header-agnostic representations, and correctness-guaranteed self-supervision can improve the robustness of numerical reasoning across diverse expert-domain tables.

</details>


### [127] [Large-Scale Data Parallelization of Product Quantization and Inverted Indexing Using Dask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21645)
*Ashley N. Abraham,Andrew Strelzoff,Haley R. Dozier,Althea C. Henslee,Mark A. Chappell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合乘积量化（PQ）、倒排索引和Dask的分布式近似最近邻（ANN）搜索方法，通过分治策略将大规模数据处理的计算开销降低到中规模数据所需的水平，同时不牺牲搜索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最近邻（NN）搜索面临巨大的计算开销，精确搜索在处理海量数据时受限于计算资源。虽然近似最近邻（ANN）搜索和乘积量化（PQ）在一定程度上缓解了这一问题，但大规模高维数据的聚类仍然存在内存和时间的双重瓶颈。本文旨在探索一种新的分治策略，在不牺牲准确率的前提下降低大规模ANN搜索的计算需求。

Method: 采用乘积量化（PQ）进行内存高效的向量压缩，结合倒排索引（Inverted Indexing）实现快速检索，并利用Dask进行分布式并行计算，将大规模数据集分割处理后再合并结果，实现分治策略。

Result: 该方法能够在保持搜索精度的同时，将大规模数据的计算需求降低到中规模数据处理所需的水平。

Conclusion: 结合PQ、倒排索引和Dask的分布式分治策略是一种有效降低大规模ANN搜索计算开销的方法，能够在精度无损的前提下显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: Large-scale Nearest Neighbor (NN) search, though widely utilized in the similarity search field, remains challenged by the computational limitations inherent in processing large scale data. In an effort to decrease the computational expense needed, Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search is often used in applications that do not require the exact similarity search, but instead can rely on an approximation. Product Quantization (PQ) is a memory-efficient ANN effective for clustering all sizes of datasets. Clustering large-scale, high dimensional data requires a heavy computational expense, in both memory-cost and execution time. This work focuses on a unique way to divide and conquer the large scale data in Python using PQ, Inverted Indexing and Dask, combining the results without compromising the accuracy and reducing computational requirements to the level required when using medium-scale data.

</details>


### [128] [Evaluating Post-hoc Explanations of the Transformer-based Genome Language Model DNABERT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21690)
*Isabel Kurth,Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将AttnLRP（层相关性传播的注意力机制扩展）应用于Transformer-based基因组语言模型DNABERT-2，实现核苷酸和token级别的解释，并通过多指标评估证明其能可靠地捕捉已知生物学模式，表明基因组语言模型与CNNs一样可用于生物学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CNNs在基因组序列上的解释已被证明能捕获相关生物学模式，但更强大的Transformer-based基因组语言模型（gLMs）是否同样具备可解释性尚不清楚。鉴于生物学洞察往往比预测性能本身更有价值，因此亟需验证gLMs能否产生可靠的生物学相关解释。

Method: 将AttnLRP（层相关性传播对注意力机制的扩展）应用于DNABERT-2模型；提出从token级别到核苷酸级别的解释迁移策略；在基因组数据集上使用多个评估指标进行验证；并与基线CNN模型的解释进行系统比较。

Result: AttnLRP在DNABERT-2上产生了与已知生物学模式一致的可解释性结果；证明了gLMs与CNNs一样能够提供生物学洞察；解决了不同架构间相关性归因的可比性问题。

Conclusion: AttnLRP适配于Transformer-based基因组语言模型是可行的，DNABERT-2的解释能够可靠地对应已知生物学模式，这表明基因组语言模型可以像CNNs一样用于推导生物学见解，为gLMs的可解释性做出了贡献。

Abstract: Explaining deep neural network predictions on genome sequences enables biological insight and hypothesis generation-often of greater interest than predictive performance alone. While explanations of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been shown to capture relevant patterns in genome sequences, it is unclear whether this transfers to more expressive Transformer-based genome language models (gLMs). To answer this question, we adapt AttnLRP, an extension of layer-wise relevance propagation to the attention mechanism, and apply it to the state-of-the-art gLM DNABERT-2. Thereby, we propose strategies to transfer explanations from token and nucleotide level. We evaluate the adaption of AttnLRP on genomic datasets using multiple metrics. Further, we provide an extensive comparison between the explanations of DNABERT-2 and a baseline CNN. Our results demonstrate that AttnLRP yields reliable explanations corresponding to known biological patterns. Hence, like CNNs, gLMs can also help derive biological insights. This work contributes to the explainability of gLMs and addresses the comparability of relevance attributions across different architectures.

</details>


### [129] [Fairness under uncertainty in sequential decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21711)
*Michelle Seng Ah Lee,Kirtan Padh,David Watson,Niki Kilbertus,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对序列决策中的公平性问题，提出了一种不确定性分类体系（模型不确定性、反馈不确定性、预测不确定性），通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化这些不确定性，并展示忽略不确定性如何加剧对弱势群体的伤害，最后通过算法示例和实验证明不确定性感知的决策能减少群体间结果差异，同时保持机构目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平机器学习研究主要集中于监督学习，但现实中的ML应用多为在线序列决策（如贷款审批），其中历史决策影响未来决策，且存在反事实不可观测、样本有限等问题，导致弱势群体因历史排斥和选择性反馈而系统性数据不足，传统公平方法无法有效处理这种不确定性分布不均的问题。

Method: 提出一个包含三类不确定性（模型不确定性、反馈不确定性、预测不确定性）的分类体系；通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化模型不确定性和反馈不确定性；分析忽略未观测空间对决策者和决策对象的伤害；给出能够减少弱势群体结果方差同时保持机构期望效用的算法示例；在模拟数据上进行实验验证。

Result: 不确定性感知的探索策略能够改变公平性指标；选择性反馈和不确定性不均会产生群体间差距；所提出的框架能够帮助从业者诊断、审计和治理公平风险。

Conclusion: 当不公平是由不确定性驱动而非偶然噪声时，必须将不确定性纳入考量才能实现公平且有效的决策。该框架为从业者提供了共同语言和工具来评估和管理序列决策系统中的公平风险。

Abstract: Fair machine learning (ML) methods help identify and mitigate the risk that algorithms encode or automate social injustices. Algorithmic approaches alone cannot resolve structural inequalities, but they can support socio-technical decision systems by surfacing discriminatory biases, clarifying trade-offs, and enabling governance. Although fairness is well studied in supervised learning, many real ML applications are online and sequential, with prior decisions informing future ones. Each decision is taken under uncertainty due to unobserved counterfactuals and finite samples, with dire consequences for under-represented groups, systematically under-observed due to historical exclusion and selective feedback. A bank cannot know whether a denied loan would have been repaid, and may have less data on marginalized populations.
  This paper introduces a taxonomy of uncertainty in sequential decision-making -- model, feedback, and prediction uncertainty -- providing shared vocabulary for assessing systems where uncertainty is unevenly distributed across groups. We formalize model and feedback uncertainty via counterfactual logic and reinforcement learning, and illustrate harms to decision makers (unrealized gains/losses) and subjects (compounding exclusion, reduced access) of policies that ignore the unobserved space. Algorithmic examples show it is possible to reduce outcome variance for disadvantaged groups while preserving institutional objectives (e.g. expected utility). Experiments on data simulated with varying bias show how unequal uncertainty and selective feedback produce disparities, and how uncertainty-aware exploration alters fairness metrics. The framework equips practitioners to diagnose, audit, and govern fairness risks. Where uncertainty drives unfairness rather than incidental noise, accounting for it is essential to fair and effective decision-making.

</details>


### [130] [Transferable Physics-Informed Representations via Closed-Form Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21761)
*Jian Cheng Wong,Isaac Yin Chung Lai,Pao-Hsiung Chiu,Chin Chun Ooi,Abhishek Gupta,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pi-PINN框架，通过可迁移的物理信息表示和闭式头适应（伪逆求解）实现快速且泛化的PDE求解，比传统PINN快100-1000倍，相对误差降低10-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN在缺乏训练样本时难以泛化到未见过的PDE实例，无法有效预测或求解新问题。

Method: 基于快速伪逆PINN框架（Pi-PINN），学习共享嵌入空间中的可迁移物理信息表示，通过最小二乘最优伪逆在PDE约束下实现闭式头适应，结合数据驱动的多任务学习损失与物理信息损失。

Result: 在泊松方程、亥姆霍兹方程和伯格斯方程上，Pi-PINN比典型PINN快100-1000倍，比纯数据驱动模型相对误差低10-100倍，即使在仅有两个训练样本时也表现优异。

Conclusion: 可迁移表示与闭式头适应相结合的方法能显著提升PINN在跨PDE系列问题上的效率和泛化能力，对科学和工程应用具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have garnered significant interest for their potential in solving partial differential equations (PDEs) that govern a wide range of physical phenomena. By incorporating physical laws into the learning process, PINN models have demonstrated the ability to learn physical outcomes reasonably well. However, current PINN approaches struggle to predict or solve new PDEs effectively when there is a lack of training examples, indicating they do not generalize well to unseen problem instances. In this paper, we present a transferable learning approach for PINNs premised on a fast Pseudoinverse PINN framework (Pi-PINN). Pi-PINN learns a transferable physics-informed representation in a shared embedding space and enables rapid solving of both known and unknown PDE instances via closed-form head adaptation using a least-squares-optimal pseudoinverse under PDE constraints. We further investigate the synergies between data-driven multi-task learning loss and physics-informed loss, providing insights into the design of more performant PINNs. We demonstrate the effectiveness of Pi-PINN on various PDE problems, including Poisson's equation, Helmholtz equation, and Burgers' equation, achieving fast and accurate physics-informed solutions without requiring any data for unseen instances. Pi-PINN can produce predictions 100-1000 times faster than a typical PINN, while producing predictions with 10-100 times lower relative error than a typical data-driven model even with only two training samples. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of transferable representations with closed-form head adaptation to enhance the efficiency and generalization of PINNs across PDE families and scientific and engineering applications.

</details>


### [131] [An effective variant of the Hartigan $k$-means algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21798)
*François Clément,Stefan Steinerberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在 k-means 聚类问题中，Hartigan 算法通常优于 Lloyd 算法 5%-10%，而作者提出的对 Hartigan 方法的微小变体可额外提升 2%-5% 的性能，且该提升随维度或 k 值增加而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Hartigan 算法已知比 Lloyd 算法效果更好，但仍有进一步改进空间，作者希望通过微小修改来提升聚类性能。

Method: 对 Hartigan 算法进行非常微小的变体修改。

Result: 相比 Hartigan 算法，新变体额外提升 2%-5% 的性能，且该改善在维度或 k 值增大时更为显著。

Conclusion: Hartigan 算法的微小变体能有效提升 k-means 聚类性能，且高维或大 k 场景下收益更大。

Abstract: The k-means problem is perhaps the classical clustering problem and often synonymous with Lloyd's algorithm (1957). It has become clear that Hartigan's algorithm (1975) gives better results in almost all cases, Telgarsky-Vattani note a typical improvement of $5\%$ -- $10\%$. We point out that a very minor variation of Hartigan's method leads to another $2\%$ -- $5\%$ improvement; the improvement tends to become larger when either dimension or $k$ increase.

</details>


### [132] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个通用商空间扩散建模框架，并应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成，通过减少对群作用对应分量的学习需求，简化了学习难度，并保证了采样器能恢复目标分布，在小分子和蛋白质结构生成上优于传统对称性处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在科学领域（如分子3D结构生成）取得进展，但许多任务存在内在对称性（如SE(3)群对称性），使得目标分布本质上定义在商空间上。传统群等变扩散模型需要学习群作用对应的分量，增加学习难度；而启发式对齐策略缺乏合适的采样器。因此需要建立统一的商空间扩散建模框架。

Method: 建立通用商空间扩散建模的形式化框架，将目标分布定义在群作用的商空间上，减少对群作用对应分量的学习。将该框架应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成，并设计保证恢复目标分布的正确采样器。

Result: 在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上进行了实验验证，表明所提出的商空间扩散模型优于传统的对称性处理方法。

Conclusion: 商空间扩散模型提供了一个新的框架，通过利用系统的对称性简化学习难度，并保证正确采样，在分子结构生成中展现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [133] [A Scale-Adaptive Framework for Joint Spatiotemporal Super-Resolution with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21903)
*Max Defez,Filippo Quarenghi,Mathieu Vrac,Stephan Mandt,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种尺度自适应的联合时空超分辨率框架，通过将时空超分辨率分解为条件均值预测（含注意力机制）和残差条件扩散模型，仅调整三个与因子相关的超参数（噪声调度幅度、时间上下文长度、质量守恒函数）即可在无需重新训练架构的情况下，跨越空间1-25倍、时间1-6倍的超分辨率因子，在法国降水再分析数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视频超分辨率方法在气候应用中通常只单独超分辨率空间或时间维度，而联合时空模型往往针对单一超分辨率因子对设计，无法跨空间分辨率和时间帧率迁移，缺乏一个可重用、尺度自适应的框架。

Method: 将时空超分辨率分解为两个部分：(1) 基于注意力机制的条件均值确定性预测；(2) 残差条件扩散模型，可选质量守恒变换以保持聚合总量。尺度自适应性通过调整三个与因子相关的超参数实现：扩散噪声调度幅度β（随因子增大以增加多样性）、时间上下文长度L（保持不同帧率下的注意力视野相当）、质量守恒函数f（对大因子进行 taper 以限制极端值放大）。

Result: 在法国降水再分析数据集Comephore上，同一架构可跨越空间超分辨率因子1-25倍和时间超分辨率因子1-6倍，验证了架构和调参配方的可重用性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可跨尺度使用的联合时空超分辨率框架和调参配方，避免了为每个超分辨率因子对重新设计架构，为气候应用中的多尺度时空升尺度提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep-learning video super-resolution has progressed rapidly, but climate applications typically super-resolve (increase resolution) either space or time, and joint spatiotemporal models are often designed for a single pair of super-resolution (SR) factors (upscaling spatial and temporal ratio between the low-resolution sequence and the high-resolution sequence), limiting transfer across spatial resolutions and temporal cadences (frame rates). We present a scale-adaptive framework that reuses the same architecture across factors by decomposing spatiotemporal SR into a deterministic prediction of the conditional mean, with attention, and a residual conditional diffusion model, with an optional mass-conservation (same precipitation amount in inputs and outputs) transform to preserve aggregated totals. Assuming that larger SR factors primarily increase underdetermination (hence required context and residual uncertainty) rather than changing the conditional-mean structure, scale adaptivity is achieved by retuning three factor-dependent hyperparameters before retraining: the diffusion noise schedule amplitude beta (larger for larger factors to increase diversity), the temporal context length L (set to maintain comparable attention horizons across cadences) and optionally a third, the mass-conservation function f (tapered to limit the amplification of extremes for large factors). Demonstrated on reanalysis precipitation over France (Comephore), the same architecture spans super-resolution factors from 1 to 25 in space and 1 to 6 in time, yielding a reusable architecture and tuning recipe for joint spatiotemporal super-resolution across scales.

</details>


### [134] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了批量设置下多校准（multicalibration）的极小值样本复杂度。针对固定κ>0且组族大小|G|≤ε^{-κ}的情形，证明样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})（上下界匹配），这分离了多校准与边际校准（样本复杂度Θ̃(ε^{-2})）的样本复杂度，并展示了批量多校准与在线多校准具有相同难度。对于κ=0，则样本复杂度回到Θ̃(ε^{-2})，呈现尖锐阈值现象。更一般地，对于加权L_p多校准度量（1≤p≤2），建立了匹配的上下界，最优指数为3/p。还将下界模板扩展到可elicitable性质类，并结合在线界获得了包括expectiles和有限密度分位数在内的校准性质的匹配界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多校准在批量设置下的极小值样本复杂度，厘清其与边际校准的差异，以及批量设置与在线设置之间的难度关系。

Method: 通过在线到批量的转换（online-to-batch reduction）构造随机预测器实现上界；下界使用信息论或对抗性论证技术，并建立可elicitable性质的下界模板。

Result: 建立了批量多校准的样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})（κ>0时），并分离了多校准与边际校准（Θ̃(ε^{-2})）。对于加权L_p度量，最优指数为3/p。并扩展到expectiles和有限密度分位数等性质。

Conclusion: 批量多校准的样本复杂度显著高于边际校准，且与在线多校准复杂度一致；存在关于组族大小的尖锐阈值现象；结果可推广到更广泛的校准性质类。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [135] [Fine-Tuning Regimes Define Distinct Continual Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21927)
*Paul-Tiberiu Iordache,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证持续学习中的微调机制（即可训练参数子空间）本身是重要的评估变量，通过形式化投影优化框架，在5个数据集、11种任务顺序和4种方法上发现方法排名在不同微调深度下不一致，且更深的微调导致更大更新幅度、更高遗忘率及二者更强关联，提出应将可训练深度作为明确实验因素纳入评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法比较通常固定微调机制，但微调机制本身可能影响比较结论，需要系统研究微调深度作为评估变量的影响。

Method: 形式化地将适应机制定义为在固定可训练子空间上的投影优化，实验采用任务增量持续学习、五种可训练深度机制、四种标准方法（online EWC, LwF, SI, GEM），在五个基准数据集（MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, CIFAR-100）及每个数据集11种任务顺序上进行系统比较。

Result: 方法相对排名在不同微调机制下不一致；更深适应机制与更大更新幅度、更高遗忘率及二者更强关联相关。

Conclusion: 持续学习中的比较结论可能严重依赖于所选的微调机制，应提出感知机制的评估协议，将可训练深度作为显式实验因素处理。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) studies how models acquire tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned knowledge. Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

# cs.GL [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [A Brief History of Fréchet Distances: From Curves and Probability Laws to FID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21745)
*Yuli Wu*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 本文追溯了Fréchet距离的发展历程：从1906年Fréchet博士论文中抽象集合的距离概念，到1990年代多边形曲线间Fréchet距离的算法计算；再到1957年他基于耦合的概率分布距离（Wasserstein距离与最优传输）；最后以深度生成模型评估中的Fréchet Inception Distance (FID)作为现代应用案例。附录包含Fréchet 1906年论文、1957年论文及Lévy 1950年笔记的TeX格式英译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理Fréchet距离从纯数学到计算几何再到机器学习的跨领域发展脉络，并揭示概率分布上的耦合型Fréchet距离与几何曲线上的Fréchet距离之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用编年史叙述方式，按时间顺序梳理关键文献；通过附录提供历史文献的英文翻译以方便读者；通过FID展示Fréchet距离在现代深度学习中的应用。

Result: 呈现了Fréchet距离在三个领域的演进：抽象度量空间→计算几何中的曲线距离→概率论中的Wasserstein距离→深度学习中的生成模型评估指标FID（即学习特征空间中多元高斯分布间的Wasserstein-2距离）。

Conclusion: Fréchet距离的概念历经百年发展，从抽象数学延展到计算几何和概率分布，最终在深度学习时代以FID的形式焕发新生，体现了数学概念跨学科的生命力。

Abstract: This note provides a chronological account of Fréchet distances, starting with Maurice Fréchet's 1906 doctoral thesis on distances in abstract sets and tracing the Fréchet distance between polygonal curves and its algorithmic computation in the 1990s. It then continues with his 1957 paper on a coupling-based distance between probability laws with a brief glimpse of Wasserstein distance and optimal transport. We further attempt to draw connections between the distributional, coupling-based facet of Fréchet distances on probability laws and the geometric facet on curves. The note ends with a modern use case, the Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) in the era of deep generative model evaluation, interpretable as the Wasserstein-2 distance between multivariate Gaussians in a learned feature space. An appendix includes \TeX{}ified faithful English translations of Fréchet's 1906 thesis and 1957 paper, and Lévy's 1950 note for reader convenience.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Linear Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21467)
*Arnaud Spiwack,Csongor Kiss,Jean-Philippe Bernardy,Nicolas Wu,Richard A. Eisenberg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文介绍线性约束（Linear Constraints）作为Haskell类型类约束的线性对应物，通过将线性参数隐式化并由编译器自动填充，避免了显式线性参数带来的编程冗余。论文形式化地描述了这一合格类型系统及其推理算法，该算法扩展了GHC现有的约束求解器。线性约束的正确性由其可脱糖（desugar）为Linear Haskell来保证。本文是作者先前工作（arXiv:2103.06127）的修订和扩展版本，简化了形式系统和约束求解器，并增加了更多应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性类型系统能够验证文件句柄、手动管理的内存等资源的安全性，但显式编写线性参数会导致代码冗长和额外的编程负担。线性约束旨在解决这一不足，将线性参数隐式化，减少程序员的手动管理工作量。

Method: 论文将线性约束形式化为一个合格类型系统（qualified type system），并设计了扩展GHC现有约束求解器的推理算法。线性约束的正确性通过其到Linear Haskell的脱糖（desugaring）来保证。

Result: 相较于作者之前的版本，本文显著简化了形式系统和约束求解器，并描述了更多实际应用场景。

Conclusion: 线性约束通过隐式线性参数降低编程冗余，其类型系统的正确性由脱糖到Linear Haskell保证，且实际应用范围得到了扩展。

Abstract: Linear constraints are the linear counterpart of Haskell's class constraints. Linearly typed parameters allow the programmer to control resources such as file handles and manually managed memory as linear arguments. Indeed, a linear type system can verify that these resources are used safely. However, writing code with explicit linear arguments requires bureaucracy. Linear constraints address this shortcoming: a linear constraint acts as an implicit linear argument that can be filled in automatically by the compiler.
  We present this new feature as a qualified type system, together with an inference algorithm which extends GHC's existing constraint solver algorithm. Soundness of linear constraints is ensured by the fact that they desugar into Linear Haskell.
  This paper is a revised and extended version of a previous paper by the same authors (arXiv:2103.06127). The formal system and the constraint solver have been significantly simplified and numerous additional applications are described.

</details>
